
## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留稅法一般課稅權、稅捐要件與總路由；151–153承擔國際稅、間接稅與稅捐程序。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-010-TAX_LAW"
  chinese_name: "稅法"
  english_name: "Tax Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家在沒有個別交換對價的情況下，憑什麼對人民的所得、財產、消費、交易或其他稅捐客體課予金錢負擔；而這個負擔如何在法定要件、量能、公平、反避稅與程序保障之間取得資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家需要穩定財源提供公共服務與維持制度運作，但課稅直接侵入人民財產領域。若課稅可以只靠行政便利或事後政策目的決定，稅捐權會變成高度不可預測的財產支配。因此稅法發展出高度法定化的課稅構成、申報核課、稽徵程序、救濟與跨境分配規則。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家財政需要 vs 人民財產權與法律安定"
    - "租稅法定 vs 反避稅與實質課稅"
    - "量能公平 vs 稅制可操作性"
    - "形式交易安排 vs 真實經濟實質"
    - "納稅義務履行 vs 稅捐規避／逃漏"
    - "稽徵效率 vs 程序保障"
    - "來源地課稅 vs 居住地課稅"
    - "避免雙重課稅 vs 避免雙重不課稅"

  shortest_maxim:
    "稅法第一眼不是『政府算了多少錢』；先問『哪一個稅、哪一個主體、哪一個課稅客體、哪一個期間、哪一個法定連結點』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 16.1 第一眼｜TAX TYPE → TAXPAYER → TAX BASE → NEXUS → PERIOD

稅務案件最危險的錯誤是從總金額開始。

正確第一眼：

```text
TAX TYPE
＝所得稅？營業／增值稅？貨物／消費稅？關稅？財產稅？遺產／贈與稅？其他公課？

TAXPAYER
＝誰依法成為納稅義務人／扣繳義務人／代徵人？

TAX BASE
＝對什麼所得、價值、財產、交易、消費或移轉課稅？

NEXUS
＝居住地？來源地？常設機構？財產所在地？交易發生地？進口？

PERIOD
＝哪一課稅年度／申報期間／交易時點？
```

如果這五個問題混在一起，AI 很容易做出：

> **看到錢進帳，就推定是所得。**

> **看到公司有利潤，就推定在所有國家都能課。**

> **看到交易沒有繳稅，就推定逃漏稅。**

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 16.10 證據特徵

稅法證據常是「交易真實性＋課稅連結＋計算」：

- 報稅資料；
- 發票；
- 帳簿；
- 財報；
- 銀行流水；
- 契約；
- 公司／信託／基金架構；
- 實質營運資料；
- 人員與辦公場所；
- 物流；
- 關係人交易；
- 移轉訂價報告；
- 居住天數；
- 常設機構活動；
- 所得來源證明；
- 扣繳資料；
- 進出口資料；
- 稅捐機關核定與調查紀錄。

核心：

> **會計分類是重要證據，但不自動等於稅法分類。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 16.12 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依特定法域、稅目與期間現查：

1. 稅目及課稅主體；
2. 稅捐客體與稅基；
3. 稅率；
4. 免稅、扣除、抵免；
5. 居住者／非居住者；
6. 所得來源；
7. 常設機構；
8. 反避稅；
9. 移轉訂價；
10. 申報、核課、追徵與時效；
11. 稅捐處罰與刑事責任；
12. 退稅與救濟；
13. 租稅協定文字與生效；
14. OECD 2025 Model 更新是否已進入特定雙邊協定；
15. VAT／GST／關稅等跨境間接稅；
16. 數位經濟與全球最低稅等現行制度。

## 16.11 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "錢進帳＝所得。"
  - "會計有收入＝稅法一定同額課稅。"
  - "公司虧損＝一定沒有任何稅負。"
  - "形式合法＝反避稅永遠不能介入。"

  - "同一所得兩國都想課＝一定有一國違法。"
  - "OECD Model Tax Convention＝現行雙邊租稅協定。"
  - "稅務機關認定＝課稅事實已終局成立。"
  - "稅務規劃＝當然違法或當然合法。"
```

```yaml
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FLOW_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B51"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "A:政府／國家／公權力、D:組織／企業／市場"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-151-INTERNATIONAL_TAX_TRANSFER_PRICING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際稅法／移轉訂價法"
  english_name: "International Tax / Transfer Pricing Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當一個所得、企業、資產、交易或跨國企業集團同時與兩個以上課稅法域發生連結時，哪個國家依國內法具有課稅權、租稅協定是否限制該權力、所得如何在居住地／來源地／常設機構／關係企業間分配；關係企業交易是否符合arm's-length或其他現行法定方法；又如何處理BEPS、Pillar Two、雙重課稅與MAP／APA？"

  historical_reason:
    "各國的所得稅權原本從本國的居住、來源與領土連結出發，但跨國企業可以把法律人格、資產、融資、IP、功能與風險配置在不同法域。若只讓每國各自套國內法，容易發生雙重課稅；若只看形式所在地，又可能發生利潤與實質活動分離、雙重不課稅與稅基侵蝕。國際稅法因此形成國內法、雙邊租稅協定、移轉訂價、反避稅與多邊協調的多層結構。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識taxpayer、jurisdictions、tax year、domestic nexus、income / transaction type、actual treaty text、PE／residence／source與related-party transaction；不要先因『跨國公司』就套OECD Model、Pillar Two或transfer pricing。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "residence taxation vs source taxation"
    - "domestic taxing power vs treaty limitation"
    - "juridical double taxation vs double non-taxation"
    - "separate-entity accounting vs integrated MNE economics"
    - "arm's-length principle vs administrability / simplification"
    - "legal form vs economic substance"
    - "tax competition vs tax-base protection"
    - "tax certainty vs anti-avoidance"
    - "unilateral measures vs multilateral coordination"
    - "taxpayer planning vs abuse / evasion"
    - "global minimum tax coordination vs domestic legislative sovereignty"
    - "information exchange vs confidentiality / taxpayer rights"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際稅第一眼不是『同一筆錢在哪國稅率低』；先問『哪國國內法先取得課稅連結、哪份生效協定再限制它、哪個主體與交易被分配多少所得、還有哪些反避稅與程序機制真正已在該年度生效』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-113.1 第一眼｜DOMESTIC NEXUS → TREATY → TAXPAYER / RESIDENCE → SOURCE / PE → INCOME CHARACTER → RELATED-PARTY TRANSACTION → ALLOCATION / PRICING → ANTI-AVOIDANCE → RELIEF → PROCEDURE

```text
DOMESTIC NEXUS
＝哪個國家依本國法先主張課稅？

TREATY
＝兩國之間是否真的有生效租稅協定？適用哪一版、哪一年度？

TAXPAYER / RESIDENCE
＝個人？公司？透明實體？基金？trust？雙重居住？

SOURCE / PE
＝所得來源在哪？是否有PE / fixed place / dependent agent等連結？

INCOME CHARACTER
＝business profits? dividends? interest? royalties? capital gains? employment? services?

RELATED-PARTY TRANSACTION
＝goods? services? financing? IP? cost sharing? restructuring?

ALLOCATION / PRICING
＝functions / assets / risks? comparables? method? tested party?

ANTI-AVOIDANCE
＝GAAR? treaty anti-abuse? CFC? interest limitation? hybrid mismatch? domestic SAAR?

RELIEF
＝exemption? foreign tax credit? corresponding adjustment? MAP?

PROCEDURE
＝filing? documentation? CbCR? GIR? APA? audit? time bar? appeal?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-113.17 International tax evidence architecture

- entity chart；
- tax residence certificates；
- actual bilateral treaty / protocol / MLI position；
- contracts；
- accounting ledgers；
- intercompany invoices；
- transfer pricing local / master file；
- CbCR；
- DEMPE / function interviews；
- personnel / office / decision records；
- PE activity evidence；
- financing terms；
- withholding records；
- tax returns；
- rulings / APA；
- audit adjustments；
- MAP correspondence；
- Pillar Two calculation / GIR where applicable。

核心：

> **集團圖是法律與經濟關係的索引，不是移轉訂價結論。**

## G2-113.19 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域、條約、納稅人與課稅年度現查：

1. residence / source rules；
2. actual treaty text / protocol / MLI；
3. PE definition；
4. income character；
5. withholding rate；
6. foreign tax credit / exemption；
7. transfer pricing legislation；
8. accepted methods / documentation；
9. Amount B adoption；
10. CFC / interest limitation / hybrid / GAAR；
11. Pillar Two implementation / QDMTT / IIR / UTPR；
12. safe harbours；
13. CbCR / GIR filing；
14. APA；
15. MAP / arbitration；
16. limitation period / penalty；
17. exchange of information；
18. interaction with domestic appeals。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-152-VAT_GST_INDIRECT_TAX_LAW"
  chinese_name: "VAT／GST／間接稅法"
  english_name: "VAT / GST / Indirect Tax Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當商品、服務、數位供應、平台交易、進口或消費發生時，哪個法域依destination／place-of-supply／import rules取得間接稅權；誰是taxable person、supplier、deemed supplier、importer或withholding / reverse-charge義務人；output tax、input tax credit、exemption、zero-rating、registration、invoice、OSS／IOSS與digital reporting如何共同決定實際稅負與程序？"

  historical_reason:
    "VAT／GST通常以多階段課徵與進項扣抵使稅負最終落在消費，但跨境交易、服務、數位供應與平台經濟使『在哪裡消費、誰應代收、誰能扣抵』變得比貨物移動本身更複雜。若把VAT當成普通營業稅或只看發票金額，容易發生重複課稅、漏稅或拒絕合法扣抵。"

  first_move:
    "先辨 supply / import、goods / services、B2B / B2C、supplier / customer status、place of supply、taxable amount、rate、exemption / zero rate、reverse charge、input-credit chain與registration；不要從『開沒開發票』直接跳到稅額。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "destination taxation vs cross-border administrability"
    - "tax neutrality vs fraud control"
    - "output tax collection vs input tax recovery"
    - "exemption vs zero-rating"
    - "formal invoice requirements vs substantive neutrality"
    - "platform facilitation vs deemed-supplier liability"
    - "small-business simplification vs tax-base protection"
    - "real-time digital reporting vs compliance burden / privacy"
    - "domestic autonomy vs harmonised VAT architecture"
    - "customs import event vs domestic supply event"

  shortest_maxim:
    "VAT第一眼不是『誰收到錢』；先問『發生哪一個供應／進口、供應地在哪、誰依法是收稅或reverse-charge主體、這筆稅能不能進項扣抵，以及該日期究竟是哪一版數位申報／平台規則已經適用』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-114.1 第一眼｜TRANSACTION → GOODS / SERVICES → B2B / B2C → PLACE OF SUPPLY → TAXABLE PERSON → LIABILITY MECHANISM → RATE / EXEMPTION → INPUT CREDIT → INVOICE / REPORTING → REGISTRATION / SPECIAL SCHEME

```text
TRANSACTION
＝supply? import? intra-group? transfer of own goods? voucher?

GOODS / SERVICES
＝physical goods? services? electronically supplied service?

B2B / B2C
＝customer taxable-person status affects place / reverse charge?

PLACE OF SUPPLY
＝which jurisdiction has consumption connection?

LIABILITY
＝supplier? customer reverse charge? platform deemed supplier? importer?

RATE
＝standard? reduced? zero? exempt?

INPUT CREDIT
＝business use? blocked input? partial exemption?

DOCUMENT
＝invoice? e-invoice? import document? proof of status / location?

SCHEME
＝OSS? IOSS? special SME scheme? domestic registration?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-114.16 VAT evidence architecture

- contracts / orders；
- invoices / e-invoices；
- customer VAT status；
- VAT IDs；
- shipping / logistics；
- customs documents；
- service-delivery records；
- IP / billing location evidence for digital services；
- payment records；
- OSS / IOSS returns；
- domestic VAT returns；
- input deduction ledgers；
- platform transaction logs；
- refund claims；
- audit correspondence；
- e-reporting files；
- fraud-risk evidence。

核心：

> **發票是VAT鏈的重要法律文件，但不是交易本身的替身。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-153-TAX_PROCEDURE_ASSESSMENT_COLLECTION_DISPUTES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "稅捐程序／核課徵收與爭訟法"
  english_name: "Tax Procedure / Assessment / Collection & Disputes Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "即使實體稅法可能產生納稅義務，稅捐機關如何合法取得資料、進行查核、作成核課／更正、計算利息與處罰、追徵與強制徵收；納稅人何時取得通知、陳述、查閱、保密、異議、復查、行政或司法救濟、暫緩執行、退稅與跨境爭端解決資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "租稅通常由大量自行申報與行政核課運作。國家若只有實體課稅權卻沒有調查與徵收能力，稅制無法執行；但若行政機關可以無期限查帳、以推定取代證據、先徵收再讓納稅人無實效救濟，課稅權就會變成難以審查的財產權力。因此稅捐程序不是實體稅法的附件，而是課稅權取得現實強制效果之前的資格鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先定位程序節點：return、audit、information request、assessment、penalty、collection、refund、administrative objection、court appeal、MAP或cross-border recovery；再查deadline、burden、suspensive effect與authority，不要把稅局一張通知直接當終局稅債。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "revenue collection efficiency vs due process"
    - "self-assessment vs official verification"
    - "taxpayer cooperation vs privilege / confidentiality / proportionality"
    - "information asymmetry vs burden of proof"
    - "anti-evasion enforcement vs presumption / legal certainty"
    - "immediate collection vs effective remedy"
    - "finality / limitation periods vs correction of under-taxation"
    - "penalty deterrence vs culpability / proportionality"
    - "domestic remedies vs treaty MAP"
    - "cross-border recovery cooperation vs local procedural safeguards"
    - "digital / AI risk scoring vs explainability / error correction"

  shortest_maxim:
    "稅捐程序第一眼不是『稅局說欠多少』；先問『現在在哪個程序節點、哪個機關有什麼調查／核課／徵收權、期限與證明責任在哪、這個處分能不能救濟、救濟會不會停止執行，以及跨境程序有沒有另一條法源鏈』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-115.1 第一眼｜RETURN → AUDIT → INFORMATION → ASSESSMENT → PENALTY → COLLECTION → OBJECTION / REVIEW → COURT → REFUND / FINALITY → CROSS-BORDER PROCEDURE

```text
RETURN
＝self-assessment? withholding? third-party report?

AUDIT
＝desk review? field audit? transfer-pricing audit? investigation?

INFORMATION
＝what power? whose records? third party? foreign authority?

ASSESSMENT
＝original? amended? estimated? jeopardy/emergency?

PENALTY
＝civil surcharge? administrative fine? criminal referral?

COLLECTION
＝voluntary payment? lien? levy? seizure? installment? security?

REVIEW
＝objection? reconsideration? tribunal? court?

EFFECT
＝does appeal suspend collection? bond/security required?

FINALITY
＝limitation? res judicata? reopening?

CROSS-BORDER
＝MAP? information exchange? foreign recovery assistance?
```

---

## G2-115.18 Tax procedure evidence architecture

- filed return；
- assessment notice；
- audit plan / request；
- information request；
- service / delivery proof；
- books / records；
- third-party reports；
- interview records；
- authority calculation；
- limitation timeline；
- penalty reasoning；
- collection demand；
- lien / seizure records；
- objection / appeal；
- court filings；
- payment / security；
- refund claim；
- MAP complaint；
- competent-authority correspondence；
- foreign recovery request。

核心：

> **稅務卷宗的每一張紙都有程序位置；不能把後段核課理由倒灌成前段原始事實。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_FISCAL_FRAMEWORK_2024_REFORM_IN_FORCE__NATIONAL_BUDGET_DEBT_AND_CURRENT_SURVEILLANCE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The EU's reformed economic-governance framework has been in force since 30 April 2024 and is operating through medium-term fiscal-structural plans and annual monitoring. It does not replace constitutional budget authority, debt-issuance law, appropriation rules or non-EU public-finance regimes.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — New economic governance framework**
  - URL: https://economy-finance.ec.europa.eu/economic-governance-framework/what-economic-governance-framework/evolution-eu-economic-governance/new-economic-governance-framework_en
  - Verified point: The reformed EU economic-governance framework entered into force on 30 April 2024 and uses medium-term fiscal-structural plans.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-202-PUBLIC_FINANCE_BUDGET_APPROPRIATIONS_PUBLIC_DEBT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公共財政／預算、撥款與政府債務法"
  english_name: "Public Finance / Budget, Appropriations & Public Debt Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "政府、地方政府或其他public entity如何把稅收、費用、移轉、資產收益與借款轉成依法可支出的公共資金；年度／中期預算由誰提出、誰批准、哪一項appropriation授權哪一機關為何目的承擔commitment並付款；追加／緊急支出、基金、off-budget entity、government guarantee、contingent liability與public debt如何進入財政風險、會計、透明與審計責任鏈？"

  historical_reason:
    "稅法回答國家如何合法取得人民財產的一部分，但並不回答取得之後政府是否可以任意花。現代憲政因此把公共支出拆成budget formulation、legislative authorization、appropriation、execution、accounting與audit；借款又把今天的政策成本移到未來納稅人，guarantee與PPP則可能在沒有即期付款時創造巨大或有財政負擔。公共財政法因此是政府作為『管理公共錢的人』的核心法，而不是會計技術的附錄。"

  first_move:
    "先確定public entity與money source，再判斷budget / appropriation / fund authority；接著追 commitment、procurement/contract、payment、accounting與cash execution。若涉及borrow/guarantee，再另查debt authority、limit、approval、disclosure與contingent-risk treatment。不得因『預算有列』就跳到支出、採購或契約當然合法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "democratic appropriation vs executive flexibility"
    - "annual budget control vs multi-year policy"
    - "fiscal discipline vs crisis response"
    - "present benefits vs intergenerational debt"
    - "public investment vs debt sustainability"
    - "budget completeness vs off-budget entities"
    - "fiscal transparency vs complexity"
    - "contingent liability vs apparent low current spending"
    - "earmarked funds vs unified budget control"
    - "central fiscal control vs local autonomy"
    - "cash control vs accrual recognition"
    - "independent fiscal oversight vs elected budget authority"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公共財政第一眼不是『政府有錢』；先問『錢從哪個法源進來、哪個預算／撥款授權它為什麼目的花、誰能承諾與付款、借款與保證把多少風險搬到未來、最後誰記帳與審計』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-72.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → SOURCE → BUDGET FRAMEWORK → APPROPRIATION → ALLOTMENT / COMMITMENT → PROCUREMENT / CONTRACT → PAYMENT → ACCOUNTING → DEBT / GUARANTEE → SUPPLEMENTARY / EMERGENCY → AUDIT

```text
ENTITY
＝central government? local government? fund? agency? SOE?

SOURCE
＝tax? fee? grant? resource revenue? asset sale? borrowing?

BUDGET FRAMEWORK
＝annual? medium-term? fiscal rule? ceiling?

APPROPRIATION
＝which legislature / authority authorized what amount / purpose?

ALLOTMENT / COMMITMENT
＝who may legally commit future payment?

PROCUREMENT / CONTRACT
＝was spending vehicle itself lawful?

PAYMENT
＝treasury authority? cash? verification?

ACCOUNTING
＝recognition / classification / reporting?

DEBT / GUARANTEE
＝borrowing authority? debt limit? contingent liability?

SUPPLEMENTARY / EMERGENCY
＝reallocation? reserve? supplementary appropriation?

AUDIT
＝internal control? supreme audit? parliament? public reporting?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-72.14 Evidence architecture

- constitution；
- budget framework statute；
- annual budget bill / act；
- appropriation schedule；
- medium-term fiscal plan；
- fiscal rule；
- supplementary budget；
- reserve authorization；
- allotment / commitment record；
- procurement file；
- contract；
- payment voucher；
- treasury record；
- public account；
- debt authorization；
- bond / loan documents；
- guarantee；
- contingent-liability statement；
- SOE / fund accounts；
- fiscal-risk report；
- audit report；
- parliamentary oversight record；
- independent fiscal institution assessment。

核心：

> **公共財政的證據不是「錢有沒有真的出去」而已；而是公共錢從政策構想到法律授權、承諾、支付、負債與審計的整條責任鏈。**

EU State-aid law is highly time-sensitive in 2026: METSAF was adopted on 29 April and is temporary through 31 December 2026, while the GBER replacement remains under review with planned 2027 entry into force. Applicable Treaty basis, notification/exemption route and other international subsidy rules still require case-specific checks.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Middle East Crisis Temporary State Aid Framework (METSAF)**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/state-aid/legislation/metsaf_en
  - Verified point: METSAF was adopted on 29 April 2026 and is temporary through 31 December 2026.
- **European Commission — GBER review**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/state-aid/gber-review_en
  - Verified point: The 2026 GBER review is ongoing; adoption of a revised GBER is planned for end-2026 with entry into force planned for 1 January 2027.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-203-STATE_AID_PUBLIC_SUBSIDY_CONTROL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家援助／公共補貼控制法"
  english_name: "State Aid / Public Subsidy Control Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "政府或public body透過grant、tax advantage、loan、guarantee、equity、goods/services、public purchase、price/income support或其他public-resource measure向企業／產業提供經濟利益時，何時只是一般公共政策支出，何時進入EU State Aid、WTO subsidy或國內補貼控制；benefit / advantage、selectivity / specificity、competition / trade effect、compatibility / exception、notification / standstill、recovery、WTO dispute或countervailing duty如何分軸？"

  historical_reason:
    "政府用錢推動產業、公共服務、區域發展、能源轉型與危機救助本身並非異常；但當公共資源只讓特定企業取得市場上原本拿不到的利益時，補貼可能改變競爭與跨境貿易。EU State Aid建立內部市場的事前／事後控制與recovery；WTO SCM則從國際貿易角度規範specific subsidies、prohibited subsidies、adverse effects與countervailing measures。兩套制度都處理『政府給利益』，卻不是同一套要件。"

  first_move:
    "先畫public-resource measure與beneficiary，再判economic activity、advantage/benefit與selectivity/specificity；之後才分EU State Aid、WTO SCM或domestic subsidy-control route。不得因政府付款就直接叫state aid，也不得因補貼有公共目的就自動排除競爭／貿易控制。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "industrial policy vs competitive neutrality"
    - "public-service financing vs overcompensation"
    - "regional development vs selectivity"
    - "crisis rescue vs moral hazard"
    - "green transition support vs market distortion"
    - "tax policy autonomy vs selective tax advantage"
    - "government ownership vs private-investor benchmark"
    - "domestic policy space vs international trade effects"
    - "rapid aid vs notification / standstill"
    - "beneficiary reliance vs unlawful-aid recovery"
    - "subsidy transparency vs commercial confidentiality"
    - "state aid control vs public-finance authorization"

  shortest_maxim:
    "補貼控制第一眼不是『政府給錢就是違法』；先問『哪種public resource、誰真正受益、是否取得市場優勢、是否選擇性／特定、影響哪個競爭或貿易法域、是否有合法例外／相容性、要不要通知、違法後是追回還是走WTO／countervailing路徑』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-73.1 第一眼｜PUBLIC RESOURCE → MEASURE → BENEFICIARY → ECONOMIC ACTIVITY → ADVANTAGE / BENEFIT → SELECTIVITY / SPECIFICITY → TRADE / COMPETITION → EXCEPTION / COMPATIBILITY → PROCEDURE → RECOVERY / REMEDY

```text
PUBLIC RESOURCE
＝grant? tax? guarantee? equity? loan? goods? public purchase?

MEASURE
＝scheme or individual measure?

BENEFICIARY
＝undertaking? industry? public enterprise? consumer indirectly?

ECONOMIC ACTIVITY
＝market activity or non-economic public function?

ADVANTAGE / BENEFIT
＝better than market / normal conditions?

SELECTIVITY / SPECIFICITY
＝all firms? sector? region? discretion? objective general criteria?

TRADE / COMPETITION
＝EU internal trade/competition? WTO trade effect?

EXCEPTION / COMPATIBILITY
＝block exemption? SGEI? disaster? regional? R&D? green? other?

PROCEDURE
＝notification? standstill? investigation? WTO notification / CVD investigation?

RECOVERY / REMEDY
＝recover unlawful aid? withdraw subsidy? remove adverse effects? countervailing duty?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-73.14 Evidence architecture

- budget / appropriation；
- aid scheme；
- grant decision；
- tax ruling / incentive；
- loan / guarantee；
- equity valuation；
- market benchmark；
- beneficiary accounts；
- public-body decision；
- tender / procurement record；
- sector / geographic eligibility；
- subsidy calculation；
- competition / trade data；
- EU notification；
- block-exemption record；
- Commission decision；
- recovery order；
- WTO notification；
- SCM investigation；
- CVD petition；
- injury evidence；
- benefit calculation；
- repayment record。

核心：

> **補貼控制的證據不是「企業拿了多少政府錢」而已；而是公共資源如何讓特定市場主體取得何種可比較優勢，以及那個優勢進入哪一套競爭／貿易控制。**

```yaml
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 108｜DLM-102｜擔保交易／動產擔保法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B34"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-102-SECURED_TRANSACTIONS_MOVABLE_SECURITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "擔保交易／動產擔保法"
  english_name: "Secured Transactions / Movable Security Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "debtor / grantor如何用現有或未來movable asset、receivable、inventory、bank account、IP或digital asset支持obligation；security right何時成立、何時對third parties effective、如何register / possess / control、與其他claimants如何排priority、default後如何enforce、insolvency時如何與stay / avoidance / distribution接軌？"

  historical_reason:
    "企業最有價值的資產往往不是土地，而是inventory、receivables、equipment、bank accounts、IP與其他movables。若法律只容許少數形式擔保或要求實際交付占有，SMEs很難把營運資產轉為credit。modern secured-transactions law因此採functional approach、broad asset coverage、notice registry、clear priority與predictable enforcement，讓資產仍能被debtor使用同時支持credit；但這也要求對第三人透明並與insolvency、公平交易與consumer protection銜接。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "access to credit vs debtor asset freedom"
    - "functional substance vs formal transaction labels"
    - "secret liens vs public notice"
    - "secured-credit priority vs unsecured creditors"
    - "speedy enforcement vs debtor / third-party safeguards"
    - "future assets vs certainty of collateral"
    - "floating inventory vs buyer protection"
    - "control-based priority vs registry transparency"
    - "secured lending vs insolvency collective process"
    - "receivables finance vs debtor defences"
    - "digital assets vs legacy property categories"
    - "cross-border collateral vs applicable law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "擔保交易法第一眼不是『有抵押／質押所以債權人優先』；先問『哪個obligation、哪個collateral、security right何時成立、怎麼對第三人有效、誰還有競爭權利、priority規則是什麼、default後能怎麼執行、insolvency是否改變結果』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 108.1 第一眼｜OBLIGATION → GRANTOR / DEBTOR → COLLATERAL → SECURITY AGREEMENT → CREATION / ATTACHMENT → THIRD-PARTY EFFECTIVENESS → REGISTRY / POSSESSION / CONTROL → PRIORITY → PROCEEDS → DEFAULT → ENFORCEMENT → INSOLVENCY / REMEDY

```text
OBLIGATION
＝loan? purchase price? guarantee? future advance?

GRANTOR / DEBTOR
＝who owns / has rights in collateral?

COLLATERAL
＝equipment? inventory? receivable? account? IP? digital asset?

SECURITY AGREEMENT
＝what assets / secured obligations described?

CREATION / ATTACHMENT
＝has security right arisen between parties?

THIRD-PARTY EFFECTIVENESS
＝what makes it opposable / effective against others?

REGISTRY / POSSESSION / CONTROL
＝which perfection method applies?

PRIORITY
＝who wins against competing creditor / buyer / insolvency estate?

PROCEEDS
＝does security continue into transformed value?

DEFAULT
＝what event triggers remedies?

ENFORCEMENT
＝sale? collection? possession? court?

INSOLVENCY
＝stay? avoidance? distribution? priority?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 108.39 Evidence architecture

- loan / secured obligation；
- security agreement；
- asset ownership；
- collateral schedule；
- registry notice / search；
- possession / control agreement；
- account statements；
- receivables ledger；
- debtor notices；
- inventory records；
- proceeds tracing；
- prior liens；
- statutory liens；
- default notice；
- valuation；
- sale / collection records；
- insolvency filing；
- digital-asset wallet / custody / control records；
- governing-law clauses。

核心：

> **擔保交易案最容易犯的錯，是看見一張「抵押／質押／擔保契約」就跳到優先受償；中間至少還有collateral rights、third-party effectiveness、priority、default、enforcement與insolvency六個gate。**

以下必須依obligation、collateral、grantor、creditor、forum、date現查：

2. transaction characterization；
3. grantor rights / capacity；
4. security agreement form；
5. collateral description；
6. secured obligation；
7. future assets / future advances；
8. creation / attachment；
9. third-party effectiveness；
10. registry；
11. possession；
12. control；
13. priority；
14. purchase-money priority；
15. buyers / transferees；
16. inventory；
17. proceeds；
18. receivables；
19. anti-assignment clause；
20. debtor notice / defences / set-off；
21. bank account；
22. negotiable instrument / document；
23. IP；
24. digital assets；
25. statutory liens；
26. enforcement；
27. notice / sale standard；
28. surplus / deficiency；
29. guarantor / third-party grantor；
30. insolvency stay / avoidance；
31. consumer protections；
32. conflict of laws；
33. UNCITRAL Model Law status / domestic enactment；
34. UN Receivables Convention current non-entry-into-force；
35. UNIDROIT DAPL Principles at non-binding level；
36. UNCITRAL 2026 digital supplement only as work in progress。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "BANK_RESOLUTION_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__EU_CMDI_2026_IN_FORCE_WITH_2028_APPLICATION__JURISDICTION_SCOPE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The EU CMDI reform entered into force on 10 May 2026 but the Commission states that the new reform applies from May 2028. Bank-resolution answers therefore must distinguish present law, transition law and future application, and must still check the relevant banking-union/national regime.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — A stronger framework for handling bank failures**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/publications/stronger-framework-handling-bank-failures_en
  - Verified point: The new CMDI framework entered into force on 10 May 2026; the reform is stated to apply from May 2028.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留銀行／存款關係、銀行業入口與銀行危機總路由；154–156分別取得資本流動性、系統風險、零售銀行行為優先權。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-103-BANKING_DEPOSITS_BANK_RESOLUTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "銀行／存款與銀行危機處理法"
  english_name: "Banking / Deposits & Bank Resolution Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "誰可依法吸收存款、提供銀行信用與支付核心功能；deposit、custody、client asset如何區分；bank如何在capital、liquidity、asset quality、governance、large exposures與operational risk下被監管；bank失敗時deposit insurance、central-bank liquidity、recovery、resolution、bail-in、bridge bank、sale與ordinary insolvency如何分工？"

  historical_reason:
    "銀行以可即時或短期提取的負債支持較長期、較不流動且有信用風險的資產，並同時承擔支付與信用創造功能。這種期限轉換與高槓桿使銀行對confidence極敏感：即使資產長期仍有價值，也可能因短期提款壓力失去流動性；反之資金充裕也可能掩蓋資產已經虧損。銀行法因此發展出牌照、審慎監理、存款保險、早期干預與特殊resolution。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "deposit convertibility vs maturity transformation"
    - "credit creation vs prudential restraint"
    - "bank profitability vs capital / liquidity buffers"
    - "depositor confidence vs moral hazard"
    - "bank secrecy vs AML / supervision / tax / justice"
    - "home-state group efficiency vs host-state depositor protection"
    - "shareholder rights vs resolution speed"
    - "creditor hierarchy vs systemic stability"
    - "central-bank liquidity vs fiscal solvency support"
    - "digital bank innovation vs licence perimeter"
    - "algorithmic lending efficiency vs fair treatment"
    - "deposit guarantee vs market discipline"
    - "ordinary insolvency fairness vs critical-function continuity"

  shortest_maxim:
    "銀行法第一眼不是『這家銀行還有錢嗎』；先問『它吸收誰的存款、資產負債期限怎麼錯配、capital與liquidity各在哪裡、deposit受不受保障、若今天不能繼續營業哪個critical function必須在明天早上仍能運作』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 109.1 第一眼｜BANKING FUNCTION → LICENCE / ENTITY → DEPOSIT / FUNDING → ASSETS / CREDIT → CAPITAL → LIQUIDITY → GOVERNANCE / RISK → DEPOSITOR PROTECTION → RECOVERY → RESOLUTION → INSOLVENCY / REMEDY

```text
BANKING FUNCTION
＝deposit-taking？lending？payments？custody？market making？

LICENCE / ENTITY
＝bank？credit institution？branch？subsidiary？digital bank？

DEPOSIT / FUNDING
＝retail deposits？wholesale funding？bonds？repo？

ASSETS / CREDIT
＝loans？securities？interbank？derivatives？

CAPITAL
＝loss-absorbing equity / eligible instruments？

LIQUIDITY
＝cash / central-bank reserves / liquid assets / funding access？

GOVERNANCE / RISK
＝credit / market / IRRBB / operational / concentration？

DEPOSITOR PROTECTION
＝insured? limit? payout? transfer?

RECOVERY
＝management actions before failure?

RESOLUTION
＝sale? bridge? bail-in? transfer? stay?

INSOLVENCY / REMEDY
＝residual estate? creditor claims? review?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 109.30 Evidence architecture

- licence；
- legal entity / branch chart；
- deposit terms；
- ledger；
- insured-deposit data；
- capital ratios；
- liquidity metrics；
- asset-quality review；
- collateral；
- concentration；
- board risk reports；
- supervisory findings；
- recovery plan；
- resolution plan；
- valuation；
- transfer / bail-in instrument；
- deposit-insurance payout；
- central-bank support；
- creditor hierarchy。

核心：

> **銀行案最危險的捷徑，是把「帳上有錢」「監管比率合格」「政府介入」其中任何一件事當成銀行健康、存款安全或投資人受保的完整證明。**

## 109.32 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依bank、entity、jurisdiction、date現查：

1. banking activity definition；
2. deposit-taking prohibition / licence；
3. bank categories；
4. branch / subsidiary；
5. capital；
6. liquidity；
7. leverage；
8. credit risk / provisioning；
9. large exposures；
10. related-party lending；
11. operational resilience；
12. governance；
13. consumer credit；
14. interest / fees；
15. discrimination；
16. bank secrecy；
17. AML / sanctions；
18. deposit insurance eligibility / limit；
19. foreign branch coverage；
20. recovery planning；
21. early intervention；
22. resolution trigger；
23. bail-in / transfer / bridge powers；
24. resolution valuation；
25. creditor safeguards；
26. resolution funding；
27. central-bank liquidity；
28. fiscal support；
29. bank insolvency residual regime；
30. Basel 2024 domestic implementation；
31. IADI 2025 use；
32. FSB Key Attributes implementation；
33. administrative / judicial review。

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:

  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 112｜DLM-106｜保證／獨立擔保法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B36"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-106-SURETYSHIP_INDEPENDENT_GUARANTEES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保證／獨立擔保法"
  english_name: "Suretyship / Independent Guarantees Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "第三人以何種undertaking支持principal obligation；該undertaking是accessory suretyship、co-debt、indemnity、independent demand guarantee、counter-guarantee或standby letter of credit；何時成立、何時可demand、guarantor/issuer有哪些defences、payment後如何subrogation / recourse、underlying dispute與documentary independence如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "商業交易常要求一個信用較高的第三人承擔『如果主債務人不履行，就由我付款』的風險。傳統suretyship把擔保責任緊貼主債務；大型工程與跨境交易則發展出demand guarantee / standby，使beneficiary能先憑符合文件的demand取得流動性，再將underlying dispute留到之後處理。這使保證法的核心變成：哪些undertakings是accessory、哪些independent，何時付款機制可以和最終實體責任分離。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "credit support vs over-enforcement"
    - "accessory liability vs independence"
    - "fast documentary payment vs underlying justice"
    - "beneficiary protection vs abusive demand"
    - "bank certainty vs fraud / illegality control"
    - "guarantor recourse vs principal insolvency"
    - "commercial autonomy vs consumer/small-guarantor protection"
    - "counter-guarantee chain vs role confusion"
    - "strict compliance vs trivial documentary defects"
    - "expiry certainty vs latent underlying claims"
    - "corporate authority vs third-party reliance"
    - "multiple guarantors vs contribution"
    - "guarantee wording vs mandatory law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保證法第一眼不是『有人願意擔保』；先問『這是accessory還是independent undertaking、誰是principal / guarantor / beneficiary、demand條件寫什麼、主債抗辯能不能進來、付款後誰向誰追償』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 112.1 第一眼｜UNDERLYING OBLIGATION → INSTRUMENT TYPE → PARTIES / AUTHORITY → SCOPE → CONDITIONS OF DEMAND → PRESENTATION → DEFENCES / FRAUD → HONOUR → SUBROGATION / RECOURSE → INSOLVENCY / REMEDY

```text
UNDERLYING OBLIGATION
＝loan？construction？sale？tax/customs? lease?

INSTRUMENT TYPE
＝suretyship？joint debt？indemnity？demand guarantee？standby?

PARTIES / AUTHORITY
＝principal/applicant？guarantor/issuer？beneficiary？counter-guarantor?

SCOPE
＝amount？currency？interest？costs？duration？

CONDITIONS OF DEMAND
＝simple demand？statement of breach？certificate？documents?

PRESENTATION
＝where？when？original/electronic? partial draw?

DEFENCES / FRAUD
＝principal defences? manifest abuse? illegality?

HONOUR
＝pay now? reject? partial honour?

SUBROGATION / RECOURSE
＝guarantor against principal / co-guarantor?

INSOLVENCY / REMEDY
＝claim rank? restitution? injunction? damages?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 112.31 Evidence architecture

- underlying contract；
- guarantee text；
- governing law；
- incorporated URDG / ISP；
- application / reimbursement agreement；
- board / authority；
- amendments；
- demand；
- supporting documents；
- presentation timestamp；
- bank examination；
- rejection / honour notice；
- fraud evidence；
- payment；
- subrogation / recourse；
- principal / guarantor insolvency。

核心：

> **保證案不能把「主債有沒有違約」與「擔保文件此刻要不要付款」混成一個問題。先分類undertaking，再決定underlying merits能進到哪一層。**

26. contribution；
27. collateral impairment；
28. principal insolvency；

```yaml
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B36"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-107-LEASING_FINANCIAL_LEASING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "租賃／融資租賃法"
  english_name: "Leasing / Financial Leasing Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "資產owner / lessor在何種期間與條件下把possession / use交給lessee；交易是ordinary lease、operating lease、financial lease、hire-purchase、sale-and-leaseback還是實質secured financing；supplier、lessor、lessee對selection、delivery、defect、maintenance、rent、residual value、default、repossession、third parties與insolvency如何分工？"

  historical_reason:
    "企業需要設備卻未必希望一次買斷，資金提供者也可能比直接貸款更願意保留asset title。金融租賃因此形成典型三角關係：lessee選設備與supplier，lessor出資購買並讓lessee長期使用，租金回收資本成本。這種結構介於物權、契約與金融之間；若不先分類，最容易把title、economic ownership、產品責任與信用風險混成一件事。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "title ownership vs economic use / risk"
    - "financing function vs lease form"
    - "lessor title vs lessee possession"
    - "supplier defect vs rent obligation"
    - "non-cancellability vs fairness"
    - "residual value vs purchase option"
    - "asset maintenance vs financing role"
    - "repossession speed vs due process"
    - "registration / third-party effect vs substance"
    - "sale-and-leaseback liquidity vs recharacterisation"
    - "fixture rules vs movable finance"
    - "lessor insolvency vs lessee continuity"
    - "lessee insolvency vs asset recovery"

  shortest_maxim:
    "租賃法第一眼不是『東西是誰的』；先問『誰選asset、誰買、誰實際控制使用、誰承擔supplier / maintenance / casualty風險、租金是在付使用還是在回收finance、default時title究竟能做到什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 113.1 第一眼｜ASSET → TRANSACTION TYPE → LESSOR / LESSEE / SUPPLIER → TITLE / POSSESSION → DELIVERY / ACCEPTANCE → RENT → MAINTENANCE / RISK → OPTION / RESIDUAL → DEFAULT → THIRD PARTIES → INSOLVENCY / REMEDY

```text
ASSET
＝equipment？vehicle？machine？immovable？software-linked equipment?

TRANSACTION TYPE
＝ordinary lease？operating？financial？hire-purchase？sale-leaseback?

LESSOR / LESSEE / SUPPLIER
＝who selected / bought / uses?

TITLE / POSSESSION
＝who owns? who possesses? who registers?

DELIVERY / ACCEPTANCE
＝supplier performance? inspection? acceptance?

RENT
＝fixed? variable? non-cancellable? tax?

MAINTENANCE / RISK
＝repair? insurance? casualty? compliance?

OPTION / RESIDUAL
＝purchase? renewal? return? residual-value risk?

DEFAULT
＝rent default? misuse? insolvency?

THIRD PARTIES
＝creditors? land owner? purchaser?

INSOLVENCY / REMEDY
＝repossession? stay? continuation? damages?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 113.34 Evidence architecture

- asset description / serial；
- supply agreement；
- lease agreement；
- supplier selection；
- purchase invoice；
- title / registry；
- delivery；
- acceptance certificate；
- rent schedule；
- maintenance；
- insurance；
- defect reports；
- option；
- residual guarantee；
- default notice；
- repossession；
- assignment；
- insolvency filing；
- fixture / land records。

核心：

> **租賃案不能只看「誰名字在權狀上」或「誰每月付款」。要把title、possession、finance、supplier risk、residual value與default remedy拆開，才能知道它究竟是租賃還是被租賃外觀包住的融資。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-108-FACTORING_RECEIVABLES_FINANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保理／應收帳款融資法"
  english_name: "Factoring / Receivables Finance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "supplier / assignor對customer / debtor的receivable如何被有效轉讓給factor / assignee；交易是factoring、invoice discounting、forfaiting、receivables sale、secured loan還是reverse factoring；notice、future receivables、anti-assignment clause、debtor defences、set-off、priority、recourse / non-recourse、collection、dilution、duplicate financing與insolvency如何決定誰真正拿得到這筆帳款？"

  historical_reason:
    "企業最常見的資產之一不是現金而是『別人未來要付我的錢』。若只能等90天收款，成長企業會被working capital卡住。Factoring把receivables轉讓給factor，換取提前資金、帳務、收款或credit protection；但一筆債權同時牽涉supplier、factor、debtor與其他creditors，所以必須回答assignment是否成立、debtor向誰付款才免責、factor能否對抗第三人，以及原交易爭議能否跟著債權一起進來。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "liquidity access vs debtor protection"
    - "assignment freedom vs anti-assignment clauses"
    - "future receivables finance vs asset certainty"
    - "confidential financing vs debtor notice"
    - "factor priority vs competing financiers"
    - "non-recourse credit transfer vs supplier performance risk"
    - "fast invoice finance vs fraud / duplicate assignment"
    - "collection efficiency vs customer relationships"
    - "cross-border assignment vs conflict of laws"
    - "true sale vs secured loan recharacterisation"
    - "supply-chain finance vs hidden leverage"
    - "digital invoice data vs underlying performance"
    - "registry certainty vs debtor payment certainty"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保理法第一眼不是『有invoice所以可以融資』；先問『真正receivable從哪個contract生成、誰是debtor、債權是否存在／可轉讓、factor取得的是sale還是security、debtor何時收到有效notice、第三人priority又在哪一步成立』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 114.1 第一眼｜UNDERLYING CONTRACT → RECEIVABLE → TRANSACTION TYPE → ASSIGNOR / FACTOR / DEBTOR → ASSIGNMENT → NOTICE → THIRD-PARTY EFFECT → DEFENCES / SET-OFF → RECOURSE / CREDIT RISK → COLLECTION → DEFAULT / INSOLVENCY → REMEDY

```text
UNDERLYING CONTRACT
＝goods? services? licence? subscription?

RECEIVABLE
＝existing? future? conditional? disputed?

TRANSACTION TYPE
＝factoring? invoice discounting? receivables sale? secured loan? reverse factoring?

ASSIGNOR / FACTOR / DEBTOR
＝supplier? factor? customer? sub-factor?

ASSIGNMENT
＝present? future? bulk? partial?

NOTICE
＝debtor informed? payment instruction changed?

THIRD-PARTY EFFECT
＝registry? perfection? priority?

DEFENCES / SET-OFF
＝quality dispute? credit note? counterclaim?

RECOURSE / CREDIT RISK
＝with recourse? non-recourse? dilution?

COLLECTION
＝factor? supplier? servicer?

DEFAULT / INSOLVENCY
＝assignor? debtor? factor?

REMEDY
＝collection? priority? clawback? damages?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 114.40 Evidence architecture

- underlying sale/service contract；
- purchase order；
- delivery / acceptance；
- invoice；
- credit notes；
- assignment agreement；
- receivable schedule；
- debtor notice；
- acknowledgment；
- registry；
- competing filings；
- factor advance；
- recourse terms；
- collection account；
- payment；
- disputes / set-off；
- duplicate-financing evidence；
- insolvency filing；
- trade credit insurance。

核心：

> **保理案最危險的錯誤，是把invoice、assignment與priority壓成一件事。Invoice要先證明債權；assignment才決定factor取得什麼；notice決定debtor向誰付款；priority再決定factor能不能對抗別的資金提供者。**

## FAILURE_MODES

## 114.41 常見誤分類

```yaml

  - "anti-assignment＝assignment一定無效。"

  - "UNIDROIT 1988 Convention＝全球 factoring code。"
  - "UNIDROIT 2023 Model Law＝treaty。"
  - "UN 2001 Receivables Convention＝已生效。"
```

```yaml
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B52"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-156-FINANCIAL_CONSUMER_RETAIL_BANKING_CONDUCT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "金融消費者／零售銀行行為法"
  english_name: "Financial Consumer / Retail Banking Conduct Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "銀行、consumer-credit provider、payment-account provider或其他零售金融服務提供者，從產品設計、廣告、pre-contract disclosure、creditworthiness、定價、數位介面、銷售、服務、arrears、debt collection、fraud/scam response到complaint/redress，應如何公平、透明且負責任地對待consumer；同意、簽名與風險揭露何時仍不足以消除不公平、脆弱性、mis-selling或法定保護？"

  historical_reason:
    "零售金融契約表面上多由消費者自願簽署，但金融產品具有複雜定價、長期限、資訊不對稱、行為偏誤與高退出成本。數位化、即時授信、BNPL、AI credit scoring與詐騙又使『點一下同意』更不足以代表理解與公平。金融消費者法因此不只要求資訊存在，也逐漸要求product quality、responsible lending、fair treatment、vulnerability protection、data safeguards、complaints與effective redress。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識consumer / product / provider / intermediary / channel / transaction date / applicable sector law，再拆產品設計、廣告、pre-contract information、creditworthiness、fees、sales conduct、servicing、arrears、data、fraud與redress；不要只因consumer signed就結束分析。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "consumer autonomy vs information / behavioural asymmetry"
    - "credit access vs over-indebtedness"
    - "speed / digital convenience vs meaningful understanding"
    - "risk-based pricing vs discrimination / vulnerability"
    - "product innovation vs regulatory perimeter"
    - "provider profitability vs fair treatment"
    - "automation / AI efficiency vs explainability / fairness"
    - "fraud prevention vs consumer reimbursement / allocation of loss"
    - "debt recovery vs dignity / proportionality"
    - "financial inclusion vs unsuitable product exposure"
    - "standard disclosure vs outcome-based consumer protection"
    - "contractual consent vs mandatory protection"

  shortest_maxim:
    "金融消費者法第一眼不是『他有沒有簽』；先問『他買的是什麼金融產品、誰在何種資訊與權力結構下設計和銷售、成本與風險怎麼呈現、授信是否負責任、後續服務與救濟是否讓同意真正具有資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-126.1 第一眼｜CONSUMER → PRODUCT → PROVIDER → CHANNEL → DESIGN → DISCLOSURE → CREDITWORTHINESS / SUITABILITY → FEES → SERVICING → ARREARS / FRAUD → COMPLAINT → REDRESS

```text
CONSUMER
＝natural person outside trade/business? vulnerable circumstances?

PRODUCT
＝deposit? account? overdraft? card? personal loan? mortgage? BNPL?

PROVIDER
＝bank? non-bank lender? intermediary? fintech? merchant-linked credit?

CHANNEL
＝branch? app? platform? phone? agent?

DESIGN
＝target market? complexity? repayment structure? default consequence?

DISCLOSURE
＝APR / total cost / fees / key risk / comparison?

CREDITWORTHINESS / SUITABILITY
＝can consumer repay? is product appropriate under applicable regime?

FEES
＝interest? penalty? overdraft? account fee? early repayment?

SERVICING
＝statements? changes? switching? support?

ARREARS / FRAUD
＝forbearance? debt collection? scam / unauthorised payment?

COMPLAINT / REDRESS
＝internal complaint? ombudsman? regulator? court?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-126.24 Evidence architecture

- consumer / business-purpose status；
- product / account / credit agreement；
- advertising；
- pre-contract disclosures；
- APR / fees / total cost calculations；
- creditworthiness inputs；
- AI / scoring model governance；
- consent / e-signature logs；
- sales call / chat / branch notes；
- vulnerability indicators and support records；
- account statements；
- arrears / collection records；
- fraud alerts / authentication data；
- complaint history；
- ombuds / ADR / regulator decisions；
- applicable law by transaction date；
- transition / grandfathering provisions。

核心：

> **金融消費案件不能從『他按了同意』閉合；要重建產品、資訊、授信、銷售、服務、損害與救濟整條生命週期。**

# G2-127｜第五十二批交界辨識器

```yaml
BATCH_52_INTERSECTION_ROUTER:

  BANK_GOING_CONCERN_RESILIENCE:
    primary: "DLM-154"
    ask:
      - "capital quality / RWA / leverage?"
      - "LCR / NSFR / funding concentration?"
      - "Basel vs domestic implementation?"
      - "CRR3 / CRD6 / FRTB timing?"

  SYSTEM_WIDE_RISK:
    primary: "DLM-155"
    ask:
      - "CCyB / SyRB / systemic buffer?"
      - "G-SIB / O-SII designation?"
      - "housing / leverage / interconnectedness?"
      - "reciprocity / buffer release?"

  RETAIL_FINANCIAL_CONDUCT:
    primary: "DLM-156"
    ask:
      - "consumer credit / account / mortgage / BNPL?"
      - "disclosure / APR / creditworthiness?"
      - "AI lending / vulnerability / debt collection?"
      - "complaint / redress / fraud allocation?"

  BANK_DEPOSITS_FAILURE_RESOLUTION:
    primary: "DLM-103"
    rule:
      - "deposit insurance / bank run / recovery / resolution / bail-in / bridge bank / insolvency continue to route to DLM-103."
      - "do not create duplicate domain from DLM-155."

  GENERAL_FINANCIAL_REGULATION:
    primary: "DLM-011"
    role:
      - "mother card / perimeter / overall financial-regulation first move"

  PAYMENT_EXECUTION:
    primary: "DLM-104"
    secondary: "DLM-156"
    rule:
      - "payment execution / safeguarding / e-money / clearing first route DLM-104; retail fair-treatment consequences may additionally trigger DLM-156."

  CONSUMER_BASELINE:
    primary: "DLM-018"
    secondary: "DLM-156"

  AML_CFT:
    primary: "DLM-068"
    rule:
      - "AML customer due diligence is not the same as consumer creditworthiness or prudential supervision."

  AI_DATA_CYBER:
    routes:
      - "DLM-097 AI governance"
      - "DLM-098 cybersecurity"
      - "DLM-133 privacy / personal data"
      - "DLM-135 cross-border data"

  SECURITIES_CONDUCT:
    primary: "DLM-147"
    secondary: "DLM-156"
    rule:
      - "retail securities recommendation / broker conduct first route DLM-147; general financial-consumer principles may supplement."

  INVESTMENT_FIRM_PRUDENTIAL:
    primary: "DLM-150"
    rule:
      - "bank prudential capital is DLM-154; investment-firm prudential regime is DLM-150 unless law reclassifies the firm into bank-like treatment."
```

最重要的 batch-52 routing lock：

```text
BANK CAPITAL / LIQUIDITY
!= SYSTEMIC RISK POLICY
!= RETAIL CUSTOMER CONDUCT
!= BANK RESOLUTION
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "MORTGAGE_CREDIT_BASELINE_CURRENT__ARREARS_FORECLOSURE_GUIDANCE_CURRENT__FORECLOSURE_AND_HOME_PROTECTION_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Directive 2014/17/EU remains the EU mortgage-credit baseline and is transposed across EU Member States. EBA arrears/foreclosure guidance is applicable and its compliance table was updated in May 2026, but judicial/non-judicial foreclosure, cure, sale, deficiency, redemption and possession remain jurisdiction-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Mortgage credit**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/consumer-finance-and-payments/retail-financial-services/credit/mortgage-credit_en
  - Verified point: Directive 2014/17/EU remains the EU mortgage-credit baseline and has been transposed by all EU Member States.
- **European Banking Authority — Guidelines on arrears and foreclosure**
  - URL: https://www.eba.europa.eu/activities/single-rulebook/regulatory-activities/consumer-protection-and-financial-innovation/guidelines-arrears-and-foreclosure
  - Verified point: Amended EBA arrears/foreclosure Guidelines are applicable; the compliance table was last updated 26 May 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-196-RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGE_CONSUMER_HOME_CREDIT_FORECLOSURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "住宅抵押／消費型房貸與止贖法"
  english_name: "Residential Mortgage / Consumer Home Credit & Foreclosure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "自然人以住宅不動產作擔保，或以credit取得／保有住宅權利時，creditor / intermediary從廣告、ESIS / pre-contract information、creditworthiness、valuation、rate/currency、contract、registration到servicing應負何種義務；借款人arrears後何時必須forbear、何時可acceleration、啟動foreclosure / repossession / sale，出售價格、home-loss程序與residual debt又如何被保護與審查？"

  historical_reason:
    "住宅房貸把家庭最重要的長期居住資產與高額、長期限、利率敏感的金融負債綁在一起。一般消費貸款只失去信用或收入時，房貸違約可能同時造成失去住所；一般抵押物執行只談priority與拍賣時，住宅制度還會加入creditworthiness、估價、foreclosure前forbearance、程序保障與剩餘債務問題。因此房貸既是金融消費法、土地擔保法，也是居住喪失風險的執行法。"

  first_move:
    "先確認consumer、credit purpose、residential property與security，再固定origination date、pre-contract disclosure、creditworthiness、valuation、rate/currency、registration與servicing；違約後先重建arrears、forbearance、acceleration、notice與foreclosure authority，最後才進sale price、possession、residual debt與remedy。不得從『有抵押＋違約』直接跳到『房屋可立即拍賣』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "access to home credit vs over-indebtedness"
    - "secured-credit certainty vs home protection"
    - "fast enforcement vs reasonable forbearance"
    - "creditor priority vs borrower procedural rights"
    - "market valuation vs forced-sale discount"
    - "variable / foreign-currency pricing vs consumer understanding"
    - "responsible underwriting vs financial inclusion"
    - "contract acceleration vs proportionality / mandatory safeguards"
    - "property sale vs residual-debt rehabilitation"
    - "mortgage market liquidity vs individual home loss"

  shortest_maxim:
    "房貸止贖第一眼不是『借款人欠錢、銀行有抵押』；先問『這是哪種住宅credit、授信與估價是否合格、違約前後程序走到哪、是否先有合理forbearance、誰有權加速、何時能賣、賣多少、賣完是否還欠』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-48.1 第一眼｜BORROWER → PURPOSE → PROPERTY / SECURITY → CREDIT PRODUCT → ORIGINATION → DISCLOSURE → CREDITWORTHINESS → VALUATION → RATE / CURRENCY → SERVICING → ARREARS → FORBEARANCE → ACCELERATION → FORECLOSURE → SALE → RESIDUAL DEBT → REMEDY

```text
BORROWER
＝consumer? owner-occupier? buy-to-let? business purpose?

PURPOSE
＝acquire? retain? refinance? renovate?

PROPERTY / SECURITY
＝residential immovable property? mortgage? comparable security?

CREDIT PRODUCT
＝fixed? variable? foreign-currency? bridge? interest-only?

ORIGINATION
＝creditor? intermediary? advice?

DISCLOSURE
＝ESIS / APRC / costs / risks / reflection period?

CREDITWORTHINESS
＝income? obligations? affordability? reliable information?

VALUATION
＝independent / competent? market value? conflicts?

RATE / CURRENCY
＝index? reset? conversion? exchange risk?

SERVICING
＝statements? changes? complaints?

ARREARS
＝amount? cause? fees? communication?

FORBEARANCE
＝restructure? term extension? payment plan? sale period?

ACCELERATION
＝lawful trigger? notice? cure?

FORECLOSURE
＝judicial? non-judicial? authorization? possession?

SALE
＝valuation? auction? best efforts / fair price?

RESIDUAL DEBT
＝shortfall? repayment arrangement? insolvency?

REMEDY
＝stay? challenge? damages? unfair term? debt adjustment?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-48.14 Evidence architecture

- credit application；
- consumer-purpose evidence；
- ESIS / disclosures；
- creditworthiness file；
- income / debt records；
- appraisal / valuation；
- mortgage deed / registration；
- loan contract；
- rate / currency terms；
- account statements；
- arrears ledger；
- hardship communications；
- forbearance assessment；
- default notice；
- acceleration notice；
- foreclosure filing；
- service proof；
- auction / sale documents；
- valuation at enforcement；
- distribution statement；
- residual-debt calculation；
- possession / eviction order。

核心：

> **房貸案的證據不是一紙抵押設定；它是一條從授信到失去住宅、再到剩餘債務的長期生命週期。**

## G3-48.16 Positive Law Gate

以下必須現查：

1. consumer / business purpose；
2. residential-property scope；
3. creditor / intermediary authorization；
4. pre-contract disclosure / ESIS；
5. reflection / withdrawal rights；
6. APRC / fees；
7. creditworthiness；
8. valuation；
9. variable rate；
10. foreign currency；
11. early repayment；
12. mortgage creation / registration；
13. priority；
14. servicing；
15. default definition；
16. default charges；
17. hardship / forbearance；
18. acceleration；
19. foreclosure authorization；
20. judicial / non-judicial route；
21. notice / service；
22. sale method / valuation；
23. surplus / shortfall；
24. residual debt；
25. unfair terms；
26. tenant / occupant protection；
27. insolvency；
28. limitation；
29. Directive 2014/17/EU current scope / domestic implementation。

```yaml
  - "房價高於貸款＝授信一定負責任。"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "PAYMENT_FRAMEWORK_DYNAMIC__INSTANT_PAYMENTS_PHASED__PSD2_REVIEW_POLITICAL_AGREEMENT_NOT_UNIFORMLY_APPLICABLE"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

EU payment law is in transition: instant-payment obligations applied in phases in 2025 with further non-euro-area phases in 2027, while the PSD2 review had reached political agreement by 27 November 2025. Current operative text and Member-State implementation must therefore be separated from agreed/future reform.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Payment services**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/consumer-finance-and-payments/payment-services/payment-services_en
  - Verified point: Instant-payment obligations are phased (2025 and 2027); the PSD2 review reached political agreement on 27 November 2025.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-104-PAYMENTS_CLEARING_ELECTRONIC_MONEY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "支付／清算與電子貨幣法"
  english_name: "Payments / Clearing & Electronic Money Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "payer發出payment instruction後，誰負authentication / execution / safeguarding / FX / routing / settlement duties；issuer、acquirer、PSP、network、processor、clearing house、settlement bank與merchant如何分工；payment何時authorised、何時irrevocable、何時final、錯誤／詐欺／chargeback如何處理；e-money與bank deposit、stablecoin、stored value如何分流？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代付款很少是兩人直接交付現金，而是透過多個金融與技術節點更新帳本。支付速度越快、參與者越多，越需要finality、liquidity、operational resilience與consumer allocation rules；否則一筆撤回或participant failure可能沿清算網路傳染。非銀行PSP、e-money、fast payments與跨境平台又使傳統『銀行匯款』分類不足。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "speed vs fraud detection"
    - "settlement finality vs error correction"
    - "consumer reimbursement vs moral hazard"
    - "open access vs systemic risk"
    - "bank vs non-bank PSP regulation"
    - "innovation vs safeguarding"
    - "interoperability vs cyber / operational concentration"
    - "cross-border efficiency vs AML / sanctions / data laws"
    - "transparent fees vs complex correspondent chains"
    - "instant payment vs liquidity management"
    - "payment privacy vs traceability"
    - "commercial-bank money vs central-bank settlement money"
    - "e-money convenience vs redemption / insolvency protection"

  shortest_maxim:
    "支付法第一眼不是『錢有沒有到』；先問『誰發指令、誰驗證、誰持有資金、經哪個clearing / settlement rail、哪一刻變成irrevocable / final、如果是fraud或error由哪個participant先負擔再追償』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 110.1 第一眼｜PAYER / PAYEE → PAYMENT INSTRUMENT → PSP ROLES → AUTHORISATION → AUTHENTICATION → EXECUTION / ROUTING → CLEARING → SETTLEMENT ASSET → FINALITY → ERROR / FRAUD → REVERSAL / REMEDY

```text
PAYER / PAYEE
＝consumer? business? government?

PAYMENT INSTRUMENT
＝card? account transfer? direct debit? e-money? instant payment?

PSP ROLES
＝issuer? acquirer? bank? wallet? processor? network?

AUTHORISATION
＝did payer legally authorise?

AUTHENTICATION
＝how credentials were verified?

EXECUTION / ROUTING
＝who forwarded instruction?

CLEARING
＝obligations calculated / exchanged?

SETTLEMENT ASSET
＝central-bank money? commercial-bank money?

FINALITY
＝when irrevocable / unconditional?

ERROR / FRAUD
＝mistake? scam? stolen credential? duplicate?

REVERSAL / REMEDY
＝recall? chargeback? refund? restitution? court?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 110.32 Evidence architecture

- payment instruction；
- consent / mandate；
- device / authentication logs；
- issuer / acquirer records；
- network messages；
- clearing files；
- settlement accounts；
- timestamps；
- sanctions / AML flags；
- FX / fee data；
- chargeback；
- recall；
- safeguarding accounts；
- e-money ledger；
- outage logs；
- customer communications；
- system rules；
- finality statute；
- insolvency records。

核心：

> **支付案最容易被一張手機截圖騙走：畫面寫「成功」只能證明某個app state。真正要問的是哪個participant的哪個ledger在什麼時間產生什麼不可逆法律效果。**


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-109-NEGOTIABLE_INSTRUMENTS_BILLS_NOTES_CHEQUES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "票據／可轉讓支付工具法"
  english_name: "Negotiable Instruments / Bills, Notes & Cheques Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一張bill of exchange、promissory note、cheque或其他可轉讓支付工具，何時符合形式並有效簽發；誰是drawer / maker / drawee / acceptor / payee / endorser / holder；instrument如何透過delivery / endorsement流通，何時產生acceptance、presentment、dishonour、notice、recourse與較強holder protection；原交易抗辯、偽造／無權簽名與票據責任如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "現金不適合長距離、大額與延期商業交易。票據制度把『未來付款』壓縮成可攜帶、可背書、可提示、可再融資的書面／電子工具，使交易人不必每次都重新調查原始買賣關係。但越讓instrument能脫離underlying transaction流通，就越需要嚴格形式、簽名責任、holder地位、抗辯分類與dishonour程序。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "transferability vs debtor defences"
    - "formal certainty vs underlying justice"
    - "holder protection vs theft / fraud"
    - "signature liability vs agency / forgery"
    - "speed of payment vs verification"
    - "paper possession vs electronic control"
    - "instrument autonomy vs underlying contract"
    - "bank clearing efficiency vs payer stop-payment rights"
    - "good-faith acquisition vs true owner claims"
    - "endorsement formalism vs commercial practice"
    - "maturity certainty vs acceleration / extension"
    - "cross-border uniformity vs divergent domestic traditions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "票據法第一眼不是『他欠多少錢』；先問『這張紙／record究竟是不是票據、誰簽了哪一個票據位置、誰現在是holder、怎麼取得、何時提示／承兌／拒付，原交易抗辯能不能對這個holder主張』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 115.1 第一眼｜INSTRUMENT TYPE → FORM → SIGNATURE / AUTHORITY → ISSUE → HOLDER CHAIN → ACCEPTANCE → MATURITY → PRESENTMENT → DISHONOUR → DEFENCES → RECOURSE / ENFORCEMENT

```text
INSTRUMENT TYPE
＝bill of exchange？promissory note？cheque？ordinary IOU？

FORM
＝unconditional order/promise？sum certain？payee/order? date? signature?

SIGNATURE / AUTHORITY
＝maker? drawer? agent? forged?

ISSUE
＝when delivered for legal effect?

HOLDER CHAIN
＝payee → endorsement → transferee?

ACCEPTANCE
＝drawee accepted bill?

MATURITY
＝sight? fixed date? after sight?

PRESENTMENT
＝for acceptance? for payment?

DISHONOUR
＝non-acceptance? non-payment?

DEFENCES
＝real/absolute? personal? holder status?

RECOURSE / ENFORCEMENT
＝drawer? endorser? maker? acceptor? court?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 115.33 Evidence architecture

- original instrument / authoritative electronic record；
- issue date；
- signatures；
- authority；
- endorsement chain；
- acceptance；
- maturity；
- presentment；
- dishonour；
- notices；
- protest；
- alteration evidence；
- payment / clearing；
- underlying contract；
- holder acquisition circumstances；
- lost/replacement records；
- applicable domestic / treaty law。

核心：

> **票據案不能只問「這張紙寫多少錢」。真正決定權利的是它是不是法定instrument、誰在哪個位置簽名、holder如何取得、程序有沒有走完，以及這個holder究竟要承受多少underlying defences。**

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-110-DOCUMENTARY_CREDITS_COLLECTIONS_TRADE_FINANCE_DOCUMENTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "跟單信用狀／託收與貿易金融單證法"
  english_name: "Documentary Credits / Collections & Trade Finance Documents Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "買方與賣方如何透過banking instruments把陌生交易的payment / performance risk轉換成documentary risk；信用狀中issuer / confirmer何時形成獨立付款undertaking、何謂complying presentation、discrepancy如何處理；託收中bank只是依collection instruction處理documents / payment / acceptance時，誰仍承擔buyer credit risk與goods risk？"

  historical_reason:
    "跨境買賣的核心困局是：賣方怕先出貨後收不到錢，買方怕先付款後拿不到貨。Documentary credit以銀行信用介入，讓bank只依一組預先約定documents判斷是否honour；documentary collection則降低銀行信用介入程度，只讓銀行保管／交付單據並代為收款或取得承兌。制度因此必須嚴格區分underlying sale、documentary undertaking與bank handling role。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "documentary certainty vs real-world goods"
    - "independence vs fraud control"
    - "strict compliance vs commercial triviality"
    - "bank speed vs discrepancy review"
    - "seller payment security vs buyer documentary control"
    - "confirmation protection vs bank counterparty risk"
    - "paper documents vs digital records"
    - "private ICC rules vs mandatory national law"
    - "sanctions / AML screening vs honour timing"
    - "document authenticity vs bank examination limits"
    - "collection economy vs lack of bank payment undertaking"
    - "transport document control vs goods release"
    - "underlying injunction vs autonomy of credit"

  shortest_maxim:
    "貿易金融單證法第一眼不是『貨有沒有問題』；先問『這是credit還是collection、哪家bank承擔什麼undertaking、要求哪些documents、presentation是否符合、銀行此刻是在examining documents還是在替客戶收款』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 116.1 第一眼｜UNDERLYING SALE → TRADE-FINANCE METHOD → BANK ROLES → CREDIT / COLLECTION TERMS → REQUIRED DOCUMENTS → PRESENTATION → EXAMINATION / HANDLING → DISCREPANCY → HONOUR / ACCEPTANCE / RELEASE → FRAUD / SANCTIONS → REIMBURSEMENT / REMEDY

```text
UNDERLYING SALE
＝goods / Incoterm / shipment / payment term?

TRADE-FINANCE METHOD
＝documentary credit? standby? collection? open account?

BANK ROLES
＝issuing? advising? confirming? nominated? remitting? collecting?

CREDIT / COLLECTION TERMS
＝UCP? eUCP? URC? governing law?

REQUIRED DOCUMENTS
＝invoice? bill of lading? insurance? certificate? draft?

PRESENTATION
＝where / when / by whom?

EXAMINATION / HANDLING
＝credit examination? collection instruction?

DISCREPANCY
＝what exact mismatch?

HONOUR / ACCEPTANCE / RELEASE
＝pay? negotiate? accept draft? release docs?

FRAUD / SANCTIONS
＝exception / block / licence?

REIMBURSEMENT / REMEDY
＝applicant-bank? bank-bank? seller-buyer? court?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 116.35 Evidence architecture

- sale contract；
- credit application；
- LC / amendments；
- UCP/eUCP clauses；
- advising / confirmation；
- required documents；
- original/e-record presentation；
- timestamps；
- examination notes；
- discrepancies；
- refusal notice；
- applicant waiver；
- honour / negotiation；
- reimbursement；
- fraud evidence；
- sanctions / AML holds；
- collection instruction；
- D/P / D/A release；
- acceptance / payment；
- bank messages。

核心：

> **貿易金融單證案要強迫自己問兩次：第一，銀行按文件是否要付／放單；第二，現實貨物與買賣究竟有沒有履約。這兩個答案可以同時一個Yes、一個No。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml

  qualified_status: "CSD_SETTLEMENT_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__EU_T1_2027_TRANSITION__2026_PREPARATORY_DEADLINES__TECHNICAL_AND_JURISDICTION_CHECK_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

The EU move to T+1 is legally scheduled for 11 October 2027. ESMA identifies 2026 as a critical preparation year and 7 December 2026 as the first regulatory deadline for allocation/confirmation processes. Current CSD/CCP duties and transition milestones must be checked separately.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ESMA — Central Securities Depositories / T+1**
  - URL: https://www.esma.europa.eu/esmas-activities/markets-and-infrastructure/central-securities-depositories
  - Verified point: Regulation (EU) 2025/2075 mandates the EU transition to T+1 settlement by 11 October 2027.
- **ESMA — Firms to finalise preparations ahead of T+1 deadlines**
  - URL: https://www.esma.europa.eu/press-news/esma-news/esma-calls-firms-finalise-preparations-ahead-t1-settlement-deadlines
  - Verified point: ESMA identifies 11 October 2027 as the T+1 move and 7 December 2026 as the first regulatory deadline for allocation/confirmation processes.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-149-SECURITIES_SETTLEMENT_CSD_CCP_LAW"
  chinese_name: "證券結算／CSD／CCP法"
  english_name: "Securities Settlement / Central Securities Depositories / Central Counterparties Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "證券交易成交後，誰負責matching、clearing、novation、netting、margin、default management、securities safekeeping、book-entry transfer與cash/securities final settlement；CSD、SSS、CCP、clearing member、settlement participant、custodian與client之間的法律資格、資產隔離、settlement finality、fail discipline、recovery / resolution及跨境recognition如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "證券交易的可見事件是『買賣成交』，但真正讓市場可以重複高速交易的是成交後看不見的post-trade infrastructure。若交易對手、CCP、CSD、custodian或settlement bank任一層失敗，會出現principal risk、liquidity risk、custody risk與連鎖違約。因此這個領域不是付款法的附註，也不是券商conduct的尾巴；它治理的是市場承諾何時變成不可逆的權利移轉，以及中央基礎設施如何在高集中度下取得營運與失敗處理資格。"

  first_move:
    "先畫出交易後鏈：trade execution → clearing / CCP → settlement instruction → CSD / SSS → cash leg / securities leg → custody / account entitlement；不要把成交、清算、交割與保管當成同一事件。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "settlement speed vs operational readiness"
    - "netting efficiency vs concentration of infrastructure risk"
    - "CCP risk mutualisation vs moral hazard"
    - "settlement finality vs insolvency avoidance powers"
    - "asset segregation vs omnibus efficiency"
    - "cash penalties vs market liquidity"
    - "mandatory buy-in discipline vs stressed-market stability"
    - "centralisation vs interoperability / competition"
    - "third-country access vs supervisory control"
    - "margin safety vs procyclicality"
    - "default management vs non-defaulting member burden"
    - "recovery continuity vs resolution / public-interest intervention"

  shortest_maxim:
    "成交不是交割；清算不是保管；CCP不是CSD。先把誰成為counterparty、誰記錄證券、誰移轉現金、何時final、失敗由誰吸收畫出來，再談『交易已完成』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-105.1 第一眼｜TRADE → CLEARING → CCP / NOVATION → NETTING → MARGIN → SETTLEMENT INSTRUCTION → CSD / SSS → DVP → FINALITY → CUSTODY → FAIL / DEFAULT → RECOVERY / RESOLUTION

```text
TRADE
＝exchange / MTF / OTC execution?

CLEARING
＝bilateral? centrally cleared?

CCP
＝does a central counterparty interpose / novate?

NETTING
＝gross or net obligations?

MARGIN
＝initial / variation? eligible collateral?

SETTLEMENT INSTRUCTION
＝who instructs whom, when matched?

CSD / SSS
＝where securities are recorded and transferred?

DVP
＝how securities and cash legs are linked?

FINALITY
＝when transfer / netting becomes legally final?

CUSTODY
＝who holds client entitlement and at what tier?

FAIL / DEFAULT
＝settlement fail or participant default?

RECOVERY / RESOLUTION
＝who bears losses and who can intervene?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-105.27 Evidence architecture

- execution record / trade confirmation；
- clearing status / CCP rulebook；
- novation / open-offer terms；
- margin calls / collateral records；
- default-fund contribution；
- settlement instructions；
- matching timestamps；
- CSD participant records；
- securities accounts / reconciliation；
- cash settlement records；
- settlement fail reason / penalty；
- buy-in trigger / Commission implementing act if any；
- CCP authorisation / recognition；
- AAR classification / reports；
- recovery / resolution plans；
- custody chain / segregation records；
- current CSDR / EMIR / national insolvency law。

核心：

> **Post-trade案不能因畫面顯示『成交』就宣布完成。真正的完成資格要追到清算義務、交割指令、證券與現金finality、保管鏈以及失敗時誰吸收損失。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留證券／金融工具辨識、證券市場共同原理與總路由；142–150承擔已專門化的發行、揭露、市場濫用、基金、中介、結算與審慎。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-008-SECURITIES_REGULATION"
  chinese_name: "證券法／資本市場法"
  english_name: "Securities Regulation / Capital Markets Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當企業透過可交易金融工具向不特定或廣泛投資人取得資本，而投資人無法直接掌握企業資訊時，法律如何讓資訊、交易、市場中介與價格形成取得最低可信度？"

  historical_reason:
    "證券市場把企業融資與投資人的資金連結起來，但發行人與內部人通常比外部投資人掌握更多資訊；交易市場又可能受到操縱、詐欺、利益衝突與中介失靈影響。證券法因此不只處理『契約真假』，而是建立揭露、註冊／許可、市場行為、中介監管與執法機制，以支撐投資人保護、市場公平效率透明及系統風險控制。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "資本形成效率 vs 投資人保護"
    - "資訊自由流通 vs 內線資訊不對稱"
    - "市場創新 vs 監管邊界"
    - "企業融資自治 vs 公開市場強制揭露"
    - "交易自由 vs 操縱／詐欺禁止"
    - "速度與流動性 vs 市場完整性"
    - "私人損害救濟 vs 公法監管執法"
    - "個別投資風險 vs 系統性風險"

  shortest_maxim:
    "證券法第一眼不是『投資人有沒有賠錢』；先問『什麼工具、誰在發行／交易、向誰、在哪個市場、靠什麼資訊形成價格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 14.1 第一眼｜INSTRUMENT → ACTOR → TRANSACTION → MARKET → INFORMATION

證券案件先拆五件事：

```text
INSTRUMENT
＝股票？債券？基金？衍生性商品？投資契約？其他受監管金融工具？

ACTOR
＝發行人？內部人？股東？券商？投顧？基金？交易所？平台？投資人？

TRANSACTION
＝發行？私募？公開募集？次級市場交易？收購？回購？

MARKET
＝交易所？OTC？私人交易？跨境平台？

INFORMATION
＝什麼資訊被揭露、遺漏、延遲、誤導或被少數人先取得？
```

如果這五件事沒有先拆，最容易出現：

```text
投資損失
→ 證券詐欺
```

這是不合格的跳躍。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 14.10 證據特徵

證券案件的重要資料常帶有「時間軸」：

- 公開說明書；
- 申報文件；
- 財報；
- 重大訊息；
- 交易所公告；
- 董事會／股東會資料；
- 內部郵件；
- 訊息發布時間；
- 交易紀錄；
- 下單紀錄；
- 帳戶關係；
- 通話／聊天；
- 公司內部知悉時間；
- 投資人交易時間；
- 價格／成交量資料；
- 資金流；
- 分析師報告；
- 承銷文件；
- 客戶適合度／風險評估；
- 監管機關調查材料。

核心：

> **證券法常不是只問「資訊內容」，而是問「誰在什麼時間知道什麼，又在什麼時間做了什麼」。**

---

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 14.12 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依現行法域現查：

1. 「證券／金融商品」定義；
2. 公開募集／私募／豁免；
3. 發行註冊與申報；
4. 持續揭露與重大訊息；
5. 虛假陳述／遺漏責任；
6. 內線交易；
7. 市場操縱；
8. 券商、投顧、基金等中介資格；
9. 交易所與市場規則；
10. 收購、公開收購、持股申報；
11. 民事／行政／刑事責任；
12. 損失因果關係與損害計算；
13. 跨境發行與監管；
14. 新型金融商品／數位資產的監管分類。


ICSID's 2022 Rules remain the current procedural rules. UNCITRAL Working Group III continued ISDS-reform work in 2026, including standing-mechanism and procedural proposals. Jurisdiction still depends on the applicable investment instrument, consent, party status, temporal scope and any treaty-specific limitations.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ICSID — 2022 Rules and Regulations resources**
  - URL: https://icsid.worldbank.org/rules-regulations/2022-rules-and-regulations/resources
  - Verified point: The 2022 ICSID Rules remain the current procedural rules published by ICSID for Convention and Additional Facility proceedings.
- **UNCITRAL — Working Group III: ISDS Reform**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/working_groups/3/investor-state
  - Verified point: The 54th session met 23–27 March 2026 and work continues toward a 55th session on 12–16 October 2026, including standing-mechanism and procedural reforms.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-115-INTERNATIONAL_INVESTMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際投資法"
  english_name: "International Investment Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個foreign investor／investment是否落在特定investment treaty、investment chapter、contract或domestic investment law的保護範圍；國家哪一項measure能歸責、是否違反expropriation、FET、national treatment、MFN、FPS、transfer、umbrella clause或其他義務；又是否存在有效State consent、jurisdiction、admissibility、exceptions與可執行的ISDS路徑？"

  historical_reason:
    "跨境投資人把資本、設備、技術與長期營運放進host State後，面臨一般出口商較少承擔的政治與主權風險：徵收、差別待遇、監管劇變、外匯限制、司法失靈或國營企業行為。傳統外交保護把爭端留在國家與國家之間；現代BIT、FTA investment chapters與ICSID等制度則在特定條件下讓investor直接對State提出國際請求。但這種直接程序也使公共監管、環境、稅、健康與民主政策空間進入國際責任審查，因此其核心永遠是『保護投資』與『保留合法治理空間』的資格平衡。"

  first_move:
    "先找真正的法源與consent，不先問國家措施是否『對投資人不公平』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "investment protection vs regulatory autonomy"
    - "State consent vs investor direct standing"
    - "treaty claim vs contract claim"
    - "stability / legitimate expectations vs democratic regulatory change"
    - "property protection vs public-interest regulation"
    - "nationality planning vs abuse of process"
    - "investor rights vs investor obligations / responsible investment"
    - "confidential dispute resolution vs public transparency"
    - "compensation vs public-finance burden"
    - "old-generation treaties vs reform-oriented new treaties"
    - "arbitral consistency vs decentralized tribunals"
    - "domestic remedies vs international adjudication"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際投資法第一眼不是『外國公司受損了』；先問『哪個treaty保護哪個investor／investment、State是否真的同意這個forum、哪一項measure可歸責、哪一個protection standard被觸發、例外與程序門檻有沒有成立』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-2.1 第一眼｜SOURCE / CONSENT → INVESTOR → INVESTMENT → TEMPORAL / TERRITORIAL SCOPE → STATE MEASURE / ATTRIBUTION → STANDARD OF TREATMENT → EXCEPTIONS → JURISDICTION / ADMISSIBILITY → LIABILITY → DAMAGES → ENFORCEMENT

```text
SOURCE / CONSENT
＝BIT? FTA investment chapter? multilateral treaty? contract? domestic law?

INVESTOR
＝natural person? company? dual national? corporate chain?

INVESTMENT
＝shares? enterprise? concession? debt? IP? project?

TEMPORAL / TERRITORIAL SCOPE
＝when acquired? when measure occurred? treaty in force? survival clause?

STATE MEASURE / ATTRIBUTION
＝legislature? ministry? court? SOE? municipality?

STANDARD
＝expropriation? FET? NT? MFN? FPS? transfer? umbrella?

EXCEPTIONS
＝security? tax? prudential? health? environment? essential interests?

JURISDICTION / ADMISSIBILITY
＝consent? nationality? legality? time bar? waiver? fork-in-road?

LIABILITY
＝breach under exact treaty text?

DAMAGES
＝causation? valuation date? DCF? contributory fault?

ENFORCEMENT
＝ICSID Convention? New York Convention? domestic immunity?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-2.32 Evidence architecture

- exact treaty text / amendments / protocol；
- treaty entry into force / termination / survival；
- nationality documents；
- corporate ownership/control chain；
- investment documents；
- admission/licence；
- State measure；
- attribution evidence；
- specific assurances；
- domestic proceedings；
- consent / notice of dispute；
- cooling-off period；
- waiver / fork-in-road；
- corruption evidence；
- damages model；
- ICSID / UNCITRAL procedural rules；
- award / annulment / set-aside；
- enforcement assets / immunity。

核心：

> **國際投資案不能從「外資被政府傷害」開始閉合。要先證明這個人、這筆資產、這個時間、這個State measure與這個forum真的都落在同一份有拘束力的投資保護與consent鏈裡。**

## G2-2.34 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依treaty、State、investor、investment、date現查：

1. IIA / BIT / FTA text；
2. treaty status；
3. survival / sunset clause；
4. investor definition；
5. investment definition；
6. legality requirement；
7. nationality / dual nationality；
8. corporate control；
9. denial of benefits；
10. temporal scope；
11. territorial scope；
12. State consent；
13. ICSID Convention / Additional Facility；
14. UNCITRAL / other rules；
15. attribution；
16. FET；
17. expropriation；
18. national treatment；
19. MFN；
20. FPS；
21. umbrella clause；
22. transfer；
23. tax carve-out；
24. security / prudential / public-interest exceptions；
25. corruption / abuse；
26. local remedies / litigation period；
27. fork-in-road；
28. waiver；
29. limitation；
30. transparency；
31. third-party funding；
32. damages；
33. annulment / set-aside；
34. recognition / enforcement；
35. State immunity。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-142-PUBLIC_OFFERING_PROSPECTUS_SECURITIES_ISSUANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公開發行／公開說明書法"
  english_name: "Public Offering / Prospectus / Securities Issuance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項證券發行、公開募集、公開銷售或申請交易市場准入，何時必須註冊、申報、核准或提出prospectus／registration statement；誰對發行文件負責、哪些豁免可用、跨境發行如何取得合法市場入口？"

  historical_reason:
    "公開發行把資金募集從少數熟識當事人的私人交易，放大成大量投資人依賴標準化文件作決策的市場事件。發行人掌握企業、財務與風險資訊，投資人則在高度時間壓力與資訊不對稱下投入資金。現代證券發行法因此以registration、prospectus、責任分配、承銷與豁免制度，把『可以向市場要錢』改造成需要取得資格的法律行為；其目的不是替投資人挑好公司，而是讓市場入口本身可被審查。"

  first_move:
    "先判定instrument、offer/sale、issuer/offeror、investor class、jurisdiction與市場入口；不要先從『公司想IPO』或『有prospectus』直接推定已取得合法發行資格。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "capital formation vs investor protection"
    - "mandatory disclosure vs offering cost"
    - "public offering vs private placement"
    - "regulatory approval vs issuer responsibility"
    - "speed to market vs scrutiny"
    - "standardised prospectus vs issuer-specific risk"
    - "cross-border passporting vs national jurisdiction"
    - "underwriter gatekeeping vs commercial incentive"
    - "primary issuance vs secondary distribution"
    - "innovation in capital raising vs regulatory perimeter"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公開發行法第一眼不是『公司有沒有募到錢』；先問『什麼證券、誰向誰提出什麼offer、在哪個法域、靠哪個registration／prospectus／exemption進入市場』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-82.1 第一眼｜INSTRUMENT → OFFER / SALE → ISSUER / OFFEROR → INVESTOR CLASS → JURISDICTION → REGISTRATION / EXEMPTION → DISCLOSURE DOCUMENT → RESPONSIBILITY → EFFECTIVENESS / APPROVAL → DISTRIBUTION

```text
INSTRUMENT
＝share? bond? note? investment contract? fund interest? tokenised security?

OFFER / SALE
＝public offer? private placement? resale? employee plan? crowdfunding?

ISSUER / OFFEROR
＝issuer? selling shareholder? underwriter? intermediary?

INVESTOR CLASS
＝retail? qualified? accredited? institutional? limited offerees?

JURISDICTION
＝where offered? where issuer incorporated? market admitted? cross-border nexus?

REGISTRATION / EXEMPTION
＝registration required? statutory exemption? safe harbour? threshold?

DISCLOSURE DOCUMENT
＝registration statement? prospectus? offering memorandum? base prospectus?

RESPONSIBILITY
＝issuer? directors? offeror? underwriter? expert? auditor?

EFFECTIVENESS / APPROVAL
＝review completed? effective? approved? passported?

DISTRIBUTION
＝when can solicitation / allocation / sale / settlement occur?
```

核心：
