## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-143-ISSUER_CONTINUOUS_DISCLOSURE_SECURITIES_FRAUD_LAW"
  chinese_name: "發行人持續揭露／證券詐欺法"
  english_name: "Issuer Continuous Disclosure / Securities Fraud Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "發行人進入公開資本市場後，哪些定期、即時、事件驅動或重大資訊必須由誰在何時揭露；陳述錯誤、遺漏、延遲或選擇性揭露何時只是申報缺失，何時進一步取得民事、行政或刑事證券詐欺責任資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "首次prospectus只能描述某一時間點。公開市場卻每天以新資訊形成價格，因此現代證券制度必須把揭露從一次性文件延伸為periodic reports、current reports、inside-information disclosure、重大持股與其他持續性資訊制度。与此同时，若任何揭露錯誤都自動等於fraud，則市場風險、會計判斷與商業預測會被事後結果吞噬；因此持續揭露義務與證券詐欺責任必須分層。"

  first_move:
    "先找issuer status、market / listing status、具體disclosure duty、statement / omission、materiality與時間；不要從股價下跌或公司出事倒推出『先前揭露一定詐欺』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "timely disclosure vs confidentiality"
    - "investor information vs information overload"
    - "materiality vs hindsight"
    - "periodic certainty vs event-driven speed"
    - "forecasting vs fraud exposure"
    - "issuer responsibility vs auditor / adviser responsibility"
    - "market price reaction vs legal causation"
    - "public enforcement vs private damages"
    - "selective disclosure vs legitimate confidentiality"
    - "financial reporting judgment vs intentional deception"

  shortest_maxim:
    "持續揭露第一眼不是『股價跌了』；先問『當時存在什麼法定揭露義務、誰說了什麼或漏了什麼、何時應說、資訊是否重大、哪一種責任要件真的成立』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-83.1 第一眼｜REPORTING STATUS → DUTY SOURCE → DISCLOSURE TYPE → STATEMENT / OMISSION → MATERIALITY → TIMING → MENTAL STATE → TRANSACTION / RELIANCE → CAUSATION / LOSS → REMEDY

```text
REPORTING STATUS
＝public issuer? listed? Exchange Act reporting? regulated-market issuer?

DUTY SOURCE
＝statute? regulation? listing rule? MAR? specific undertaking?

DISCLOSURE TYPE
＝annual? half-year? quarterly? current event? inside information? ownership?

STATEMENT / OMISSION
＝what exactly was said / omitted?

MATERIALITY
＝would it matter under applicable legal test?

TIMING
＝when known? when due? when released?

MENTAL STATE
＝strict? negligence? recklessness? scienter? depends on cause of action

TRANSACTION / RELIANCE
＝required for this remedy?

CAUSATION / LOSS
＝what loss is legally connected?

REMEDY
＝filing correction? sanction? damages? rescission? criminal case?
```

## G2-83.19 Evidence architecture

- issuer status / listing status；
- filing calendar；
- 10-K / 10-Q / 8-K / 20-F / 6-K or local equivalents；
- annual / half-year reports；
- inside-information notices；
- board / audit committee minutes；
- disclosure committee records；
- internal emails / drafts；
- accounting workpapers；
- audit communications；
- investor presentations；
- earnings calls；
- analyst communications；
- press releases；
- regulator comments；
- restatements；
- trading / price / volume data；
- corrective disclosures；
- loss model；
- class-period timeline。

核心：

> **證券揭露案的事實不是『公司後來出事』，而是『在每一個具體時間點，誰知道什麼、依法應說什麼、實際說了什麼、投資市場如何接收到它』。**

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-144"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "內線交易／市場濫用與操縱法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "C"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FLOW_CAPITAL_ALLOCATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FLOW_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-84｜DLM-144｜內線交易／市場濫用與操縱法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B48"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、D:組織／企業／市場"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-144-INSIDER_TRADING_MARKET_ABUSE_MANIPULATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "內線交易／市場濫用與操縱法"
  english_name: "Insider Trading / Market Abuse & Manipulation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某人取得重大未公開／inside information後的交易、建議、洩漏，或某些交易、下單、資訊散布與基準行為，何時從合法資訊優勢、造市、交易策略或市場表達，跨入insider dealing、unlawful disclosure、market manipulation或其他market-abuse禁止？"

  historical_reason:
    "二級市場價格需要相信成交與資訊不是被少數人用秘密資訊或虛假訊號任意重寫。市場濫用法因此同時處理information asymmetry與false price formation；但合法研究、資訊分析、大額交易、造市、回購、穩定措施、企業併購與market sounding也可能造成資訊與價格不對稱。若只以『知道消息』『賺很多』『價格大動』判違法，就會把市場運作本身犯罪化，因此必須以資訊性質、取得關係、行為、意圖／認知、法定safe harbour與市場效果分層。"

  first_move:
    "先分information-based abuse與conduct-based manipulation；再確認instrument / venue / jurisdiction、inside-information或MNPI資格、行為人資訊來源、實際trade/order/disclosure與適用主觀要件。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "informational advantage vs unfair use of confidential information"
    - "market liquidity vs manipulation prevention"
    - "legitimate trading strategy vs artificial price formation"
    - "confidential deal preparation vs market disclosure"
    - "insider status vs information-based liability"
    - "algorithmic trading vs spoofing / layering"
    - "market soundings vs unlawful tipping"
    - "buybacks / stabilisation vs artificial support"
    - "social-media speech vs pump-and-dump"
    - "administrative market integrity vs criminal punishment"

  shortest_maxim:
    "市場濫用第一眼不是『誰先知道、誰賺錢』；先問『什麼資訊、從哪裡來、法律上是否inside/MNPI、誰做了什麼trade/order/disclosure、又是哪一條禁止真的被觸發』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-84.1 第一眼｜INSTRUMENT / VENUE → INFORMATION OR CONDUCT → PERSON / SOURCE → KNOWLEDGE / DUTY → TRADE / ORDER / DISCLOSURE → LEGITIMATE BEHAVIOUR / SAFE HARBOUR → MARKET EFFECT → ENFORCEMENT

```text
INSTRUMENT / VENUE
＝regulated market? MTF? OTF? OTC-linked instrument? security? derivative?

INFORMATION OR CONDUCT
＝inside info? MNPI? false order? rumour? benchmark input?

PERSON / SOURCE
＝director? employee? adviser? shareholder? tippee? outsider? hacker?

KNOWLEDGE / DUTY
＝what did they know and why?

TRADE / ORDER / DISCLOSURE
＝buy? sell? cancel? amend? recommend? tip? place/cancel orders?

LEGITIMATE BEHAVIOUR
＝market maker? prior obligation? takeover use? wall-crossing? buyback?

MARKET EFFECT
＝false signal? artificial price? demand distortion? actual effect required?

ENFORCEMENT
＝administrative? civil? criminal? exchange discipline?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-84.20 Evidence architecture

- instrument / venue status；
- inside-information content；
- information creation timeline；
- confidentiality / access logs；
- insider lists；
- emails / chats / calls；
- board materials；
- deal room access；
- broker records；
- account ownership / beneficial ownership；
- order-level data；
- cancellations / modifications；
- trade executions；
- market-depth / price / volume data；
- linked accounts；
- tipper / tippee communications；
- 10b5-1 plans；
- market-sounding records；
- buyback / stabilisation records；
- suspicious transaction / order reports；
- public disclosures；
- subsequent publication time。

核心：

> **市場濫用不是靠『他一定知道』完成；要把資訊生成、取得、傳遞、交易／下單與市場時間軸接成可追溯鏈。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## G2-84.22 Positive Law Gate

必查：

1. covered instrument；
2. venue / territorial scope；
3. inside information / MNPI definition；
4. materiality / precision；
5. public / nonpublic status；
6. person / insider category；
7. duty / source of information；
8. possession vs use standard；
9. tipper / tippee rule；
10. transaction / cancellation / amendment；
11. recommendation / inducement；
12. unlawful disclosure；
13. market manipulation definition；
14. false / misleading signals；
15. artificial price；
16. intent / knowledge / recklessness；
17. legitimate behaviour；
18. 10b5-1 / equivalent defence；
19. market sounding；
20. buyback / stabilisation safe harbour；
21. STOR / surveillance duty；
22. administrative / civil / criminal consequences；
23. attempted conduct；
24. limitation / jurisdiction。

```yaml
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B49"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-145-INVESTMENT_FUNDS_COLLECTIVE_INVESTMENT_SCHEMES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "投資基金／集合投資計畫法"
  english_name: "Investment Funds / Collective Investment Schemes Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個把多數投資人的金錢或資產集中起來、依共同投資政策運作並由專業管理人管理的安排，是否構成受監理的fund／collective investment scheme；其vehicle、manager、depositary／custodian、valuation、liquidity、redemption、portfolio limits、leverage、disclosure、marketing與investor protection資格如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "集合投資讓個別投資人以較低交易成本取得分散投資與專業管理，但也把資產控制、估值、流動性、費用、利益衝突與資訊掌握集中到管理人及服務提供者。投資人持有的是基金份額，不是直接控制基金每一項資產；當大量投資人同時申購或贖回時，個別契約自由也會轉化為共同池公平與系統流動性問題。因此基金法的核心不是『這檔基金會不會賺』，而是共同投資結構如何取得合法成立、運作、估值、退出與分配風險的資格。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識vehicle、pooling、investment policy、investor class、open/closed-ended、redemption、manager與custody structure；不要只因名稱叫fund、ETF、trust、partnership或private fund就直接套同一套規則。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "pooling efficiency vs investor control"
    - "professional management vs agency conflict"
    - "daily liquidity vs illiquid assets"
    - "valuation discretion vs equal treatment"
    - "open-ended redemption vs first-mover advantage"
    - "retail access vs product complexity"
    - "portfolio freedom vs diversification / concentration limits"
    - "leverage / derivatives vs risk amplification"
    - "manager delegation vs responsibility retention"
    - "fund-level disclosure vs investor information overload"
    - "cross-border marketing vs local investor protection"
    - "innovation in fund structures vs regulatory perimeter"

  shortest_maxim:
    "基金法第一眼不是『很多人的錢交給專家投資』；先問『誰的錢被如何pool、什麼vehicle、誰管理、誰保管、投資人如何進出、NAV怎麼算、流動性與利益衝突如何被治理』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-93.1 第一眼｜VEHICLE → POOLING → INVESTOR CLASS → OPEN / CLOSED → MANAGER → DEPOSITARY / CUSTODY → PORTFOLIO RULES → VALUATION → LIQUIDITY → REDEMPTION → DISCLOSURE → DISTRIBUTION

```text
VEHICLE
＝corporation? trust? contractual fund? partnership? unit trust?

POOLING
＝multiple investors? common policy? passive holding company? club deal?

INVESTOR CLASS
＝retail? professional? qualified / accredited? institutional?

OPEN / CLOSED
＝continuous redemption? exchange trading? fixed capital? interval structure?

MANAGER
＝internal? external management company? AIFM? adviser?

DEPOSITARY / CUSTODY
＝who holds / safeguards assets? cash monitoring? ownership verification?

PORTFOLIO RULES
＝eligible assets? diversification? concentration? borrowing? derivatives?

VALUATION
＝NAV? fair value? frequency? illiquid assets? pricing error?

LIQUIDITY
＝asset liquidity vs redemption terms? stress? LMTs?

REDEMPTION
＝daily? periodic? gate? suspension? side pocket?

DISCLOSURE
＝prospectus? KID? annual / semiannual reports? risk / fee disclosure?

DISTRIBUTION
＝who may market? to whom? in which jurisdiction?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-93.29 Evidence architecture

- constitutional / trust / partnership / fund documents；
- prospectus / offering memorandum；
- investor eligibility records；
- manager / AIFM / adviser authorisation；
- depositary / custody agreement；
- valuation policy / NAV records；
- pricing sources / models；
- liquidity profile / stress testing；
- redemption requests / gates / suspension decisions；
- LMT policy；
- portfolio holdings / concentration；
- leverage calculations；
- loan-origination records；
- fee / expense schedules；
- conflicts / affiliated transactions；
- marketing materials；
- regulatory filings；
- board / committee minutes；
- current national transposition law。

核心：

> **基金案不能只從『投資人虧錢』開始閉合。先證明這是什麼pool、哪一套fund regime、誰控制資產、投資人何時有何退出權、NAV與流動性承諾如何成立。**

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-146-ASSET_MANAGEMENT_INVESTMENT_ADVISERS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "資產管理／投資顧問法"
  english_name: "Asset Management / Investment Advisers Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個人或機構何時因有償提供investment advice、discretionary portfolio management、fund management或其他資產管理服務而取得adviser／manager法位；其registration／authorisation、mandate、fiduciary／best-interest duties、care、loyalty、conflicts、fees、allocation、custody、delegation、marketing與recordkeeping義務如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "資產管理最特殊的法律風險，不是交易本身，而是客戶把決策權、資訊優勢與資產配置判斷交給另一個人。管理人可能同時選產品、分配交易、選broker、收費、推薦關係企業商品、決定估值來源或持有客戶資產。這些角色會把資訊不對稱變成持續的代理衝突。因此資產管理法的第一問題不是『建議最後賺不賺』，而是誰取得替別人判斷與配置資產的資格，以及其利益衝突如何在可追溯責任下被限制。"

  first_move:
    "先分類service：general information、personal recommendation、investment advice、discretionary portfolio management、fund management、execution-only、brokerage；再看client、compensation、discretion、custody與jurisdiction。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "professional expertise vs client dependency"
    - "manager discretion vs client mandate"
    - "fiduciary loyalty vs commercial compensation"
    - "conflict disclosure vs conflict elimination"
    - "product shelf economics vs client best interest"
    - "delegation / outsourcing vs retained responsibility"
    - "performance incentives vs excessive risk"
    - "best execution vs affiliated routing"
    - "scalability / automation vs individualized duty"
    - "marketing performance vs fair presentation"
    - "client sophistication vs non-waivable duties"
    - "global management vs local licensing"

  shortest_maxim:
    "資產管理法第一眼不是『建議對不對』；先問『誰以什麼法位替誰作或影響投資決策、拿什麼報酬、握有多少裁量與資產控制、利益衝突怎麼被治理』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-94.1 第一眼｜SERVICE → ADVISER / MANAGER STATUS → CLIENT → MANDATE → DISCRETION → COMPENSATION → DUTY → CONFLICT → CUSTODY → DELEGATION → EXECUTION → DISCLOSURE → ENFORCEMENT

```text
SERVICE
＝general research? advice? personal recommendation? portfolio management? fund management?

STATUS
＝registered investment adviser? exempt adviser? management company? AIFM? investment firm?

CLIENT
＝retail? institutional? fund? pension? family office?

MANDATE
＝advisory only? discretionary? model portfolio? sub-advisory?

DISCRETION
＝who decides buy/sell/allocation?

COMPENSATION
＝AUM fee? fixed fee? performance fee? commission? revenue share?

DUTY
＝care? loyalty? fiduciary? best interest? suitability?

CONFLICT
＝affiliate product? fee differential? principal trade? allocation? gifts?

CUSTODY
＝who can access / move assets?

DELEGATION
＝sub-adviser? model provider? cloud / AI? administrator?

EXECUTION
＝who selects broker / venue? best execution?

DISCLOSURE
＝Form ADV? client agreement? conflicts? performance?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-94.28 Evidence architecture

```text
SERVICE CLASSIFICATION
→ WHO DID WHAT FOR WHOM?

REGULATORY STATUS
→ authorised / registered / exempt?

CLIENT MANDATE
→ what authority was actually granted?

DUTY
→ care / loyalty / suitability / best interest / contract?

CONFLICT
→ what economic incentive existed?

DISCLOSURE / CONSENT
→ what was told, when, and was it comprehensible?

CONDUCT
→ recommendation / trade / allocation / fee / custody / delegation?

HARM / REMEDY
→ causation / loss / disgorgement / rescission / regulatory sanction?
```

核心：

> **資產管理案不能只問『這筆投資是不是差』。要把服務法位、客戶授權、衝突、實際決策與損害鏈逐段拆開。**

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-147-BROKER_DEALER_SECURITIES_INTERMEDIARY_CONDUCT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "證券商／投資中介行為法"
  english_name: "Broker-Dealer / Securities Intermediary Conduct Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個人或機構何時因為effecting transactions、dealing for own account、receiving / transmitting orders、distribution、recommendation或其他investment services而取得broker-dealer／investment firm／market intermediary法位；其registration、client classification、recommendation standard、suitability / appropriateness、best execution、conflicts、product governance、communications、client asset protection與supervision義務如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "證券市場不是發行人與投資人直接相遇的抽象場所。大量交易透過broker、dealer、investment firm、registered representative、tied agent、platform與其他中介發生。中介同時握有商品知識、訂單資訊、分銷報酬、路由權、客戶分類與交易執行能力，因此『幫客戶成交』本身會產生資訊、利益與執行品質問題。這個領域要治理的不是所有市場價格，而是中介如何在客戶與市場之間取得合法角色並履行相應行為義務。"

  first_move:
    "先辨角色與service：broker? dealer? investment firm? reception/transmission? execution? dealing on own account? advice? underwriting? platform?；再判client category、recommendation是否存在、產品／交易類型與jurisdiction。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "sales function vs client protection"
    - "commission / spread revenue vs recommendation quality"
    - "execution speed vs best execution"
    - "proprietary inventory vs client interest"
    - "product distribution vs product governance"
    - "retail accessibility vs complexity"
    - "standardisation vs client-specific suitability"
    - "digital self-service vs recommendation design"
    - "client classification vs protection waiver"
    - "cross-border access vs licensing"
    - "SRO supervision vs statutory responsibility"
    - "market liquidity provision vs conflict / manipulation risk"

  shortest_maxim:
    "投資中介法第一眼不是『客戶自己按了買進』；先問『誰以什麼角色讓這筆交易發生、是否作了recommendation、客戶屬哪一類、商品與路由如何被選、利益衝突與執行品質如何被治理』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-95.1 第一眼｜ACTIVITY → STATUS → CLIENT → COMMUNICATION → RECOMMENDATION → PRODUCT → SUITABILITY / APPROPRIATENESS → CONFLICT → EXECUTION → CLIENT ASSETS → RECORDS / SUPERVISION

```text
ACTIVITY
＝solicitation? receive/transmit? execute? deal own account? underwrite? distribute?

STATUS
＝broker? dealer? investment firm? tied agent? introducing broker?

CLIENT
＝retail? professional? eligible counterparty? institutional?

COMMUNICATION
＝advertising? general information? recommendation? personal recommendation?

RECOMMENDATION
＝was action suggested / called for?

PRODUCT
＝security? fund? derivative? structured deposit? crypto financial instrument?

SUITABILITY / APPROPRIATENESS
＝which test applies?

CONFLICT
＝commission? proprietary product? payment / routing? inventory?

EXECUTION
＝price? cost? speed? likelihood? venue?

CLIENT ASSETS
＝custody / segregation / safeguarding?

SUPERVISION
＝who supervised representative / system / branch?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-95.29 Evidence architecture

- registration / licence / SRO membership；
- client classification；
- account agreement；
- Form CRS / disclosures；
- representative communications；
- recommendation logs；
- product shelf / target-market records；
- cost / fee comparison；
- conflict inventory；
- compensation plan；
- order tickets；
- execution venue / routing data；
- best-execution review；
- client instructions；
- custody / segregation records；
- supervision / branch review；
- complaints / disciplinary history；
- current MiFID / SEC / SRO rules。

核心：

> **中介案不能只問『客戶是不是自己買的』。要把招攬、推薦、分類、成本、衝突、執行與監督逐段還原。**

```yaml
  - "FINRA不是statute＝可以忽略。"
```

# G2-96｜第四十九批交界辨識器

```yaml
BATCH_49_INTERSECTION_ROUTER:

  DLM_008_SECURITIES_PARENT:
    trigger:
      - "instrument / issuer / market / information general classification"
    route_to:
      - "DLM-142 public offering"
      - "DLM-143 issuer disclosure / fraud"
      - "DLM-144 market abuse"
      - "DLM-145 funds"
      - "DLM-146 advisers / asset management"
      - "DLM-147 brokers / intermediaries"

  DLM_145_FUNDS:
    trigger:
      - "pooled vehicle"
      - "fund units / shares"
      - "NAV / valuation"
      - "redemption / liquidity"
      - "UCITS / AIF"
      - "depository / custody"
      - "fund-level leverage / portfolio limits"

  DLM_146_ASSET_MANAGEMENT:
    trigger:
      - "investment advice"
      - "discretionary mandate"
      - "portfolio management"
      - "fiduciary / care / loyalty"
      - "adviser conflicts / fees / allocation"
      - "sub-adviser / delegation"

  DLM_147_INTERMEDIARY:
    trigger:
      - "broker / dealer / investment firm"
      - "recommendation"
      - "order reception / transmission / execution"
      - "suitability / appropriateness"
      - "best execution"
      - "distribution conflict"

  DLM_142_PUBLIC_OFFERING:
    relation:
      "fund interests may themselves be securities offered to investors; fund regulation does not replace offering law."

  DLM_143_DISCLOSURE_FRAUD:
    relation:
      "fund / adviser / broker disclosure may create separate anti-fraud or issuer-liability questions."

  DLM_144_MARKET_ABUSE:
    relation:
      "intermediary trading conduct may also become insider dealing / manipulation; client conduct and market integrity remain separate axes."

  DLM_011_FINANCIAL_REGULATION:
    relation:
      "prudential capital, systemic risk, authorisation perimeter and supervisory architecture remain parent financial-regulation questions."

  DLM_070_ACCOUNTING:
    relation:
      "fund valuation / financial reporting may require accounting analysis."

  DLM_071_AUDIT:
    relation:
      "fund financial statements / assurance / auditor independence remain separate."

  DLM_103_BANKING:
    relation:
      "bank acting as custodian / distributor does not convert all activities into banking law; role-by-role routing required."

  DLM_104_PAYMENTS:
    relation:
      "cash transfer / settlement rails do not determine fund or brokerage legality."

  DLM_105_CRYPTO_ASSET:
    relation:
      "crypto fund / tokenised fund / crypto intermediary first requires instrument and regulatory-perimeter classification."
```

核心：

```text
POOL
→ DLM-145

MANDATE / ADVICE
→ DLM-146

TRANSACTION INTERMEDIATION / RECOMMENDATION
→ DLM-147
```

不要用單一「投資服務」把三種責任鏈吞掉。

# DLM-012｜保險法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-012"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "保險法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "C"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FLOW_RISK_TRANSFER"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "MOTHER"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_NARROWED"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 18｜DLM-012｜保險法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B04"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留保險共同概念與總路由；157–162承擔契約、審慎、銷售、危機、再保險與保戶保障。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-012-INSURANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保險法"
  english_name: "Insurance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "不確定的個別風險如何透過保險契約被移入一個風險池；保險人如何承諾在未來特定事故發生時給付，而法律又如何處理資訊不對稱、道德風險、承保範圍、理賠與保險人長期償付能力？"

  historical_reason:
    "個別災害可能對一個人或企業造成巨大損失，但若大量相似風險被集合、定價與分散，就能轉化為較可管理的共同風險。保險因此同時是一種私人契約與風險池制度；保險人現在收取保費、未來承擔不確定給付，所以法律不只處理契約解釋，也必須監管其資本、準備金、投資、治理與理賠能力。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "風險轉移 vs 道德風險"
    - "保險人定價資訊需求 vs 被保險人資訊負擔"
    - "契約文字確定性 vs 消費者合理期待"
    - "承保範圍 vs 除外責任"
    - "個別公平 vs 風險池精算公平"
    - "快速理賠 vs 詐欺防制"
    - "保費競爭 vs 長期償付能力"
    - "保險人投資收益 vs 保戶資產安全"
    - "個別公司治理 vs 系統性與集團風險"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保險法第一眼不是『有損失就賠』；先問『保的是什麼風險、誰的利益、哪個期間、哪個事故、哪個因果、哪個除外』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 18.1 第一眼｜RISK → INTEREST → COVERAGE → EVENT → CAUSATION → EXCLUSION

保險案件先跑：

```text
RISK
＝承保的是什麼風險？

INSURED INTEREST / PERSON / SUBJECT
＝誰／什麼利益被保？

COVERAGE PERIOD
＝事故是否落在承保期間？

INSURED EVENT / LOSS
＝實際發生了什麼？

CAUSATION
＝損失是否由承保風險造成？

EXCLUSION / CONDITION
＝是否有除外、免責、條件、通知、合作義務？
```

只有：

> 「發生損失」

完全不足以完成理賠責任。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 18.11 證據特徵

保險案件的重要材料：

- 要保書；
- 健康／風險問卷；
- 保單；
- 批單；
- 條款版本；
- 保費紀錄；
- 核保資料；
- 業務員說明／錄音；
- 保險事故證明；
- 醫療紀錄；
- 警察／消防／海事報告；
- 修復／估價；
- 鑑定；
- 理賠申請；
- 拒賠理由；
- 理賠調查；
- 再保險；
- 代位資料；
- 保險人財務與準備金資料（監理案件）。

核心：

> **保單文字只是起點；真正爭議常在投保時資訊、事故時事實與拒賠時理由三個時間點。**

---

## 18.13 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依險種與法域現查：

1. 保險契約成立與形式；
2. 告知／陳述義務；
3. 保險利益；
4. 承保範圍；
5. 除外與免責；
6. 因果與近因；
7. 保險事故通知；
8. 理賠時效；
9. 人身／財產／責任／海上／健康等不同險種；
10. 代位；
11. 重複保險；
12. 再保險；
13. 保險消費者保護；
14. 保險人償付能力、資本與準備金；
15. IAIS ICP／ComFrame 之本國實施；
16. 保險公司危機處理與清算。

# DLM-157｜保險契約／承保理賠法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-157"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "保險契約／承保理賠法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "C"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FLOW_RISK_TRANSFER"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FLOW_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-133｜DLM-157｜保險契約／承保理賠法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B53"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-157-INSURANCE_CONTRACT_COVERAGE_CLAIMS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保險契約／承保理賠法"
  english_name: "Insurance Contract / Coverage & Claims Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個insurance contract是否有效成立、誰是policyholder／insured／beneficiary、哪一個risk與period被承保、事故是否落入insuring clause、exclusion／condition／warranty是否有效且可被援用、pre-contract disclosure或misrepresentation造成什麼效果、claim需要證明哪些事實、fraud／subrogation／contribution／limits如何運作，以及哪一法域的強制保護或choice-of-law規則限制契約自由？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險契約並不是普通商品買賣的延伸。締約時保險人無法完整觀察風險，被保險人也通常無法完整預測未來事故；事故發生後，雙方又會圍繞policy wording、causation、exclusion、notice、proof of loss與claim conduct重新配置資訊優勢。早期保險法因此高度發展 disclosure、utmost good faith、warranty與indemnity doctrine；現代法域則大量以consumer protection、proportionate remedies、claims handling與強制條款修正傳統嚴格規則。這使保險契約成為一個『先界定風險，再判事故，再判排除與救濟』的獨立法律世界。"

  first_move:
    "先取得完整policy package與適用法，不從『事故真的發生了』或『保險公司拒賠了』直接閉合。先辨識policyholder／insured／beneficiary、policy period、insured peril、insuring clause、definitions、exclusions、conditions、limits／deductibles、endorsements、pre-contract information、claim evidence與mandatory law，再判coverage。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "risk pooling vs individual claim entitlement"
    - "freedom of contract vs mandatory policyholder protection"
    - "information asymmetry vs insurer underwriting responsibility"
    - "policy wording certainty vs reasonable expectations / consumer readability"
    - "coverage grant vs exclusion"
    - "fortuity vs moral hazard"
    - "indemnity vs agreed / valued benefits"
    - "prompt claims payment vs fraud prevention"
    - "notice / cooperation duties vs disproportionate forfeiture"
    - "causal precision vs multi-causal losses"
    - "subrogation recovery vs insured full compensation"
    - "commercial sophistication vs consumer vulnerability"
    - "national contract law vs cross-border insurance market"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保險事故發生只證明事故；理賠資格要再走『哪份保單、誰被保、什麼風險、哪段期間、哪個承保條款、哪個排除／條件、哪套強制法、證據證到哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-133.1 第一眼｜POLICY → PARTY → LAW → PERIOD → RISK → EVENT → COVERAGE → EXCLUSION → CONDITION → CAUSATION → LOSS → CLAIM → REMEDY

```text
POLICY
＝policy wording? schedule? endorsement? rider? certificate?

PARTY
＝policyholder? insured? additional insured? beneficiary? third party?

LAW
＝which jurisdiction? mandatory insurance rule? choice-of-law valid?

PERIOD
＝occurrence? claims-made? retroactive date? reporting period?

RISK
＝what peril / liability / person / property is insured?

EVENT
＝what physically / legally happened?

COVERAGE
＝does the insuring clause attach?

EXCLUSION
＝does an exclusion remove otherwise granted coverage?

CONDITION
＝notice? cooperation? warranty? premium? mitigation?

CAUSATION
＝which cause legally connects event and insured loss?

LOSS
＝indemnifiable amount? limit? deductible? aggregation?

CLAIM
＝proof / timing / fraud / handling / reservation?

REMEDY
＝payment? declaration? damages? rescission / avoidance? complaint / ADR?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-133.38 Evidence architecture

- complete policy wording；
- schedule / endorsements / riders；
- application / questionnaire；
- underwriting file；
- premium / renewal record；
- insured / beneficiary status；
- loss date / notice date；
- incident report；
- photos / video / telemetry；
- police / fire / medical records where lawful；
- expert report；
- repair / replacement / income-loss records；
- reservation of rights；
- denial letter；
- claims notes；
- fraud evidence；
- third-party recovery materials；
- applicable statute / mandatory law；
- limitation dates。

核心：

> **保險理賠不是『有事故／沒事故』二分。事故先取得事件資格，接著才進保單、期間、風險、排除、條件、因果、金額與程序。**

## G2-133.41 Positive Law Gate

個案前必查：

```text
JURISDICTION
INSURANCE CLASS
CONSUMER / COMMERCIAL STATUS
APPLICABLE LAW
MANDATORY INSURANCE STATUTE
POLICY VERSION
COVERAGE TRIGGER
EXCLUSIONS / CONDITIONS
CLAIM DEADLINES
BURDEN OF PROOF
REMEDIES
```

本卡不得輸出：

```text
"所有保險法都要求……"
"國際標準規定保險人一定要賠……"
```

除非有可識別法源與scope。

```yaml
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FLOW_SPECIALIZATION"

  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-135｜DLM-159｜保險銷售／中介行為法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B53"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係、G:資訊／知識／數位"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-159-INSURANCE_DISTRIBUTION_INTERMEDIARY_CONDUCT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保險銷售／中介行為法"
  english_name: "Insurance Distribution / Intermediary Conduct Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "保險商品由insurer direct sales、agent、broker、bank、platform或ancillary intermediary向客戶分銷時，誰需要registration／authorisation、誰代表誰、是否完成demands-and-needs／suitability／appropriateness、資訊與費用揭露、product governance、conflict與remuneration要求；cross-selling、digital distribution、insurance-based investment products與complaint／redress又如何受到特別規範？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險商品在事故發生前價值難以被一般消費者直接驗證，coverage language複雜，commission與distribution chain又容易讓銷售者的收益與客戶利益分離。傳統只問『保單條款是否有效』不足以治理mis-selling、inappropriate advice、opaque remuneration與cross-selling。現代保險分銷法因此把中介資格、客戶需求、advice、資訊揭露、product oversight、conflicts、remuneration、professional competence與數位銷售拆成獨立conduct regime。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識distributor身分與distribution activity：insurer employee、agent、broker、bancassurance、ancillary intermediary、platform或IBIP adviser？再固定customer type、product class、advised／non-advised sale、remuneration、demands-and-needs資料、product target market、disclosure與cross-border passport；不要因保單有效就推定銷售過程合法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "access to insurance vs sales quality"
    - "commission-based distribution vs customer best interest"
    - "advice personalization vs scalable digital sales"
    - "product complexity vs understandable disclosure"
    - "insurer manufacturing vs intermediary distribution responsibility"
    - "customer choice vs demands-and-needs gate"
    - "cross-selling convenience vs tying / poor value"
    - "professional judgment vs algorithmic recommendation"
    - "competition among distributors vs licensing / competence"
    - "national options vs EU minimum harmonisation"
    - "insurance-based investment products vs ordinary protection products"
    - "political agreement on reform vs current applicable law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保險分銷第一眼不是『客戶簽了沒有』；先問『誰在賣、代表誰、賣的是什麼產品、客戶需要什麼、是否提供建議、佣酬與衝突在哪裡、資訊何時怎麼給、商品是否適合目標市場、哪一套conduct rule今天真的適用』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-135.1 第一眼｜ACTOR → ACTIVITY → LICENCE → CUSTOMER → PRODUCT → DEMANDS & NEEDS → ADVICE → DISCLOSURE → REMUNERATION → PRODUCT GOVERNANCE → CROSS-SELLING → DIGITAL → REDRESS

```text
ACTOR
＝insurer? employee? agent? broker? bank? ancillary intermediary? platform?

ACTIVITY
＝introducing? proposing? advising? concluding? assisting in administration / claims?

LICENCE
＝registration / authorisation / passport?

CUSTOMER
＝consumer? professional? vulnerable customer?

PRODUCT
＝non-life? life? protection? IBIP? credit protection?

DEMANDS & NEEDS
＝what insurance need was actually identified?

ADVICE
＝personal recommendation? suitability? appropriateness?

DISCLOSURE
＝product / cost / conflict / capacity / IPID / KID?

REMUNERATION
＝commission? fee? incentive? target?

PRODUCT GOVERNANCE
＝manufacturer? target market? distributor strategy? value?

CROSS-SELLING
＝insurance tied / bundled with credit or other product?

DIGITAL
＝online flow? AI recommendation? dark pattern? influencer?

REDRESS
＝complaint? ombuds? regulator? civil claim?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-135.29 Evidence architecture

- distributor registration / licence；
- agency / broker agreement；
- customer classification；
- demands-and-needs questionnaire；
- advice / suitability record；
- product target market；
- IPID / KID / policy summary；
- timing and delivery proof；
- remuneration / commission record；
- conflicts register；
- scripts / sales calls；
- online journey / screenshots；
- AI recommendation logs where lawfully available；
- cross-selling documents；
- product governance review；
- complaint file；
- insurer / intermediary communications；
- national IDD implementation law；
- applicable future-reform status。

核心：

> **保險銷售合規不是『有一份簽名表單』。要證明誰在什麼資格下，對哪一類客戶，用哪一種程序，把哪個商品放到哪個需求與風險位置。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## G2-135.32 Positive Law Gate

個案前必查：

```text
JURISDICTION
DISTRIBUTOR TYPE
REGISTRATION / AUTHORISATION
CUSTOMER TYPE
PRODUCT TYPE
ADVISED / NON-ADVISED
IBIP STATUS
DEMANDS-AND-NEEDS REQUIREMENT
SUITABILITY / APPROPRIATENESS
DISCLOSURE DOCUMENTS
REMUNERATION / CONFLICT RULE
PRODUCT GOVERNANCE
CROSS-BORDER STATUS
CURRENT REFORM / TRANSITION DATE
```

不得把：

```text
SUPERVISORY REPORT
POLITICAL AGREEMENT
DRAFT / FUTURE AMENDMENT
```

升格成今日適用法。

# G2-136｜第五十三批交界辨識器

```yaml
INTERSECTION_ROUTER_BATCH_53:

  DLM_012_INSURANCE_MOTHER_ROUTER:
    rule:
      "DLM-012保留整體保險法第一眼；DLM-157..159是specialization refinement，不回寫封存Volume I。"

  CONTRACT_VS_DISTRIBUTION:
    if:
      - "policy wording / exclusion / claim / insured event"
    route:
      - "DLM-157"
    if_sale_process:
      - "demands-and-needs / advice / commission / mis-selling"
    route_sale:
      - "DLM-159"
    lock:
      "VALID POLICY != COMPLIANT SALE；MIS-SELLING != CLAIM COVERAGE AUTOMATICALLY。"

  CONTRACT_VS_SOLVENCY:
    contract:
      - "does insurer owe this claim?"
    prudential:
      - "can insurer as undertaking support all obligations?"
    lock:
      "CLAIM LIABILITY != SOLVENCY STATUS；SOLVENCY STATUS != INDIVIDUAL CLAIM ENTITLEMENT。"

  PRUDENTIAL_VS_RESOLUTION:
    going_concern:
      - "DLM-158"
    failure_resolution:
      - "future insurance recovery / resolution specialization candidate"
    lock:
      "SCR BREACH != RESOLUTION AUTOMATICALLY；IRRD EXISTS != APPLICABLE 2026-08-25。"

  INSURANCE_VS_BANKING:
    bank:
      - "DLM-103 / 154 / 156"
    insurance:
      - "DLM-157 / 158 / 159"
    lock:
      "FINANCIAL INSTITUTION != SAME PRUDENTIAL / CONDUCT FORMULA。"

  IBIP_INTERFACE:
    insurance_contract:
      - "DLM-157"
    insurance_distribution:
      - "DLM-159"
    securities_or_asset_management_if_separately_triggered:
      - "DLM-145 / 146 / 147"
    lock:
      "INSURANCE WRAPPER DOES NOT ERASE INVESTMENT-CONDUCT ISSUES；INVESTMENT COMPONENT DOES NOT ERASE INSURANCE LAW。"

  CROSS_BORDER_CONTRACT:
    route:
      - "DLM-004"
      - "DLM-157"
    note:
      "Rome I Article 7 has insurance-specific choice-of-law structure and excludes reinsurance."

  AI_INSURANCE:
    route:
      - "DLM-097"
      - "DLM-133"
      - "DLM-157 or 159 depending underwriting/claim vs sale"
    lock:
      "AI SCORE != CLAIM FRAUD / CUSTOMER NEED / INSURED FACT。"
```

# G2-137｜第五十三批綜合案例

```text
A銀行集團同時持有一家life insurer。

銀行理專向60歲客戶推介一張unit-linked life policy：
→ 稱為「保本、像定存但收益更高」
→ 銷售紀錄只有勾選式需求表
→ 高額commission未在主要畫面清楚呈現
→ 客戶其後因市場下跌申請解約並主張被誤導

同時：
→ insurer因利率與大量surrender壓力，solvency ratio快速下降
→ group內部準備移轉資產支援insurer
→ 公司引用2026年EIOPA新guidelines說自己已按「新Solvency II」合規
→ 主管機關則要求補做capital / liquidity remediation

數月後客戶死亡：
→ beneficiary另提出death benefit claim
→ insurer主張投保時健康問卷有不實陳述
→ beneficiary稱理專當時說「這些不用寫那麼細」
```

理火拆法：

```text
SALE REPRESENTATION / NEEDS / COMMISSION / IBIP
→ DLM-159

POLICY VALIDITY / HEALTH MISREPRESENTATION / DEATH BENEFIT / EXCLUSION
→ DLM-157

INSURER CAPITAL / LIQUIDITY / GROUP SUPPORT / 2026 CURRENT SOLVENCY RULE
→ DLM-158

BANK EMPLOYEE / AGENCY CAPACITY
→ DLM-100 + DLM-156 where bank conduct independently triggered

DATA / AI if recommendation engine used
→ DLM-097 / 133
```

不能：

```text
MIS-SELLING PROVED
→ DEATH CLAIM AUTOMATICALLY PAYABLE
```

也不能：

```text
INSURER SOLVENCY RATIO LOW
→ POLICY CLAIM AUTOMATICALLY INVALID
```

更不能：

```text
EIOPA JULY 2026 REVISED GUIDELINES PUBLISHED
→ 2027 SOLVENCY II REVIEW RULES ALREADY GOVERN AUGUST 2026
```

這個案例的價值在於：

> **同一張保單，可以同時生成「契約資格、銷售資格、保險人審慎資格」三條法律鏈；任何一條成立，都沒有權替其他兩條提前閉合。**

```yaml
REGRESSION_BATCH_53:

  T157_1_ACCIDENT_COVERAGE:
    input: "事故真的發生了，所以保險公司一定要賠。"

    input: "保單裡有戰爭排除，所以所有與戰爭有關的損失都不賠。"

    pass: "查policy trigger、prejudice rule、claims-made structure與mandatory law。"

  T157_4_MISSTATEMENT_VOID:
    input: "投保資料有一處錯，所以保單自始無效。"
    pass: "區分consumer/commercial、materiality、state of mind、statutory remedy；不得全球化avoidance。"

  T157_5_FRAUD_SCORE:

    pass: "model score != fact / fraud；要求獨立證據與legal standard。"

  T157_6_TWO_POLICIES:
    input: "有兩張火險，所以房子燒掉可以拿兩倍。"

  T157_7_REINSURANCE_SAME:
    input: "再保險就是保戶多買一張保單。"

  T157_8_BAD_FAITH_GLOBAL:

  T158_1_SOLVENCY_LIQUIDITY:

  T158_2_ICS_WORLD_LAW:

  T158_3_IAIG_BIG:

    pass: "large != IAIG; verify identification / group-wide supervisor / scope。"

  T158_4_SOLVENCY_REVIEW_TODAY:
    input: "Solvency II review已立法，所以2026-08-25直接按新框架。"
    pass: "amended framework applies 2027-01-30；current 2026 rules remain relevant。"

  T158_5_EIOPA_GUIDELINE_TODAY:
    input: "EIOPA在2026-07-15已發布final guidelines，所以8月已生效。"
    pass: "publication != application；revised guidelines state 2027-01-30 application, prior guidelines until then。"

  T158_6_RISK_MARGIN_475:
    input: "2026現在Solvency II risk margin Cost-of-Capital rate就是4.75%。"
    pass: "Directive 2025/2 sets 4.75% from 2027-01-30；不得倒灌2026。"

  T158_7_REINSURANCE_RISK_GONE:
    input: "風險80%再保，所以只剩20%風險。"
    pass: "檢查attachment / exclusions / basis / counterparty / collateral / recoverability。"

  T158_8_GROUP_SAFE:
    input: "group solvency合格，所以每一子公司都安全。"

  T158_10_IRRD_SOLVENCY:

  T159_1_SIGNATURE:
```

### SOURCE_BOUNDARY

- V0.53 的三張新卡是對 `DLM-012 INSURANCE_LAW` 的 specialization refinement；DLM-012保留母路由，不回頭靜默重寫 V0.38。
- IAIS 2024 ICPs / ComFrame 是全球保險監理標準框架。ICP 18處理 intermediaries，ICP 19處理 conduct of business；它們不是各國直接可訴的單一保險法典。
- IAIS於2024-12採納ICS作為IAIGs的prescribed capital requirement與ComFrame量化部分；IAIS自身沒有直接替法域制定成文法的權力。2026 implementation path包含member baseline self-assessment；不得把adoption與domestic implementation混為一談。
- DLM-157沒有假造「全球統一保險契約法」。Rome I Article 7只是EU conflict-of-laws框架且排除reinsurance；UK CIDRA 2012與Insurance Act 2015只作比較法錨點，不得全球化其disclosure、warranty或fraud remedies。
- Insurance contract中insurable interest、late notice、bad faith、direct action、causation、warranty、claim limitation等均高度法域敏感；個案必須現查。
- Directive (EU) 2025/2已修訂Solvency II，但EIOPA current rulebook與2026 final guidance明確指向2027-01-30的新框架適用日。2026-08-25不得使用2027規則作current requirement。
- EIOPA 2026-07-15發布的revised group-solvency、reporting/disclosure、technical-provisions等Guidelines均明示2027-01-30適用；此前舊Guidelines繼續適用。`PUBLISHED != APPLICABLE`。
- Directive (EU) 2025/2設定risk-margin Cost-of-Capital 4.75%自2027-01-30起適用；不得倒灌至2026。
- Directive (EU) 2025/1 IRRD已存在且法律文本有效，但須於2027-01-29前轉置，規則自2027-01-30適用。本版只把它當future application / current-law interface，不宣稱2026已全面運行。
- IRRD同時顯示EU層級insurance guarantee schemes仍不是一個已完成的統一制度；Directive要求Commission於2027-01-29前就可能建立EU framework報告。個案仍需現查national scheme。
- IDD目前仍是EU insurance distribution的核心minimum framework。EIOPA 2026 Third IDD Report是監理實務／市場風險資料，不自行修法、不證明任何單一銷售違規。
- Retail Investment Strategy在2025-12已有Council-Parliament political agreement，2026-05又確認final compromise text；但European Parliament legislative-train在2026-06仍記載indicative plenary vote在2026-11。因此截至2026-08-25不得把RIS內容當已採納且已適用的IDD修法。
- 所有保險產品分類、mandatory coverage、cooling-off、claims handling、bad faith、commission、solvency intervention與resolution trigger均依產品、法域、日期與具體法源現查。

---

# G2-140｜V0.54 增量聲明

V0.54 不因「IRRD」「reinsurance」「insurance guarantee scheme」名稱出現就自動立卡。

本輪先以三條件做 de-dup / qualification：

```text
獨立第一眼
+
獨立法源生態
+
獨立失敗模式
```

審查結果：三個領域都已超出 DLM-157／158／159 可安全承載的範圍，因此正式新增：

```text
DLM-160｜保險恢復／危機處理法
DLM-161｜再保險法
DLM-162｜保戶保障／保險保證機制法
```

本輪最重要的去重不是「多三張卡」，而是把三種不同危機結構分開：

```text
INSURER STILL VIABLE / GOING CONCERN
→ DLM-158 prudential solvency

INSURER NON-VIABLE / LIKELY NON-VIABLE / ORDERLY EXIT
→ DLM-160 recovery / resolution

REINSURANCE RISK TRANSFER / CEDANT-REINSURER RELATION
→ DLM-161

POLICYHOLDER BACKSTOP AFTER FAILURE
→ DLM-162
```

因此：

```text
CAPITAL BREACH
!= RESOLUTION

REINSURANCE
!= DIRECT INSURANCE

RESOLUTION TOOL
!= GUARANTEE-SCHEME COVERAGE
```

本輪也確認：

> **DLM-103 是銀行／存款與銀行危機處理法，不因都叫 resolution 就吸收保險 resolution。**

銀行與保險的負債結構、保戶連續保障、資產負債久期、保單轉移、run-off 與保戶保障機制不同，不能共用一張危機處理卡。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-161-REINSURANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "再保險法"
  english_name: "Reinsurance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個ceding insurer透過treaty / facultative / proportional / non-proportional / retrocession或其他risk-transfer arrangement，把哪些risk、哪一層loss、哪一段期間與哪一個portfolio移轉給reinsurer；該安排在契約、監理與insolvency上是否真的有效，何時attachment、如何aggregation、recovery、claims control、follow-the-fortunes/settlements、collateral、credit for reinsurance、set-off、commutation與dispute resolution？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險人若只能自己承擔所有承保風險，巨災、長尾責任與跨境portfolio很容易超過單一balance sheet。再保險因此成為保險市場的第二層風險分配基礎設施。但再保險不是『保戶的第二張保單』：它通常是insurer與reinsurer之間的專業契約，並同時影響capital relief、risk concentration、cross-border exposure與insolvency recoverables。當契約文字、風險移轉實質與監理認列不一致時，最容易產生穩定錯誤。"

  first_move:
    "先確認誰是cedant、誰是reinsurer、什麼reinsurance form、covered portfolio / layer / event / period與attachment；不要先用direct-insurance的policyholder邏輯讀再保險。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "contractual allocation vs regulatory recognition"
    - "risk transfer vs financing disguised as reinsurance"
    - "cedant control vs reinsurer claims control"
    - "follow settlements vs coverage boundary"
    - "aggregation vs occurrence definition"
    - "capital relief vs counterparty credit risk"
    - "cross-border diversification vs collateral / local requirements"
    - "confidential expert market vs disclosure / supervision"
    - "portfolio transfer vs reinsurance continuity"
    - "insolvency estate vs reinsurer set-off / recapture / termination rights"
    - "cedant recovery vs direct policyholder expectations"
    - "reinsurance arbitration autonomy vs mandatory insolvency law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "再保險第一眼不是『原保險人把風險轉走了』；先問『轉了哪個風險、在什麼layer／event／period下attachment、契約如何計算recoverable、是否真的完成有效risk transfer、監理是否承認、原保險人失敗時誰還能向誰請求』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-142.1 第一眼｜PARTIES → FORM → SUBJECT BUSINESS → ATTACHMENT → LIMIT / RETENTION → EVENT / AGGREGATION → CLAIM → CONTROL → RECOVERY → CREDIT / COLLATERAL → INSOLVENCY → DISPUTE

```text
PARTIES
＝cedant? reinsurer? retrocessionaire? broker?

FORM
＝treaty? facultative? facultative-obligatory?

STRUCTURE
＝quota share? surplus? excess of loss? stop loss? catastrophe?

SUBJECT BUSINESS
＝which policies / portfolio / geography / underwriting year?

ATTACHMENT
＝when and at what loss threshold?

LIMIT / RETENTION
＝cedant retention? layer? reinstatement?

EVENT / AGGREGATION
＝one event? occurrence? hours clause? series?

CLAIM
＝covered loss? expense? allocated loss adjustment expense?

CONTROL
＝claims cooperation? claims control? settlement consent?

RECOVERY
＝calculation, proof, timing, currency?

CREDIT / COLLATERAL
＝regulatory credit? security? trust? letter of credit?

INSOLVENCY
＝set-off? cut-through? receiver? transfer? commutation?

DISPUTE
＝arbitration? court? governing law? seat?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-142.19 Evidence architecture

- slip / treaty / facultative certificate；
- wording / endorsements；
- underlying policy portfolio；
- bordereaux；
- premium records；
- loss notices；
- settlement documents；
- claims-control correspondence；
- aggregation evidence；
- actuarial calculations；
- collateral / trust documents；
- regulatory credit determination；
- reinsurer authorization status；
- commutation agreement；
- arbitration clause / award；
- insolvency / receiver order。

核心：

> **再保險不是把『原保險理賠結果』乘上一個百分比；要先找到它自己的契約、attachment、event、layer、risk transfer與監理認列鏈。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "MICA_CURRENT__2026_REVIEW_OPEN__FATF_VASP_IMPLEMENTATION_STILL_DYNAMIC__JURISDICTION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

MiCA is an operative EU framework, but the Commission opened its mandated 2026 review consultation through 30 September 2026. FATF was simultaneously continuing targeted implementation work on virtual assets/VASPs. Classification, authorization, stablecoin, custody and AML obligations therefore remain date- and jurisdiction-sensitive.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Targeted consultation on review of MiCA**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/consultations-0/targeted-consultation-review-mica-regulation_en
  - Verified point: The MiCA review consultation opened 20 May 2026 and remains open through 30 September 2026.
- **FATF — Outcomes of Plenary, 17–19 June 2026**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfgeneral/outcomes-fatf-plenary-june-2026.html
  - Verified point: FATF approved a seventh targeted update on virtual assets/VASPs and new work on gambling-sector illicit-finance risks.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

具有獨立first move／法源／證據或程序，保留為錢之流動內的專門節點。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-105-VIRTUAL_CRYPTO_ASSET_LAW"
  chinese_name: "虛擬資產／加密資產法"
  english_name: "Virtual / Crypto-Asset Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個token / crypto-asset在具體jurisdiction與use case中究竟代表什麼權利；issuer、protocol developer、CASP/VASP、exchange、custodian、wallet provider、validator、stablecoin issuer、user各負什麼義務；custody、private keys、issuance、white paper、market abuse、stablecoin reserve / redemption、staking、DeFi、AML Travel Rule、insolvency、cross-border offering與tokenised traditional assets如何分流？"

  historical_reason:
    "crypto systems把帳本、transfer、custody與asset representation從傳統金融中介拆散，讓一個global network可以在沒有單一中央帳簿的情況下記錄控制狀態。但法律並未因此消失：誰真正擁有token、custodian破產時客戶拿回什麼、stablecoin能否兌付、protocol誰能修改、exchange在哪些國家提供服務、token是否其實是security / e-money / fund interest，都需要把技術控制重新投影回財產、金融、消費、AML與程序法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "decentralisation vs accountable control"
    - "technical control vs legal ownership"
    - "permissionless transfer vs AML / sanctions"
    - "global protocol vs territorial regulation"
    - "innovation vs investor / consumer protection"
    - "stablecoin usability vs reserve / run risk"
    - "self-custody autonomy vs recovery / fraud risk"
    - "smart-contract automation vs legal remedies"
    - "open-source protocol vs identifiable service provider"
    - "tokenisation vs inherited asset law"
    - "market speed vs disclosure / market integrity"
    - "privacy-enhancing technology vs traceability"
    - "forks / upgrades vs holder expectations"

  shortest_maxim:
    "虛擬資產法第一眼不是『鏈上顯示誰有token』；先問『token在法律上代表什麼、誰控制private key／protocol／custody、holder對誰有何種claim、哪個jurisdiction的financial / property / AML law命名它，以及失敗時asset是否真的能從平台財產中分離』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 111.1 第一眼｜TOKEN / FUNCTION → LEGAL CLASSIFICATION → ISSUER / CONTROL → HOLDER RIGHT → CUSTODY / KEY → MARKET / SERVICE PROVIDER → TRANSFER / SETTLEMENT → DISCLOSURE / INTEGRITY → AML / SANCTIONS → INSOLVENCY → CROSS-BORDER REMEDY

```text
TOKEN / FUNCTION
＝payment? investment? utility? governance? stablecoin? NFT?

LEGAL CLASSIFICATION
＝security? fund? e-money? deposit-like? commodity? property?

ISSUER / CONTROL
＝issuer? foundation? protocol admin? DAO? no issuer?

HOLDER RIGHT
＝redemption? profit? voting? access? nothing against issuer?

CUSTODY / KEY
＝self-hosted? omnibus custodian? multisig?

MARKET / SERVICE PROVIDER
＝exchange? broker? transfer? staking? custody?

TRANSFER / SETTLEMENT
＝on-chain finality? off-chain internal ledger?

DISCLOSURE / INTEGRITY
＝white paper? reserve? market abuse? listing?

AML / SANCTIONS
＝VASP? travel rule? screening?

INSOLVENCY
＝segregated asset? unsecured claim? trust?

CROSS-BORDER REMEDY
＝which regulator / court / law?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 111.36 Evidence architecture

- token code / contract；
- issuer terms；
- white paper；
- reserve / custody agreements；
- wallet addresses；
- private-key governance；
- multisig；
- blockchain transactions；
- exchange internal ledger；
- KYC / geolocation；
- Travel Rule records；
- market data；
- oracle data；
- staking terms；
- protocol admin / governance；
- MiCA / local licence；
- FATF classification；
- insolvency asset records；
- chain analytics methodology。

核心：

> **crypto案件最危險的捷徑是把technical control寫成legal ownership，或把「去中心化」寫成無責任主體。鏈上可以精確記錄一件技術事件，但法律還必須回答這個事件對誰、依哪個法、生成什麼權利。**

31. property / trust；
32. secured interests；

34. FSB recommendations；
35. Basel SCO60 for banks；
36. complaint / ADR / court jurisdiction。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "FATF_STANDARDS_CURRENT__EU_AML_PACKAGE_TRANSITION_TO_2027__JURISDICTION_ENTITY_AND_RISK_SCOPE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

FATF standards remain current and were revised in 2025 to strengthen proportionality and lower-risk simplified measures. The EU AML package is in a transition period toward the 2027 application of AMLR 2024/1624. Entity scope, risk assessment, beneficial ownership, sanctions and supervisory allocation therefore require current jurisdiction checks.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **FATF — The FATF Recommendations**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfrecommendations/Fatf-recommendations.html
  - Verified point: The FATF Recommendations remain current and reflect 2025 revisions emphasizing proportionality/simplified measures and targeted, risk-based NPO controls.
- **European Commission — AML/CFT at EU level**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/financial-crime/anti-money-laundering-and-countering-financing-terrorism-eu-level_en
  - Verified point: The EU AML package includes AMLR 2024/1624, described by the Commission as applying from 2027, and the AMLA Regulation 2024/1620.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

不是某一金融產品的子法，而是讓跨金融／企業金流可辨識、可報導、可驗證的橫向控制。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-068-ANTI_MONEY_LAUNDERING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "洗錢防制法"
  english_name: "Anti-Money Laundering / CFT / CPF Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "金融系統、公司、信託、專業服務、虛擬資產與跨境交易如何被用來隱藏犯罪利益、恐怖融資或擴散融資；國家與義務機構如何以 risk-based approach 進行 CDD、beneficial ownership、transaction monitoring、STR、record keeping、FIU cooperation、targeted financial sanctions 與 asset recovery，而不把風險評估偷換成犯罪判決或全面拒絕服務？"

  historical_reason:
    "犯罪所得若能自由進入金融與商業系統，就能轉成房產、公司、證券、虛擬資產與跨境財富，讓原犯罪的經濟收益被保存。AML/CFT/CPF 因此把私人金融與專業機構變成預防性 gatekeepers；但這種治理也會產生監控、de-risking、financial exclusion、資料共享與 false positive 風險。現代 AML 法的核心不是『多抓可疑人』，而是以 proportional risk-based controls 建立可驗證的 customer / owner / transaction / reporting chain。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "financial integrity vs privacy"
    - "risk-based approach vs equal access"
    - "CDD burden vs inclusion"
    - "STR confidentiality vs defence / transparency"
    - "beneficial ownership transparency vs privacy / accuracy"
    - "FIU intelligence vs judicial evidence"
    - "preventive freeze vs property rights"
    - "PEP enhanced diligence vs stigma"
    - "virtual asset innovation vs traceability"
    - "cross-border information sharing vs data safeguards"
    - "de-risking vs proportionality"
    - "AML effectiveness vs checkbox compliance"

  shortest_maxim:
    "洗錢防制法第一眼不是『這筆錢看起來怪』；先問『原始資產／犯罪是什麼、誰真正控制帳戶／法人、交易為何這樣走、哪個義務人應做何種風險措施、可疑資訊最後進了哪一個情報／司法程序』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 74.1 第一眼｜ASSET / PREDICATE → CUSTOMER → BENEFICIAL OWNER → PRODUCT / CHANNEL → TRANSACTION → RISK → CONTROL → STR / FIU → INVESTIGATION → CONFISCATION

```text
ASSET / PREDICATE
＝犯罪所得？合法資金？TF / PF？

CUSTOMER
＝個人？公司？trust？VASP？

BENEFICIAL OWNER
＝誰最終 owns / controls？

PRODUCT / CHANNEL
＝bank？securities？insurance？real estate？crypto？cash？

TRANSACTION
＝amount、frequency、counterparty、jurisdiction、purpose？

RISK
＝customer / geographic / product / delivery / transaction risk？

CONTROL
＝CDD、EDD、simplified measure、monitoring、screening？

STR / FIU
＝是否形成 suspicious transaction report / intelligence？

INVESTIGATION
＝是否有 predicate / laundering evidence？

CONFISCATION
＝是否依法追 proceeds？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 74.31 證據特徵

- KYC / CDD；
- corporate registry；
- beneficial ownership；
- trust documents；
- account statements；
- transaction graph；
- invoices；
- trade documents；
- source-of-funds / wealth；
- PEP record；
- screening；
- internal alerts；
- STR / SAR；
- FIU dissemination；
- blockchain records；
- VASP records；
- correspondence banking；
- DNFBP files；
- freeze / confiscation；
- mutual legal assistance；
- supervisory findings。

核心：

> **AML 案件最容易把「風險監控資料」誤當「犯罪證據」。理火必須保留：compliance alert → suspicion → intelligence → investigation → admissible evidence → offence 的層級差。**

# DLM-070｜會計／財務報導法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-070"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "會計／財務報導法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "C"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "CONTROL_INTEGRITY_DISCLOSURE"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "CONTROL_PLANE"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_HORIZONTAL"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 76｜DLM-070｜會計／財務報導法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B24"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、G:資訊／知識／數位"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

不是某一金融產品的子法，而是讓跨金融／企業金流可辨識、可報導、可驗證的橫向控制。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-070-ACCOUNTING_FINANCIAL_REPORTING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "會計／財務報導法"
  english_name: "Accounting / Financial Reporting Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "法律與會計準則如何把企業或其他實體的交易、資產、負債、權益、收入、費用、現金流、估計與揭露轉成可比較的財務資訊；適用哪套 reporting framework、誰負責編製、何時認列與衡量、何時合併、什麼是 material、哪些估計／不確定性必須揭露，又如何避免把會計表述誤當經濟世界本身？"

  historical_reason:
    "資本市場、債權人、股東、監管者與契約相對人不能直接看見企業所有交易，只能依賴管理階層編製的財務資訊。會計準則因此建立共同語言，使不同企業與期間具有一定可比較性；但它必然依賴分類、估計、judgment、materiality 與 reporting boundary。財務報導法的核心不是製造『唯一真實數字』，而是規定誰、依何框架、用何方法、在何期間、以什麼揭露責任形成可被市場使用的資訊。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "economic substance vs legal / accounting form"
    - "comparability vs entity-specific judgment"
    - "relevance vs reliability / faithful representation"
    - "timeliness vs measurement uncertainty"
    - "prudence vs neutrality"
    - "management judgment vs opportunistic accounting"
    - "materiality vs information overload"
    - "fair value relevance vs model uncertainty"
    - "consolidation transparency vs complex group structures"
    - "financial reporting vs tax accounting"
    - "current performance vs going-concern uncertainty"
    - "global standards vs domestic endorsement"
    - "digital reporting efficiency vs tagging error"

  shortest_maxim:
    "會計／財報法第一眼不是『數字是多少』；先問『誰編、依哪套 framework、哪個 reporting entity、什麼交易／權利義務、何時認列、怎麼衡量、哪些 judgment／estimate、哪些資訊對使用者 material』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 76.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → FRAMEWORK → TRANSACTION / EVENT → RECOGNITION → MEASUREMENT → PRESENTATION → DISCLOSURE → MATERIALITY → CONTROL / CONSOLIDATION → FILING

```text
ENTITY
＝哪個 legal entity / group / reporting entity？

FRAMEWORK
＝IFRS？local GAAP？public-sector standard？sector rules？

EVENT
＝sale？lease？loan？acquisition？impairment？provision？related party？

RECOGNITION
＝何時進 balance sheet / P&L？

MEASUREMENT
＝cost？fair value？amortized cost？estimate？

PRESENTATION
＝哪個 statement / line item？

DISCLOSURE
＝notes、risk、judgment、uncertainty？

MATERIALITY
＝對使用者決策是否重要？

CONSOLIDATION
＝誰控制誰？是否納入 group？

FILING
＝誰簽、何時申報、用什麼數位格式？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 76.29 證據特徵

- reporting framework / endorsement；
- chart of accounts；
- ledger；
- journal entries；
- invoices；
- contracts；
- delivery / performance evidence；
- bank statements；
- valuation；
- estimates；
- management models；
- consolidation files；
- related-party registers；
- internal controls；
- board / audit committee minutes；
- financial statements；
- notes；
- filings；
- XBRL / taxonomy mapping；
- regulator correspondence。

核心：

> **財報案要從報表數字一路反推到 ledger、entry、source document、business event 與適用準則；一個被漂亮排版的數字，只有在整條生成鏈能被追蹤時才有資格被相信。**

```yaml
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "CONTROL_PLANE"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_HORIZONTAL"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 77｜DLM-071｜審計／確信法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B24"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、G:資訊／知識／數位"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

不是某一金融產品的子法，而是讓跨金融／企業金流可辨識、可報導、可驗證的橫向控制。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-071-AUDIT_ASSURANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "審計／確信法"
  english_name: "Audit / Assurance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當 auditor / assurance practitioner 被授權對管理階層或其他責任方產製的資訊提供獨立 assurance 時，他究竟查什麼、採何 evidence、達到什麼 assurance level、如何評估 material misstatement、fraud、estimates、going concern 與 controls；independence、quality management、reporting 與 professional skepticism 又如何讓 opinion 取得可信資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "財報由管理階層編製，而資本提供者與公共市場無法逐筆自行驗證，因此形成 independent audit。審計的制度價值不是重新編製一份『真實財報』，而是依風險導向程序取得 sufficient appropriate evidence，對資訊是否在適用 criteria 下不存在 material misstatement 提供合理程度的 assurance。這種有限但高度公共化的信賴必須依賴 independence、skepticism、quality management、documentation 與透明 reporting。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "client payment vs auditor independence"
    - "reasonable assurance vs public expectation of guarantee"
    - "sampling / risk-based audit vs complete verification"
    - "fraud concealment vs audit detectability"
    - "management representations vs contradictory evidence"
    - "professional judgment vs standardized methodology"
    - "confidentiality vs reporting obligations"
    - "audit efficiency vs sufficient appropriate evidence"
    - "going-concern uncertainty vs binary opinion expectations"
    - "group audit reliance vs component evidence"
    - "technology / AI efficiency vs auditability"
    - "regulatory oversight vs professional independence"

  shortest_maxim:
    "審計／確信法第一眼不是『會計師簽了沒有』；先問『是什麼 engagement、對什麼 subject matter、依什麼 criteria、提供何種 assurance、哪些 material risks、取得哪些 evidence、independence 如何、最後 opinion 真正說了什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 77.1 第一眼｜ENGAGEMENT → RESPONSIBLE PARTY → SUBJECT MATTER → CRITERIA → ASSURANCE LEVEL → MATERIALITY → RISK → EVIDENCE → CONCLUSION → REPORT

```text
ENGAGEMENT
＝audit？review？other assurance？agreed-upon procedures？

RESPONSIBLE PARTY
＝management / preparer？

SUBJECT MATTER
＝financial statements？controls？sustainability information？

CRITERIA
＝IFRS？local GAAP？other suitable criteria？

ASSURANCE LEVEL
＝reasonable？limited？none？

MATERIALITY
＝何種 misstatement 對 users 重要？

RISK
＝fraud？estimate？revenue？going concern？IT？

EVIDENCE
＝inspection？confirmation？reperformance？analytics？expert？

CONCLUSION
＝unmodified？qualified？adverse？disclaimer？limited assurance conclusion？

REPORT
＝scope、basis、key matters / going concern disclosures where applicable？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 77.32 證據特徵

- engagement letter；
- independence confirmation；
- client acceptance；
- planning；
- materiality；
- risk assessment；
- audit programme；
- samples；
- confirmations；
- invoices / contracts；
- journal testing；
- estimates；
- expert reports；
- IT controls；
- group instructions；
- management representation；
- audit committee communication；
- review notes；
- quality review；
- financial statements；
- audit opinion；
- regulator inspection；
- audit file retention。

核心：

> **審計責任案真正要審的是「auditor 當時面對哪些 risk、取得什麼 evidence、看到什麼矛盾、做了什麼 response、為何最後可以簽那個 opinion」，不是只用公司後來成功或倒閉倒推專業責任。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "FINANCIAL_REGULATORY_PERIMETER_DYNAMIC__SECTOR_LAW_TRANSPOSITION_LEVEL2_AND_SUPERVISORY_STATUS_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The common financial-regulation mother layer cannot be treated as a single static code. 2026 EU materials show materially different implementation/transition states across banking, investment-services, insurance, payments, AML and crypto frameworks; sector classification and current implementing measures control.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — MiFID II amendment transposition status**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/enforcement-and-infringements-banking-and-finance-law/monitoring-banking-and-finance-directives/markets-financial-instruments-directive-ii-amendment-mifid-ii-amended-directive-eu-2024790_en
  - Verified point: As of 7 August 2026, 22 Member States reported full transposition; five reported no measures and infringement proceedings were pending against 13.
- **European Commission — Solvency II implementing/delegated acts**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/implementing-and-delegated-acts/solvency-2_en
  - Verified point: The Solvency II framework remains current; Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/269 was published in February 2026 and the framework continues to be amended.
- **European Commission — AML/CFT at EU level**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/financial-crime/anti-money-laundering-and-countering-financing-terrorism-eu-level_en
  - Verified point: The EU AML package includes AMLR 2024/1624, described by the Commission as applying from 2027, and the AMLA Regulation 2024/1620.
- **European Commission — Targeted consultation on review of MiCA**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/consultations-0/targeted-consultation-review-mica-regulation_en
  - Verified point: The MiCA review consultation opened 20 May 2026 and remains open through 30 September 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留金融監理共同層：regulatory perimeter、授權／牌照、監督、跨業治理；銀行、支付、crypto、證券、保險的sector detail改由專門卡取得優先路由。

### PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION ROUTING LOCK

```text
FINANCIAL REGULATORY PERIMETER / AUTHORIZATION / LICENSING / COMMON SUPERVISION → DLM-011
BANKING / DEPOSITS / BANK FAILURE → DLM-103
BANK CAPITAL / LIQUIDITY → DLM-154
MACROPRUDENTIAL / SYSTEMIC RISK → DLM-155
RETAIL BANKING CONDUCT / FINANCIAL CONSUMER → DLM-156
PAYMENT / CLEARING / E-MONEY → DLM-104
CRYPTO / VIRTUAL ASSET → DLM-105

INSURANCE COMMON LAYER → DLM-012; SPECIALIZED INSURANCE → DLM-157..162
```

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-011-FINANCIAL_REGULATION"
  chinese_name: "金融監管法"
  english_name: "Financial Regulation"

  domain_core_question:
    "金融機構把他人的資金、信用、期限與風險高度集中並彼此連結時，為什麼它們不能只像普通公司一樣依契約自由承擔風險；國家如何透過准入、資本、流動性、治理、行為監理、危機處理與退出制度維持信任與金融穩定？"

  historical_reason:
    "銀行與其他金融中介通常涉及存款、槓桿、期限轉換、支付與信用創造。單一機構倒閉可能透過擠兌、支付中斷、資產拋售與互相曝險擴散到整個經濟。普通公司法與一般破產法處理『一家企業失敗』，但不一定能處理『一家金融機構失敗造成系統功能中斷』。金融監管因此建立事前審慎監理與特殊危機處理制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "金融創新與競爭 vs 安全穩健"
    - "契約自由 vs 存款人／客戶保護"
    - "高槓桿效率 vs 系統性風險"
    - "風險承擔 vs 資本與流動性緩衝"
    - "個別機構利益 vs 整體金融穩定"
    - "監管防弊 vs 道德風險"
    - "救助關鍵功能 vs 避免納稅人替股東承擔損失"
    - "跨境金融集團整合 vs 國家監管分割"

  shortest_maxim:
    "金融監管第一眼不是『契約有沒有違約』；先問『這個機構在金融系統裡做什麼功能、拿誰的錢、承擔什麼期限與槓桿、失敗時會傳染到哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 17.1 第一眼｜FUNCTION → LICENSE → BALANCE SHEET → RISK → FAILURE PATH

金融案件先問：

```text
FUNCTION
＝吸收存款？放款？支付？清算？證券中介？資產管理？衍生品？穩定幣？其他金融功能？

LICENSE / REGULATORY PERIMETER
＝它被什麼法律分類？需不需要牌照？由誰監管？

BALANCE SHEET / CLIENT ASSETS
＝資產、負債、槓桿、客戶資產怎麼配置？

RISK
＝信用？市場？流動性？操作？利率？集中？交易對手？網路？行為風險？

FAILURE PATH
＝如果它今天失敗，誰拿不到錢、哪個支付／金融功能中斷、風險會不會擴散？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 17.10 證據特徵

金融監管案件常需：

- 牌照；
- 組織架構；
- 資產負債表；
- 資本比率；
- 流動性資料；
- 大額曝險；
- 風險模型；
- 壓力測試；
- 資產分類；
- 客戶資產；
- 支付／清算流；
- 董事會風險資料；
- 監管申報；
- 主管機關函文；
- 內控與合規；
- 銷售錄音／紀錄；
- 恢復與處理計畫；
- 跨境集團曝險。

核心：

> **金融法證據往往不是只證明「發生了什麼」，而是證明「風險怎麼在資產負債表與系統中傳遞」。**

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 17.12 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依業務與法域現查：

1. 金融業務定義與牌照；
2. 銀行／非銀行金融機構分類；
3. 資本要求；
4. 流動性要求；
5. 大額曝險；
6. 存款保險；
7. 消費者／投資人保護；
8. AML/CFT；
9. 支付與清算；
10. 金融市場基礎設施；
11. recovery / resolution；
12. 金融集團與跨境監理；
13. Basel Framework 之本國實施；
14. 新型金融商品／數位資產；
15. 監管裁罰與程序救濟。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_IFR_IFD_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__MIFID_AMENDMENT_TRANSPOSITION_INCOMPLETE_2026__JURISDICTION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The EU bespoke prudential regime for non-systemic investment firms remains IFR/IFD, while related MiFID II amendment transposition was incomplete on 7 August 2026: the Commission recorded 22 Member States with full transposition and five with no communicated measures. Firm classification and national implementation must be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Prudential rules for investment firms**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/financial-markets/financial-markets-policy/prudential-rules-investment-firms_en
  - Verified point: The IFR/IFD framework remains the EU bespoke prudential regime for non-systemic investment firms.
- **European Commission — MiFID II amendment transposition status**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/enforcement-and-infringements-banking-and-finance-law/monitoring-banking-and-finance-directives/markets-financial-instruments-directive-ii-amendment-mifid-ii-amended-directive-eu-2024790_en
  - Verified point: As of 7 August 2026, 22 Member States reported full transposition; five reported no measures and infringement proceedings were pending against 13.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

審慎／系統風險是跨不同金融業態的control plane，不按交易時間順序排在單一產品後面。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-150-INVESTMENT_FIRM_PRUDENTIAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "投資機構審慎監管法"
  english_name: "Investment Firm Prudential Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一家提供投資服務、brokerage、dealing、underwriting、portfolio management或其他MiFID / securities activities的investment firm，應適用銀行型CRR/CRD或投資機構專屬IFR/IFD／其他本國financial-responsibility regime；其own funds、fixed overheads、K-factors、liquidity、concentration、group supervision、client asset exposure、governance、SREP、wind-down與failure containment資格如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "投資機構可能同時是輕資產代理人，也可能持有客戶資產、用自有資本交易、承銷巨額證券或成為重要市場流動性提供者。若所有券商都照銀行監理，會把存款／放款風險硬套進不同商業模式；但若只靠conduct rules，又無法回答公司本身倒閉時是否有足夠資源完成有秩序退出。投資機構審慎法因此不是『服務客戶要不要盡責』，而是『這個中介本身能否在失敗時不把客戶與市場一起拖下去』。"

  first_move:
    "先做entity classification與business-model map：investment firm還是credit institution；dealing on own account / underwriting與否；size / group thresholds；client money / assets；AUM / orders / trading exposures；再決定 prudential regime，不能先拿銀行資本比率套所有券商。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "proportional prudential rules vs systemic-risk containment"
    - "agency business vs principal / trading-book risk"
    - "client protection vs firm solvency"
    - "capital adequacy vs market liquidity provision"
    - "fixed overhead wind-down capacity vs risk-sensitive capital"
    - "small-firm proportionality vs regulatory arbitrage"
    - "group supervision vs legal-entity separation"
    - "client asset segregation vs residual operational risk"
    - "liquidity buffers vs business flexibility"
    - "prudential supervision vs conduct supervision"
    - "recovery / wind-down vs continued market access"
    - "EU investment-firm regime vs bank-like CRR treatment"

  shortest_maxim:
    "投資機構審慎法第一眼不是『券商有沒有騙客戶』；先問『它是哪一類investment firm、風險是在客戶、市場還是自己資產負債表、失敗時需要多少可吸收損失與流動性才能有秩序退出』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-106.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → LICENCE ACTIVITIES → CLASSIFICATION → CLIENT ASSETS / MONEY → BALANCE SHEET / TRADING → OWN FUNDS → K-FACTORS / RISK METRICS → LIQUIDITY → CONCENTRATION → GROUP → GOVERNANCE / SREP → WIND-DOWN

```text
ENTITY
＝investment firm? broker-dealer? bank / credit institution? adviser?

LICENCE ACTIVITIES
＝advice? portfolio management? execution? dealing on own account? underwriting?

CLASSIFICATION
＝bank-like / class 1? class 1 minus? class 2? small non-interconnected / class 3?

CLIENT ASSETS / MONEY
＝held / safeguarded / administered?

BALANCE SHEET / TRADING
＝principal positions? derivatives? counterparty exposure?

OWN FUNDS
＝permanent minimum? fixed overheads? K-factor? net capital?

LIQUIDITY
＝liquid asset requirement / stress need?

CONCENTRATION
＝large exposure / trading-book concentration?

GROUP
＝prudential consolidation? group capital test?

GOVERNANCE / SREP
＝internal capital / liquidity assessment? supervisory add-ons?

WIND-DOWN
＝can the firm exit without disorderly client / market harm?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-106.27 Evidence architecture

- legal entity / licence；
- authorised investment services；
- group structure；
- class determination；
- balance sheet / off-balance-sheet items；
- own funds composition；
- fixed overhead calculation；
- K-factor data / methodology；
- client money / assets records；
- trading-book / counterparty exposure；
- liquidity buffer；
- concentration reports；
- prudential consolidation；
- SREP decisions / supervisory add-ons；
- governance / remuneration policies；
- wind-down plan；
- net capital computations；
- reserve / possession-control records；
- regulatory reporting history；
- current IFR / IFD / CRR / SEC rules。

核心：

> **投資機構案不能只看『客戶有沒有受害』。審慎法先問這個中介自己如何承受風險、失敗時如何退出，以及客戶資產與市場連續性是否會被它的資產負債表拖走。**

## FAILURE_MODES

## G2-106.28 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "券商沒有mis-selling＝財務健全。"
  - "有MiFID / SEC licence＝capital與liquidity都合規。"
  - "investment firm一律適用IFR，不可能適用bank-like CRR。"
  - "公司小＝IFR class 3。"
  - "符合一個Article 12 threshold＝small and non-interconnected。"
  - "own funds＝會計股東權益。"
  - "K-factor＝主管機關主觀風險打分。"
  - "client assets不在balance sheet＝prudentially irrelevant。"
  - "資本足夠＝流動性足夠。"
  - "group內每家公司分開＝沒有prudential consolidation。"
  - "Reg BI＝美國券商資本規則。"
  - "GAAP net worth positive＝Rule 15c3-1 net capital一定合規。"
```

# G2-107｜第五十批交界辨識器

```yaml
BATCH_50_INTERSECTION_ROUTER:

  if_question_is_about_public_bid_for_control_of_listed_or_public_company:
    primary:
      - "DLM-148-PUBLIC_TAKEOVER_TENDER_OFFER_LAW"
    secondary:
      - "DLM-140-CORPORATE_MA_CONTROL_TRANSACTIONS_LAW"
      - "DLM-139-CORPORATE_GOVERNANCE_DIRECTORS_DUTIES_LAW"
      - "DLM-141-SHAREHOLDER_RIGHTS_MINORITY_REMEDIES_LAW"
      - "DLM-143-ISSUER_CONTINUOUS_DISCLOSURE_SECURITIES_FRAUD_LAW"
      - "DLM-144-INSIDER_TRADING_MARKET_ABUSE_MANIPULATION_LAW"
      - "DLM-136-MERGER_CONTROL_CONCENTRATION_LAW"

  if_question_is_about_trade_completion_clearing_settlement_csd_or_ccp:
    primary:
      - "DLM-149-SECURITIES_SETTLEMENT_CSD_CCP_LAW"
    secondary:
      - "DLM-147-BROKER_DEALER_SECURITIES_INTERMEDIARY_CONDUCT_LAW"
      - "DLM-104-PAYMENTS_CLEARING_ELECTRONIC_MONEY_LAW"
      - "DLM-011-FINANCIAL_REGULATION"
      - "DLM-098-CYBERSECURITY_LAW"

  if_question_is_about_capital_liquidity_solvency_or_wind_down_of_investment_firm:
    primary:
      - "DLM-150-INVESTMENT_FIRM_PRUDENTIAL_LAW"
    secondary:
      - "DLM-011-FINANCIAL_REGULATION"
      - "DLM-147-BROKER_DEALER_SECURITIES_INTERMEDIARY_CONDUCT_LAW"
      - "DLM-103-BANKING_DEPOSITS_BANK_RESOLUTION_LAW"
      - "DLM-070-ACCOUNTING_FINANCIAL_REPORTING_LAW"
      - "DLM-071-AUDIT_ASSURANCE_LAW"

  takeover_vs_corporate_ma:
    rule:
      "DLM-140先處理公司法交易架構、董事與股東批准；DLM-148只在控制權取得透過公開要約／tender-offer制度時處理市場程序。"

  takeover_vs_merger_control:
    rule:
      "DLM-148的mandatory bid / equitable price / equal treatment不能替代DLM-136競爭法的concentration / competitive-effects審查。"

  settlement_vs_payment:
    rule:
      "DLM-104處理支付、清算與電子貨幣的金融支付母問題；DLM-149處理證券交易post-trade、CSD、SSS、CCP與securities/cash-leg finality。"

  conduct_vs_prudential:
    rule:
      "DLM-147回答中介如何對客戶行為；DLM-150回答中介自身有沒有足夠資本、流動性與失敗處理能力。"
```

最短路由：

```text
PUBLIC BID TO ACQUIRE CONTROL
→ DLM-148

TRADE HAS EXECUTED, DID IT CLEAR / SETTLE?
→ DLM-149

CAN THE INVESTMENT FIRM SURVIVE / WIND DOWN?
→ DLM-150
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "BASEL_III_CURRENT__GLOBAL_IMPLEMENTATION_INCOMPLETE_AND_VARIED__DOMESTIC_RULES_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

Basel III remains the international prudential baseline, but global implementation is not synchronized. In March 2026 GHOS said about 75% of Basel Committee member jurisdictions had implemented or would shortly implement the standards, with remaining jurisdictions still on implementation paths.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Basel Committee / GHOS — Basel III implementation progress, 9 March 2026**
  - URL: https://www.bis.org/press/p260309.htm
  - Verified point: About 75% of Basel Committee member jurisdictions had implemented or would shortly implement the standards; remaining jurisdictions reported implementation plans.
- **Basel Committee — RCAP assessment schedule**
  - URL: https://www.bis.org/bcbs/implementation/rcap_aoi.htm
  - Verified point: Jurisdictional Basel III assessments continue on schedules extending through 2030, confirming non-uniform implementation timing.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

審慎／系統風險是跨不同金融業態的control plane，不按交易時間順序排在單一產品後面。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-154-BANK_PRUDENTIAL_CAPITAL_LIQUIDITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "銀行審慎資本／流動性法"
  english_name: "Bank Prudential Capital / Liquidity Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "銀行或credit institution在持續經營狀態下，應以多少、什麼品質的capital吸收信用、市場、操作與其他損失；應維持什麼liquidity與stable funding以承受短期提款與長期融資壓力；RWA、leverage、large exposure、stress test、governance與supervisory review如何共同形成prudential buffer；又哪些Basel／EU／domestic標準在特定日期與法域真正取得法位？"

  historical_reason:
    "銀行以高槓桿、期限轉換與可提取負債支持較長期且具有風險的資產。單看契約履約或帳面資產總額，無法回答銀行遭受損失或提款壓力時能否繼續運作。2008年金融危機後，Basel III及各法域改革把capital quality、risk-weighted assets、leverage、LCR、NSFR、stress testing與監理審查重新組合成going-concern resilience架構。其核心不是讓銀行永不失敗，而是要求損失與流動性壓力在進入危機處理以前先被可識別的buffer承受。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識entity／consolidation level、applicable prudential regime、reporting date、capital stack、RWA method、leverage exposure、liquidity profile與national implementation；不要看到『Basel III』就直接把國際標準數字當成本國現行法律。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "credit intermediation vs loss-absorbing capacity"
    - "risk sensitivity vs model complexity / gaming"
    - "internal models vs comparability / output floor"
    - "capital efficiency vs resilience"
    - "short-term liquidity transformation vs run risk"
    - "HQLA usability in stress vs mechanical ratio compliance"
    - "bank-specific supervision vs uniform minimum standards"
    - "global Basel consistency vs jurisdictional implementation timing"
    - "microprudential safety vs credit supply / competitiveness"
    - "risk-based capital vs leverage backstop"
    - "consolidated group resilience vs entity-level creditor protection"
    - "supervisory judgment vs legal predictability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "銀行審慎法第一眼不是『資本適足率多少』；先問『哪個法域、哪個entity與consolidation level、哪一種capital／RWA／leverage／liquidity指標、哪個日期、哪個transition，這個數字究竟在吸收什麼風險』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-124.1 第一眼｜ENTITY / SCOPE → CAPITAL QUALITY → RWA → OUTPUT FLOOR → LEVERAGE → LCR → NSFR → LARGE EXPOSURE → SUPERVISORY REVIEW → STRESS → REMEDY

```text
ENTITY / SCOPE
＝bank? credit institution? parent? subsidiary? branch? consolidated group?

CAPITAL QUALITY
＝CET1? AT1? Tier 2? deductions? eligibility?

RWA
＝credit? market? operational? CVA? counterparty?

OUTPUT FLOOR
＝internal-model RWA被standardised benchmark限制到哪個phase-in level？

LEVERAGE
＝non-risk-based backstop是否觸發？

LCR
＝30-day stress下HQLA / net cash outflows？

NSFR
＝one-year horizon下available / required stable funding？

LARGE EXPOSURE
＝對單一counterparty / connected clients集中到什麼程度？

SUPERVISORY REVIEW
＝Pillar 2 / SREP / domestic supervisory add-on？

STRESS
＝base case之外的loss / liquidity scenario？

REMEDY
＝capital plan? distribution restriction? remediation? early intervention?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-124.23 Evidence architecture

- applicable banking statute / CRR / CRD / prudential rule；
- entity / group / consolidation map；
- capital instruments and terms；
- CET1 / AT1 / Tier 2 reconciliation；
- RWA by risk class；
- model approvals / validation；
- output-floor calculation；
- leverage exposure measure；
- LCR / HQLA / cash-flow ladder；
- NSFR / funding maturity；
- deposit concentration；
- large exposures；
- stress-testing assumptions；
- ICAAP / ILAAP / supervisory findings where applicable；
- supervisory decisions / add-ons；
- distribution restrictions；
- transition / grandfathering provisions。

核心：

> **審慎案件不是找到一個capital ratio就結束；要證明計算範圍、資本品質、風險分母、流動性假設、日期與監理法源全部對得上。**
