Macroprudential tools sit on top of jurisdiction-specific prudential implementation and can change with the cycle. Basel implementation/assessment remains staggered across jurisdictions, so countercyclical/systemic buffers and activation dates cannot be frozen in this database.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Basel Committee / GHOS — Basel III implementation progress, 9 March 2026**
  - URL: https://www.bis.org/press/p260309.htm
  - Verified point: About 75% of Basel Committee member jurisdictions had implemented or would shortly implement the standards; remaining jurisdictions reported implementation plans.
- **Basel Committee — RCAP assessment schedule**
  - URL: https://www.bis.org/bcbs/implementation/rcap_aoi.htm
  - Verified point: Jurisdictional Basel III assessments continue on schedules extending through 2030, confirming non-uniform implementation timing.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

審慎／系統風險是跨不同金融業態的control plane，不按交易時間順序排在單一產品後面。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-155-MACROPRUDENTIAL_SYSTEMIC_RISK_REGULATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "宏觀審慎／系統性風險監管法"
  english_name: "Macroprudential / Systemic Risk Regulation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "即使每一家金融機構單獨看來都符合法定最低要求，整體金融系統是否因共同曝險、信用週期、資產價格、槓桿、流動性錯配、互聯網絡或系統重要性而形成集體脆弱；主管機關何時可使用CCyB、systemic risk buffer、G-SIB/O-SII buffers、borrower-based measures、sectoral tools或其他system-wide措施，又如何處理跨境reciprocity、時間釋放與比例原則？"

  historical_reason:
    "2008年金融危機證明，microprudential supervision逐家確認銀行安全，仍可能無法看見整個系統同時累積同一種風險。每家銀行個別降低風險的行動，在市場壓力下還可能共同賣出資產、縮減信用，反而放大系統衝擊。宏觀審慎因此把觀察單位從單一機構提升到金融週期、共同曝險、互聯結構與關鍵功能，目的不是取代個別監理，而是處理『每個局部看似合理，整體卻可能不穩定』的合成風險。"

  first_move:
    "先確認被主張的是institution-specific weakness還是system-wide / sector-wide vulnerability；再辨識designated authority、instrument、activation indicator、scope、rate、effective date與release condition，不要看到房價上升或一家大銀行就自動喊systemic risk。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "individual optimisation vs collective stability"
    - "credit growth vs financial cycle restraint"
    - "resilience buffer vs credit availability"
    - "rule-based indicators vs supervisory discretion"
    - "national macroprudential autonomy vs cross-border spillovers"
    - "early intervention vs uncertainty / false positives"
    - "systemic institution surcharge vs equal treatment"
    - "housing affordability policy vs financial-stability purpose"
    - "buffer build-up vs buffer release in stress"
    - "bank-focused tools vs non-bank / market-based finance risks"
    - "macroprudential policy vs monetary policy"
    - "macroprudential prevention vs resolution preparedness"

  shortest_maxim:
    "宏觀審慎第一眼不是『這家銀行大不大』；先問『哪一種風險正在整個系統共同累積、透過什麼傳染或週期機制放大、哪個法定工具真正可以對哪一群曝險啟動、何時又應解除』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-125.1 第一眼｜SYSTEMIC RISK SOURCE → TRANSMISSION → AUTHORITY → TOOL → SCOPE → CALIBRATION → RECIPROCITY → RELEASE → IMPACT

```text
SYSTEMIC RISK SOURCE
＝credit boom? property? leverage? liquidity? interconnectedness? common exposures?

TRANSMISSION
＝fire sale? run? margin calls? network contagion? credit crunch?

AUTHORITY
＝central bank? prudential authority? macroprudential council? ministry?

TOOL
＝CCyB? SyRB? G-SIB/O-SII buffer? LTV/DTI? sectoral measure?

SCOPE
＝all banks? exposures? sector? geography? borrower class?

CALIBRATION
＝rate / threshold / phase-in?

RECIPROCITY
＝cross-border exposures是否被other authority recognition？

RELEASE
＝何時解除／降低buffer以避免procyclical tightening？

IMPACT
＝credit supply / distribution / housing / competition effects?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-125.22 Evidence architecture

- designated authority / statutory mandate；
- buffer decision / notification；
- effective date；
- risk indicator set；
- credit / leverage / property / market data；
- exposure geography；
- G-SIB / O-SII designation；
- bucket / buffer notice；
- reciprocity decision；
- stress-test / scenario；
- network / concentration data；
- borrower-based measure text；
- release / reduction decision；
- impact assessment；
- judicial / administrative review route。

核心：

> **宏觀審慎不是把『系統性』當形容詞；必須把風險來源、傳遞機制、授權機關、工具、範圍與日期逐一接起來。**

Solvency II remains the EU prudential framework, with new delegated amendments published in February 2026 and a further updated regime taking effect from 30 January 2027. Capital, valuation, governance and group-solvency analysis therefore requires the applicable date and implementing layer.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Solvency II implementing/delegated acts**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/implementing-and-delegated-acts/solvency-2_en
  - Verified point: The Solvency II framework remains current; Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/269 was published in February 2026 and the framework continues to be amended.
- **European Commission — Q&A on Solvency II delegated regulation**
  - URL: https://finance.ec.europa.eu/news/questions-and-answers-solvency-ii-delegated-regulation-2025-10-29_en
  - Verified point: Directive (EU) 2025/2 updates Solvency II and the Commission states the new rules take effect on 30 January 2027.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-158-INSURANCE_PRUDENTIAL_SOLVENCY_CAPITAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保險審慎／清償能力與資本法"
  english_name: "Insurance Prudential / Solvency & Capital Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "保險人或insurance group在持續經營狀態下，如何估值未來insurance liabilities、建立technical provisions、配置eligible own funds、滿足risk-based capital／SCR／MCR或其他control levels、管理asset-liability mismatch、liquidity、reinsurance counterparty與group risks；IAIS ICS、Solvency II及各法域prudential rules在什麼scope與日期真正適用；何時只是supervisory standard，何時才取得本國可執行法位？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險人今天收取premium、未來可能數年甚至數十年後才給付。其危險不只是某一天現金不足，而是長尾liability估值錯誤、mortality／longevity／catastrophe／market／credit／lapse／operational等風險共同侵蝕資本。保險審慎法因此圍繞technical provisions、asset valuation、capital resources、solvency control levels、ORSA、investments、reinsurance、group supervision與supervisory intervention建立持續經營治理。它與銀行capital regulation相似但不能互換，也與 insurer 已進入 failure / resolution 的危機處理制度不同。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識undertaking／group、法域、supervisory regime、valuation date、business mix、technical provisions、own funds、capital requirement、internal model／standard formula、reinsurance、asset portfolio、ALM、liquidity與group structure；不要看到『Solvency II』『ICS』就把國際／區域標準直接套成當地單一數字。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "policyholder protection vs capital cost"
    - "market-consistent valuation vs long-term liability stability"
    - "risk sensitivity vs model complexity"
    - "standard formula comparability vs internal model specificity"
    - "capital adequacy vs liquidity adequacy"
    - "long-term investment capacity vs prudential conservatism"
    - "reinsurance risk transfer vs counterparty / basis risk"
    - "entity supervision vs group-wide risk"
    - "global comparability vs jurisdictional implementation"
    - "proportionality vs supervisory blind spots"
    - "going-concern supervision vs recovery / resolution"
    - "climate / catastrophe uncertainty vs measurable capital"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保險審慎法第一眼不是『公司資產大於負債』；先問『負債怎麼估、own funds是什麼品質、capital requirement按什麼風險算、資產負債期限是否匹配、再保險真的轉走多少風險、哪個法域哪個日期的prudential regime正在適用』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-134.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → VALUATION → TECHNICAL PROVISIONS → OWN FUNDS → CAPITAL REQUIREMENT → ALM → LIQUIDITY → REINSURANCE → ORSA → GROUP → SUPERVISION → FAILURE INTERFACE

```text
ENTITY
＝insurer? reinsurer? captive? group? IAIG?

VALUATION
＝assets / liabilities on what basis and date?

TECHNICAL PROVISIONS
＝best estimate? margin? reserves?

OWN FUNDS
＝eligible capital quality / tier?

CAPITAL REQUIREMENT
＝SCR? MCR? domestic RBC? ICS PCR?

ALM
＝duration / currency / guarantee mismatch?

LIQUIDITY
＝claims / surrender / collateral stress?

REINSURANCE
＝risk transferred? counterparty risk? recoverability?

ORSA
＝own risk / solvency assessment beyond formula?

GROUP
＝double gearing? intra-group? contagion? fungibility?

SUPERVISION
＝reporting / intervention / capital plan?

FAILURE INTERFACE
＝still going concern, or recovery / resolution now triggered?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-134.29 Evidence architecture

- licence / entity classification；
- current supervisory regime；
- solo / group perimeter；
- balance sheet / asset inventory；
- technical provision methodology；
- actuarial assumptions；
- own-funds composition；
- SCR / MCR / RBC / ICS calculation；
- standard formula / internal model approval；
- ALM report；
- liquidity plan；
- reinsurance programme；
- ORSA；
- stress / scenario analysis；
- group transactions；
- supervisory correspondence；
- capital restoration / remediation plan；
- applicable transition dates；
- IRRD / resolution status if triggered。

核心：

> **保險人有很多資產，不等於已證明有足夠可用資本；solvency ratio很高，也不等於流動性、group transferability與未來claim assumptions都沒有問題。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## G2-134.32 Positive Law Gate

個案前必查：

```text
JURISDICTION
ENTITY TYPE
SOLO / GROUP
REPORTING DATE
CURRENT PRUDENTIAL REGIME
TRANSITIONAL LAW
VALUATION BASIS
CAPITAL STANDARD
INTERNAL MODEL STATUS
REINSURANCE TREATMENT
LIQUIDITY REQUIREMENT
SUPERVISORY DECISION
RECOVERY / RESOLUTION TRIGGER
```

特別禁止：

```text
2027 RULE → 2026 FACT
IAIS STANDARD → DOMESTIC STATUTE
GROUP METRIC → ENTITY FACT
MODEL OUTPUT → OBSERVED LOSS
```

# DLM-043｜破產／無力清償法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:

  DOMAIN_NAME: "破產／無力清償法"

  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B15"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、D:組織／企業／市場"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留一般insolvency觸發、財產池、債權人集體處理與程序共同層；企業重整DLM-044及個人債務DLM-045分流。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-043-INSOLVENCY_BANKRUPTCY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "破產／無力清償法"
  english_name: "Insolvency / Bankruptcy Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當債務人的財產不足以滿足所有債權人時，法律如何把原本分散的執行競賽轉成集體程序，確認哪些財產屬於 estate、哪些債權成立與具何順位、哪些交易可被撤銷，並讓有限價值在透明、可預測與可審查的規則下被分配？"

  historical_reason:
    "在債務人仍有足夠財產時，個別強制執行可以讓每個債權人各自實現權利；一旦資產不足，先查封、資訊最多或權力最大的債權人會搶走全部價值，並可能摧毀一個整體出售或持續營運更值錢的企業。破產法因此建立 stay、estate、proof of claims、priority、avoidance、collective sale / liquidation 與 discharge 等制度，把個別競逐轉成共同處理。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "個別債權執行 vs 債權人集體公平"
    - "擔保權穩定 vs 集體程序價值"
    - "快速清算 vs 價值最大化"
    - "債權人自治 vs 法院／管理人控制"
    - "交易安定 vs 偏頗／詐害交易撤銷"
    - "舊契約權利 vs insolvency collective interest"
    - "債務人控制 vs independent administration"
    - "透明資訊 vs 商業秘密"
    - "國內資產控制 vs 跨境 main proceeding cooperation"
    - "債權回收 vs 社會／就業／公共政策"

  shortest_maxim:
    "破產法第一眼不是『誰先來要錢』；先問『債務人是否真的進入無力清償、哪些財產進 estate、哪些債權成立、誰有擔保／優先、哪些交易要追回、最後按什麼順位分』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 49.1 第一眼｜DISTRESS → COMMENCEMENT → STAY → ESTATE → CLAIMS → PRIORITY → AVOIDANCE → REALISATION → DISTRIBUTION → CLOSURE

```text
DISTRESS
＝只是現金流壓力？還是法律上的 insolvency？

COMMENCEMENT
＝誰可聲請？門檻？法院何時開始程序？

STAY
＝哪些個別訴訟／執行暫停？

ESTATE
＝哪些財產進入破產財團／estate？

CLAIMS
＝誰是債權人？債權多少？有無條件／未到期／爭議？

PRIORITY
＝擔保？程序費用？勞工？稅？普通？劣後？

AVOIDANCE
＝偏頗清償、詐害移轉、低價交易可否追回？

REALISATION
＝整體出售？分拆出售？繼續營業？

DISTRIBUTION
＝按什麼規則分配？

CLOSURE
＝何時結案？法人是否消滅？自然人是否免責？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 49.19 證據特徵

- financial statements；
- cash-flow；
- creditor ageing；
- bank；
- loan documents；
- security registrations；
- asset register；
- related-party ledger；
- transfers；
- valuations；
- proofs of claim；
- litigation；
- contracts；
- tax；
- payroll；
- insolvency petition；
- commencement order；
- trustee reports；
- distribution schedule；
- foreign proceeding orders。

核心：

> **破產案件的第一張圖不是「誰欠誰」，而是整個資產—債權—順位—時間流。**

8. proofs of claim；
9. set-off；
10. avoidance；
11. executory contracts；
12. insolvency representative；
13. creditor committee；
14. sale methods；
15. related parties；
16. employee claims；
17. tax claims；
18. group insolvency；
19. MLCBI enactment；
20. insolvency-related judgments；
21. closure / dissolution；
22. discharge where relevant。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔個人債務、保險危機與保戶保障的失敗後專門路徑。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-045-PERSONAL_INSOLVENCY_DEBT_ADJUSTMENT"
  chinese_name: "個人債務清理"
  english_name: "Personal Insolvency / Debt Adjustment"

  domain_core_question:
    "當自然人因消費、家庭、疾病、失業、小型營業或其他因素長期無法清償債務時，法律如何在基本生活、誠實債務人的 fresh start、債權人公平回收、扶養／公共義務與防止制度濫用之間安排還款、資產清理與免責？"

  historical_reason:
    "自然人不像公司可以清算後解散。若所有未清債務永久跟隨個人，利息、催收、扣薪與社會排除可能使其永遠無法回到正常經濟生活；但若免責過度容易，又會破壞信用與債權公平。個人 insolvency 因此發展出 debt counselling、payment plan、protected minimum income、asset liquidation、good-faith duties、exceptions to discharge 與 fresh start。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "債務履行 vs 人的基本生活"
    - "fresh start vs moral hazard"
    - "債權人回收 vs 長期社會排除"
    - "財產清算 vs 居住／職業工具保護"
    - "收入還款 vs 最低生活保障"
    - "誠實債務人 vs 詐欺／隱匿資產"
    - "一般債務免責 vs 扶養／刑罰／詐欺債務"
    - "快速程序 vs 完整債權審查"
    - "家庭共同財務 vs 個人責任"
    - "consumer debt vs small-business debt 混合"

  shortest_maxim:
    "個人債務清理第一眼不是『欠錢就該還』或『還不起就免掉』；先問『這個人為何無法清償、可支配資產／收入多少、基本生活要保留多少、哪些債務可調整、是否誠實揭露、何時才取得免責資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 51.1 第一眼｜ELIGIBILITY → DEBT MAP → ASSET / INCOME → ESSENTIAL NEEDS → PLAN / LIQUIDATION → GOOD FAITH → EXCLUSIONS → DISCHARGE → REHABILITATION

```text
ELIGIBILITY
＝consumer？natural person entrepreneur？有無門檻？

DEBT MAP
＝房貸？卡債？稅？扶養？學生貸款？營業債？保證？

ASSET / INCOME
＝財產、薪資、家庭支出、可支配所得？

ESSENTIAL NEEDS
＝住房、醫療、子女、職業工具需保留多少？

PLAN / LIQUIDATION
＝還款計畫？部分清算？協商？

GOOD FAITH
＝有無隱匿、假債、惡意移轉？

EXCLUSIONS
＝哪些債務不得免責？

DISCHARGE
＝何時終止未清償債務的強制實現？

REHABILITATION
＝信用／職業限制何時解除？
```

---

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "FSB_INSURANCE_RESOLUTION_GUIDANCE_2026_CURRENT__DOMESTIC_IMPLEMENTATION_AND_SCOPE_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The FSB finalised insurer recovery/resolution-planning scope guidance on 29 April 2026. It is an international policy framework designed to accommodate jurisdictional differences, not a directly applicable universal resolution statute.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Financial Stability Board — Insurer recovery/resolution planning guidance**
  - URL: https://www.fsb.org/2026/04/fsb-finalises-guidance-on-insurers-subject-to-recovery-and-resolution-planning/
  - Verified point: On 29 April 2026 the FSB finalised guidance for authorities on which insurers should be subject to recovery and resolution planning.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔個人債務、保險危機與保戶保障的失敗後專門路徑。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-160-INSURANCE_RECOVERY_RESOLUTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保險恢復／危機處理法"
  english_name: "Insurance Recovery / Resolution Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當insurance/reinsurance undertaking出現嚴重財務惡化、已不具viability或很可能失去viability，誰有權要求pre-emptive recovery planning、採取early intervention、啟動resolution、選擇portfolio transfer / run-off / restructuring / bridge / write-down-conversion / liquidation等工具；如何保護policyholders、維持critical functions、分配損失、處理cross-border group、reinsurance、funding與creditor safeguards？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險公司的失敗不同於一般企業破產。保戶可能持有數十年的life、health、annuity或liability cover；突然終止契約可能讓高風險保戶無法重新投保，亦可能放大市場與社會成本。另一方面，保險人也不能只因『保戶需要保護』而永久避免市場退出。現代insurance resolution因此形成一個獨立世界：在普通going-concern supervision失效後，如何有秩序地退出、轉移、重組或清算，同時讓損失順序、權利保障、公共利益與金融穩定保持可追溯。"

  first_move:
    "先判斷現在仍是going-concern prudential remediation，還是已進入recovery / resolution / insolvency；不要看到SCR下降就直接叫resolution，也不要等到普通破產程序開始才第一次思考保戶連續性。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "going-concern supervision vs resolution"
    - "policyholder continuity vs creditor hierarchy"
    - "private loss allocation vs public-interest intervention"
    - "early action vs property / due-process safeguards"
    - "portfolio transfer vs policyholder consent"
    - "run-off continuity vs rapid exit"
    - "group resolution vs legal-entity separateness"
    - "home authority vs host policyholder protection"
    - "resolution funding vs taxpayer exposure"
    - "reinsurance continuity vs counterparty rights"
    - "confidential planning vs market transparency"
    - "national insolvency law vs special insurance-resolution regime"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保險resolution第一眼不是『公司虧很多』；先問『它是否仍可恢復、誰依法取得resolution authority、trigger是否成立、哪一個工具可合法維持保單與critical functions、誰承擔損失、保戶與其他債權人的保障線在哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-141.1 第一眼｜VIABILITY → AUTHORITY → PLAN → TRIGGER → RESOLUTION OBJECTIVE → TOOL → LOSS ALLOCATION → POLICY CONTINUITY → FUNDING → SAFEGUARD → CROSS-BORDER → EXIT

```text
VIABILITY
＝still viable? likely non-viable? no reasonable prospect of return?

AUTHORITY
＝supervisor? resolution authority? court? receiver?

PLAN
＝recovery plan? resolution plan? ordinary business continuity plan?

TRIGGER
＝capital? liquidity? inability to pay claims? licence withdrawal? public-interest test?

OBJECTIVE
＝policyholder protection? critical functions? financial stability? public funds?

TOOL
＝portfolio transfer? run-off? restructuring? bridge? write-down? liquidation?

LOSS ALLOCATION
＝shareholders? subordinated creditors? policyholders? other creditors?

POLICY CONTINUITY
＝continue, transfer, modify, terminate, pay protected amount?

FUNDING
＝estate? resolution fund? guarantee scheme? industry levy? public backstop?

SAFEGUARD
＝NCWO? valuation? judicial review? creditor treatment?

CROSS-BORDER
＝group plan? home/host coordination? recognition of action?

EXIT
＝return to viability? transfer? run-off? liquidation? licence termination?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-141.17 Evidence architecture

- supervisory decision；
- licence / authorization status；
- solvency and liquidity data；
- recovery plan；
- resolution plan；
- valuation；
- trigger decision；
- policy portfolio data；
- creditor hierarchy；
- reinsurance agreements；
- guarantee-scheme eligibility；
- group structure；
- critical-functions assessment；
- court / authority orders；
- transfer / run-off documents；
- funding source；
- cross-border recognition material。

核心：

> **Resolution不是「保險公司快倒了」的敘事，而是一連串法定資格：誰認定失敗、何時取得特殊權力、為何普通程序不足、用哪個工具、誰承受損失、誰取得保障、如何退出。**

# DLM-162｜保戶保障／保險保證機制法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-162"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "保戶保障／保險保證機制法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "C"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FAILURE_RESOLUTION_PROTECTION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FAILURE_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-143｜DLM-162｜保戶保障／保險保證機制法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B54"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "A:政府／國家／公權力、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔個人債務、保險危機與保戶保障的失敗後專門路徑。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-162-POLICYHOLDER_PROTECTION_INSURANCE_GUARANTEE_SCHEMES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "保戶保障／保險保證機制法"
  english_name: "Policyholder Protection / Insurance Guarantee Schemes Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當insurer被認定impaired、insolvent、license withdrawn或進入resolution / liquidation時，哪些policyholder / beneficiary / claimant、哪些policy type與哪些amount受到insurance guarantee scheme / policyholder protection arrangement保障；何時trigger、採continuation / transfer / claim payment哪種功能、如何funding、cap/exclusion、subrogation、home-host allocation與cross-border coordination，且它如何與resolution estate和一般債權順位互動？"

  historical_reason:
    "保險契約可能在保險人失敗時暴露出一個特殊問題：保戶不是單純投資人，也可能無法立即用市場價格重新購得相同保障。各法域因此發展出guaranty association、insurance guarantee scheme、policyholder protection fund等不同backstop。但這些制度的trigger、產品coverage、上限、地域範圍、資金來源與是否繼續保單差異極大。若AI只因看到『保險公司倒閉』就說『政府會賠』，會直接製造錯誤權利期待。"

  first_move:
    "先找法域中是否真的存在適用的insurance guarantee / policyholder protection scheme，再查trigger、eligible claimant、covered product、cap、territorial rule、funding與interaction；不要從insurer failure直接推導保戶一定獲全額賠付。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "policyholder protection vs moral hazard"
    - "full continuity vs capped protection"
    - "industry funding vs cost pass-through"
    - "home-state responsibility vs host-state consumer expectation"
    - "life / health continuity vs non-life claim payment"
    - "speed of support vs insolvency proof / valuation"
    - "scheme payment vs estate recovery"
    - "national fragmentation vs cross-border insurance market"
    - "resolution support vs liquidation backstop"
    - "covered policyholder vs sophisticated / commercial policyholder"
    - "uniform minimum standard vs national flexibility"
    - "public confidence vs false guarantee perception"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保戶保障第一眼不是『保險公司倒了，所以基金賠』；先問『哪一個scheme、哪一個trigger、哪一類保單與保戶、保障多少、由誰出錢、先轉保單還是付現金、跨境案件誰負責、scheme付完後對estate有什麼權利』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-143.1 第一眼｜SCHEME → TRIGGER → ELIGIBLE PERSON → COVERED PRODUCT → TERRITORIAL RULE → BENEFIT / CAP → CONTINUITY → FUNDING → SUBROGATION → RESOLUTION INTERACTION → CROSS-BORDER

```text
SCHEME
＝statutory fund? guaranty association? compensation scheme?

TRIGGER
＝impairment? insolvency order? liquidation? licence withdrawal? resolution?

ELIGIBLE PERSON
＝consumer? beneficiary? third-party claimant? SME? commercial policyholder?

COVERED PRODUCT
＝life? health? annuity? motor? liability? property? reinsurance excluded?

TERRITORIAL RULE
＝home insurer? host branch? policyholder residence? risk location?

BENEFIT / CAP
＝full? percentage? monetary cap? deductible? excluded amount?

CONTINUITY
＝claim payment? policy transfer? reissue? continuation?

FUNDING
＝ex ante fund? ex post assessment? levy? borrowing?

SUBROGATION
＝scheme steps into claim against estate?

RESOLUTION INTERACTION
＝funds resolution tool? supports transfer? pays liquidation claims?

CROSS-BORDER
＝which scheme coordinates / pays?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-143.17 Evidence architecture

- insurer authorization / membership status；
- insolvency / impairment / resolution order；
- scheme statute；
- scheme rules；
- policy / claim type；
- policyholder / beneficiary status；
- residence / risk location；
- coverage cap；
- effective date；
- exclusions；
- transfer / continuation arrangement；
- funding decision；
- payment record；
- subrogation / estate claim；
- cross-border coordination material。

核心：

> **保戶保障不是『保險版存款保險』的口號，而是一張需要逐項驗證 scheme、trigger、person、product、territory、cap、funding 與 estate interaction 的資格表。**

# G2-145｜第五十四批綜合案例

```text
A跨境life insurer集團在三個法域銷售長期年金與醫療保單。

第一階段：
→ interest-rate shock與大量surrender造成liquidity壓力
→ SCR仍高於最低門檻，但快速下降
→ supervisor要求capital remediation與recovery options

第二階段：
→ 母公司再遭重大資產減損
→ 主管機關認為已likely non-viable，開始準備portfolio transfer / run-off方案
→ 部分保戶問「是不是保證基金會全額賠」

同時：
→ insurer有大型quota-share與cat XoL reinsurance
→ reinsurer主張部分claim未符合claims-cooperation clause
→ portfolio transfer是否帶走reinsurance產生爭議
→ 一個host State有IGS，一個只有特定life protection，另一個沒有同類scheme

公司又引用2026年EIOPA IRRD guidelines說：
→ 「IRRD已經全面生效，所以我們的resolution程序就是EU統一程序」
```

理火拆法：

```text
GOING-CONCERN CAPITAL / LIQUIDITY
→ DLM-158

RECOVERY / LIKELY NON-VIABLE / PORTFOLIO TRANSFER / RUN-OFF
→ DLM-160

QUOTA SHARE / CAT XOL / CLAIMS COOPERATION / REINSURANCE TRANSFER
→ DLM-161

POLICYHOLDER GUARANTEE ELIGIBILITY / CAP / TERRITORIAL SCHEME
→ DLM-162

UNDERLYING POLICY CLAIM
→ DLM-157
```

不能：

```text
SCR FALLING
→ RESOLUTION AUTOMATICALLY
```

不能：

```text
PORTFOLIO TRANSFER
→ REINSURANCE AUTOMATICALLY TRANSFERS
```

不能：

```text
ONE MEMBER STATE HAS IGS
→ EVERY EU POLICYHOLDER HAS SAME PROTECTION
```

更不能：

```text
EIOPA 2026 IRRD IMPLEMENTATION MATERIAL EXISTS
→ IRRD 2027 APPLICATION DATE DISAPPEARS
```

這個案例的價值在於：

> **同一場保險人危機，至少有「going-concern、resolution、reinsurance、guarantee scheme、underlying policy」五條不同法律鏈；危機越大，越不能把它們壓成一個『保險公司倒閉』故事。**

# G2-146｜第五十四批回歸測試

```yaml
REGRESSION_BATCH_54:

  T160_1_SCR_RESOLUTION:
    input: "SCR跌破某比例，所以resolution一定立即啟動。"
    pass: "capital stress先分DLM-158；resolution需exact statutory trigger / viability / authority / public-interest conditions。"

  T160_2_PLAN_FAILURE:
    input: "公司有resolution plan，所以主管機關已認定它會倒。"
    pass: "ex ante planning != failure finding。"

  T160_3_RECOVERY_RESOLUTION:
    input: "recovery plan就是resolution plan。"
    pass: "going-concern management recovery與special-authority resolution分離。"

  T160_4_IRRD_NOW:
    input: "IRRD已in force，所以2026-08-25已全面適用。"
    pass: "in force 2025-01-28; transposition 2027-01-29; application 2027-01-30。"

  T160_5_EIOPA_2026:
    input: "EIOPA 2026已發IRRD guidelines，所以今天已是operational resolution law。"
    pass: "implementation preparation/publication != 2027 application。"

  T160_6_BANK_COPY:
    input: "銀行和保險都有resolution，所以直接用DLM-103。"
    pass: "bank vs insurance liability / policy-continuity architecture分離；DLM-160獨立。"

  T160_7_POLICY_FIRST_PRIORITY:
    input: "保戶在全世界都是第一順位債權人。"
    pass: "priority is jurisdiction-specific insolvency/insurance law。"

  T160_8_RUNOFF:
    input: "run-off就是停止管理等保單自然消失。"
    pass: "run-off需要active servicing / claims / ALM / reinsurance / communication。"

  T161_1_DIRECT_POLICY:
    input: "原保險人買了reinsurance，所以保戶可直接找reinsurer。"
    pass: "direct insurance != reinsurance; direct claim needs cut-through / assumption / statutory basis。"

  T161_2_PAID_RECOVERY:
    input: "cedant已賠保戶，所以reinsurer一定要賠。"
    pass: "查attachment / coverage / retention / aggregation / exclusions / claims clauses。"

  T161_3_ONE_EVENT:
    input: "一場颱風就是一個reinsurance occurrence。"
    pass: "event/occurrence/aggregation由exact wording與law決定。"

  T161_4_FOLLOW_SETTLEMENT:
    input: "follow the settlements＝reinsurer不能爭任何coverage。"
    pass: "clause scope與governing law控制；通常不是unlimited obligation。"

  T161_5_VALID_CREDIT:
    input: "reinsurance contract有效，所以監理一定給100%capital credit。"
    pass: "private validity != prudential recognition / credit。"

  T161_6_RECIPROCAL:
    input: "US reciprocal jurisdiction制度已經取消所有reinsurance collateral。"
    pass: "eligibility / agreement / timing / state law / statutory conditions仍需查。"

  T161_7_EIOPA_CONSULT:
    input: "EIOPA proportional reinsurance consultation 7月結束，所以8月已是final guidance。"
    pass: "consultation closed != final adopted/applicable guidance。"

  T161_8_RETROCESSION:
    input: "retrocessionaire不賠，所以reinsurer也不用賠cedant。"
    pass: "separate contracts; no automatic discharge。"

  T162_1_FULL_GUARANTEE:
    input: "保險公司倒閉，保證基金會全額賠所有人。"
    pass: "verify scheme / trigger / person / product / cap / territory / exclusions。"

  T162_2_EU_HARMONISED:
    input: "EU已有統一insurance guarantee scheme。"
    pass: "2026-08-25仍是national patchwork；minimum common standards仍在technical-advice process。"

  T162_3_FINAL_ADVICE:
    input: "EIOPA IGS consultation已結束，所以final advice已提交。"
    pass: "deadline 2026-08-31; current date 2026-08-25，不能提前顯影。"

  T162_4_PRIORITY:
    input: "保戶有優先受償權，所以一定受IGS保障。"
    pass: "estate priority != guarantee coverage。"

  T162_5_SCHEME_TRIGGER:
    input: "insurer solvency下降，所以IGS開始付款。"
    pass: "financial stress != scheme trigger。"

  T162_6_DGS_COPY:
    input: "IGS就是把銀行存保規則換成保險名稱。"
    pass: "insurance liabilities / product continuity / claims structure different。"

  T162_7_NAIC_MODEL:
    input: "NAIC guaranty model act就是全美同一部聯邦法。"
    pass: "model framework != identical enacted state law。"

  T162_8_TAXPAYER:
    input: "guarantee fund一定由政府稅收支付。"
    pass: "funding may be industry ex ante/ex post; verify statute。"

  T54_MULTI_AXIS:
    input: "跨境life insurer快倒、reinsurer拒絕部分recoveries、不同國家保戶要求保障。"
    pass: "至少分DLM-158 / 160 / 161 / 162 / 157，不得用單一危機標籤閉合。"

  T54_DEDUP:
    input: "DLM-103既然有bank resolution，就刪掉DLM-160。"
    pass: "bank resolution != insurance resolution；兩者保留獨立第一眼。"
```

```yaml
    - "https://www.eiopa.europa.eu/eiopa-seeks-feedback-draft-technical-advice-regarding-minimum-common-standards-insurance-guarantee-2026-05-05_en"
    - "https://www.eiopa.europa.eu/publications/study-effect-harmonised-igs-standards-consumer-perceptions-and-choice_en"
    - "https://content.naic.org/sites/default/files/inline-files/MDL-520_0.pdf"
```

### SOURCE_BOUNDARY

- V0.54 三張卡是 de-dup 後取得獨立資格的新 domain，不回頭靜默改 V0.38 或 V0.53。
- DLM-160 與 DLM-158 的核心邊界是 `GOING-CONCERN PRUDENTIAL != NON-VIABLE / RESOLUTION`。capital/liquidity stress可能先只進DLM-158；resolution trigger須另有法源與decision。
- DLM-160 與 DLM-103 的核心邊界是 `BANK RESOLUTION != INSURANCE RESOLUTION`。兩者可能共享FSB resolution principles，但負債、policy continuity、run-off、portfolio transfer與policyholder-protection architecture不同。
- IAIS ICP 12 是 global supervisory standard，不是各法域直接適用的保險破產法；FSB Key Attributes亦是國際resolution標準框架，不自行創造national power。
- Directive (EU) 2025/1 IRRD 已於2025-01-28生效，但member-state transposition deadline為2027-01-29，rules apply from 2027-01-30。`IN FORCE != APPLICABLE TODAY`。
- EIOPA 2026 IRRD guidelines / ITS / technical work證明implementation build-out正在進行，但截至2026-08-25不能用來抹去2027-01-30 application date。
- DLM-161 保留 direct insurance 與 reinsurance 的 privity / contract boundary。任何 cut-through、assumption、direct action、insolvency treatment均須現查 exact law / contract。
- IAIS ICP 13是reinsurance / other risk transfer的supervisory standard，不是private contract code。
- EIOPA 2026 proportional-reinsurance consultation已於2026-07-17結束，但本輪未取得final adopted guidance證據，因此只標 `CONSULTATION CLOSED / FINAL STATUS NOT PROVEN`。
- NAIC Credit for Reinsurance models與Covered Agreements建立US reciprocal-jurisdiction framework；model law / model regulation不等於每州文字完全一致，個案仍需查state enactment與current eligibility。
- DLM-162 不宣稱存在全球或EU統一insurance guarantee scheme。EIOPA 2026資料明確描述EU仍是national schemes patchwork，甚至部分會員國缺少同類scheme。
- EIOPA對EU minimum common IGS standards的2026 consultation於2026-06-26結束；Commission要求final technical advice by 2026-08-31。本版日期2026-08-25，因此 `FINAL ADVICE DELIVERED` 保持 NOT_PROVEN / FUTURE DEADLINE。
- NAIC Life and Health Insurance Guaranty Association Model Act只作state-based US method anchor，不得升格為單一聯邦保戶保證法。
- 所有resolution trigger、creditor hierarchy、reinsurance attachment、aggregation、collateral、guarantee-scheme cap、coverage與cross-border allocation均必須依current jurisdiction / contract / date現查。

# G2-148｜V0.55 增量聲明

V0.55 先對第一卷 `DLM-028｜刑法` 做 specialization / de-dup audit。

本輪不把刑法依「罪名數量」切碎，而只拆出三個已具備：

```text
獨立第一眼
+
獨立構成要件結構
+
獨立證據型態
+
獨立抗辯／失敗模式
+
可辨識的專門法源生態
```

的新 domain：

```text
DLM-163｜殺人／死亡結果與重大人身傷害法
DLM-164｜性犯罪／同意法
DLM-165｜竊盜／詐欺與財產犯罪法
```

DLM-028 不廢止，繼續保留為一般刑法母卡，負責：

- 罪刑法定；
- act / omission；
- mens rea；
- causation；
- justification / excuse；
- attempt；
- participation；
- corporate criminal liability；
- concurrence / general-part doctrine。

本輪新增卡只在特定犯罪群已形成獨立「第一眼」時接手。

最重要的路由鎖：

```text
GENERAL CRIMINAL LAW
!= HOMICIDE / SERIOUS BODILY HARM
!= SEXUAL OFFENCES / CONSENT
!= THEFT / FRAUD / PROPERTY CRIME
```

以及：

```text
HARM
!= CRIMINAL OFFENCE

DEATH
!= MURDER

SEXUAL ACT
!= CONSENT

PROPERTY LOSS
!= THEFT
!= FRAUD
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "COMPANY_LAW_CURRENT__EU_INC_2026_IS_PROPOSAL_NOT_OPERATIVE_CODE__BENEFICIAL_OWNERSHIP_AND_DOMESTIC_FORM_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

EU company law remains fragmented across existing EU instruments and national forms. The March 2026 EU Inc. initiative is explicitly a proposal for a new harmonised regime, so it must not be treated as already-operative company law. Formation, legal personality, capital, representation and beneficial-ownership rules remain form/jurisdiction specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Company law and corporate governance**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance_en
  - Verified point: The Commission's current page records a June 2026 report on review of the Shareholder Rights Directive and continuing EU company-law activity.
- **European Commission — EU Inc. proposal**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance/eu-inc-new-harmonised-corporate-legal-regime_en
  - Verified point: EU Inc. was presented on 18 March 2026 as a proposal; it is not an already-operative harmonised company-law code.
- **FATF — The FATF Recommendations**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfrecommendations/Fatf-recommendations.html
  - Verified point: The FATF Recommendations remain current and reflect 2025 revisions emphasizing proportionality/simplified measures and targeted, risk-based NPO controls.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留公司人格、成立、資本、機關、代表、有限責任與entity-level共同層；139治理／董事義務、140控制權交易、141股東救濟、148公開收購取得專門優先。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-007-COMPANY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公司法"
  english_name: "Company / Corporate Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當多人把資本、勞務與決策權放進一個具有獨立法律人格的組織後，誰可以代表公司、誰控制公司、誰對誰負責、風險如何在股東、董事、經理人、債權人與公司本身之間配置？"

  historical_reason:
    "大型商業活動需要持續存在的資產池、可轉讓投資、集中管理與風險隔離。公司制度因此把企業從自然人集合轉化成獨立法律主體，同時必須處理所有權與控制分離、有限責任帶來的債權人風險、控制股東與少數股東衝突，以及董事經理人的代理問題。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "獨立法人格 vs 實際控制者"
    - "有限責任 vs 債權人保護"
    - "股東所有權利益 vs 董事會經營判斷"
    - "多數決 vs 少數股東保護"
    - "控制股東利益 vs 公司／全體股東利益"
    - "管理效率 vs 問責與監督"
    - "企業集團經濟一體性 vs 各公司法人格分離"
    - "短期報酬 vs 公司長期韌性與永續"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公司法第一眼不是『老闆做了什麼』；先問『是哪一個法人、哪一個機關、哪一種權限、誰對誰負責』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 13.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → ROLE → POWER → DUTY

公司案件最容易被日常語言污染。

人們會說：

> 「老闆的公司。」

> 「母公司叫子公司做的。」

> 「董事長就是公司。」

> 「股東出錢，所以公司財產就是股東的。」

公司法第一眼必須拆成：

```text
ENTITY
＝法律上是哪一個公司／法人？

ROLE
＝股東？董事？董事長？經理人？控制股東？員工？債權人？

POWER
＝這個角色依法或依章程有什麼決策／代表／監督權？

DUTY
＝他對公司、股東、債權人或其他主體負什麼義務？
```

核心：

> **經濟上的「控制」與法律上的「權限／責任」不能直接畫等號。**

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 13.9 證據特徵

公司案件的重要材料通常先回答「誰有權做這個決定」：

- 公司登記；
- 章程；
- 股東名簿；
- 股東協議；
- 董事／股東會議事錄；
- 決議；
- 授權書；
- 簽章權限；
- 關係人資料；
- 股權結構圖；
- 最終受益人／控制資料；
- 財務報表；
- 銀行流水；
- 公司與股東資產往來；
- 關係企業交易；
- 董事利益揭露；
- 電子郵件與內部指示；
- 併購／投資文件。

核心：

> **公司法證據常先證明治理資格，再證明交易結果。**

## 13.11 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依特定法域現查：

1. 公司種類與法人格；
2. 設立、資本與出資制度；
3. 股東會／董事會權限；
4. 公司代表權；
5. 董事與經理人義務；
6. 關係人交易；
7. 少數股東救濟；
8. 股東代表訴訟；
9. 公司財務危機下董事／股東責任；
10. 揭穿法人面紗／穿透責任；
11. 企業集團與控制股東責任；
12. 股份轉讓、增資、減資、分配；
13. 併購、控制權交易；
14. 解散、清算與破產的接口。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

宗教自由是E/A交叉，但本卡的獨立組織鏈是community→legal personality→internal autonomy→property/funding→public interface。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-076-RELIGIOUS_ORGANIZATIONS_FREEDOM_OF_RELIGION_BELIEF_INSTITUTIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "宗教團體法"
  english_name: "Religious Organizations / Freedom of Religion & Belief Institutions Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "個人與群體如何共同持有、改變、拒絕或實踐 religion / belief；宗教共同體如何建立 worship、leadership、legal personality、property、education、charity、employment 與 internal discipline；國家何時可基於權利、安全、兒少、刑事或一般法律介入，而不能把行政登記、主流教義或政治忠誠變成信仰資格審查？"

  historical_reason:
    "religion / belief 同時是最內在的 conscience 與高度組織化的社會制度。若只保護私人思想，宗教自由會失去共同 worship、teaching、places、leaders、funding 與 institutions；若把 institutional autonomy 無限化，又可能讓財產、勞動、兒少、暴力或詐欺失去一般法律控制。宗教團體法因此必須把 forum internum、manifestation、community autonomy、legal personality、property / finance、public service 與 permissible limitation 分層。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "forum internum vs State interference"
    - "religious manifestation vs rights / public safety"
    - "community autonomy vs individual member rights"
    - "doctrinal independence vs secular legal accountability"
    - "registration / legal personality vs freedom without authorization"
    - "religious property vs public regulation"
    - "clergy selection vs employment / equality law"
    - "religious education vs child rights"
    - "charitable mission vs anti-discrimination"
    - "religious confidentiality vs criminal / safeguarding duties"
    - "State neutrality vs accommodation"
    - "majority tradition vs minority / new / non-religious beliefs"

  shortest_maxim:
    "宗教團體法第一眼不是『這是不是正統宗教』；先問『哪一個人／群體主張哪一種 belief / manifestation、是否需要 legal personality、國家現在限制的是內在信念、共同實踐、組織運作、財產還是一般違法行為』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 82.1 第一眼｜BELIEF / CONSCIENCE → MANIFESTATION → COMMUNITY → LEGAL PERSONALITY → INTERNAL AUTONOMY → PROPERTY / FUNDING → PUBLIC INTERFACE → LIMITATION → REVIEW

```text
BELIEF / CONSCIENCE
＝theistic？non-theistic？atheistic？no belief？conversion？

MANIFESTATION
＝worship？observance？practice？teaching？dress？diet？holiday？

COMMUNITY
＝church？mosque？temple？house group？informal fellowship？belief association？

LEGAL PERSONALITY
＝registration？association？corporation？foundation？trust？

INTERNAL AUTONOMY
＝doctrine？leader selection？membership？discipline？

PROPERTY / FUNDING
＝worship place？donation？endowment？foreign contribution？

PUBLIC INTERFACE
＝school？hospital？charity？employment？public service？

LIMITATION
＝law？legitimate aim？necessity？proportionality？non-discrimination？

REVIEW
＝administrative appeal？court？constitutional / human-rights route？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 82.27 證據特徵

- constitution / religious-freedom law；
- registration；
- statutes / canons / internal rules；
- leadership appointment；
- membership；
- property title / trust；
- donations；
- bank / accounting；

- service contracts；

- employment role description；
- disciplinary file；
- worship-place permit；
- State decision / reasons；
- discrimination evidence；
- safeguarding / crime evidence；

核心：

> **宗教團體案件最重要的不是法院回答「誰信得比較對」，而是辨認國家或組織正在用哪一種權力，以及這個權力是否真的需要決定教義才能處理法律關係。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml

  verification_date: "2026-08-25"

  global_closure: false
```

FATF's current NPO approach is targeted, proportionate and risk-based, and expressly warns against suppressing legitimate civil-society activity through over-application. Legal personality, registration, foreign funding, governance, reporting and dissolution remain domestic-law questions.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **FATF — Non-profit organisations**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/topics/non-profit-organisations.html
  - Verified point: FATF emphasizes targeted and proportionate risk-based measures for the subset of NPOs exposed to terrorist-financing abuse, with 2025 procedures addressing unintended consequences.
- **FATF — The FATF Recommendations**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfrecommendations/Fatf-recommendations.html
  - Verified point: The FATF Recommendations remain current and reflect 2025 revisions emphasizing proportionality/simplified measures and targeted, risk-based NPO controls.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留association/NGO/CSO的組織形式、會員治理、資金、申報、跨境活動與解散共同層；078慈善／基金會承擔purpose-bound asset vehicle專門化。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-077-NONPROFIT_CIVIL_SOCIETY_ORGANIZATIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "非營利組織／公民社會組織法"
  english_name: "Nonprofit / Civil Society Organizations Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "人們如何為共同 lawful purpose 建立 association / NGO / civil-society organization；未登記團體與法人組織各取得什麼地位；會員、董事、管理者如何治理、籌資、聘僱、倡議、跨境合作、申報與解散；國家如何以透明、財務、AML、tax 等規則監督，而不把 registration、foreign funding 或 public criticism 變成控制公民社會的工具？"

  historical_reason:
    "公民社會組織填補國家與市場之間的集體行動空間：人權、環境、社區、文化、教育、專業、救援、倡議都需要長期組織。法人制度讓團體可以持有財產、僱人、簽約與持續存在，但也產生治理、資金、責任與濫用風險。因此 NPO law 既保障 association autonomy 與 funding access，也建立 governance、accountability、financial reporting、AML / TF-risk measures、dissolution safeguards 與 public-benefit boundaries。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "freedom of association vs State registration / oversight"
    - "organizational autonomy vs member / donor accountability"
    - "foreign funding access vs foreign-influence concerns"
    - "advocacy freedom vs political / election regulation"
    - "not-for-profit purpose vs lawful revenue generation"
    - "volunteerism vs labour protection"
    - "financial transparency vs privacy / security"
    - "AML / CFT controls vs civil-society space"
    - "self-regulation vs public enforcement"
    - "board discretion vs mission lock"
    - "cross-border cooperation vs national regulation"
    - "dissolution power vs freedom of association"

  shortest_maxim:
    "非營利組織法第一眼不是『它不賺錢』；先問『它是什麼法律形式、為什麼目的成立、誰是會員／董事、誰能控制資產、錢從哪裡來、可否分配 surplus、誰監督、若違規誰有權修正或解散』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 83.1 第一眼｜PURPOSE → ORGANIZATIONAL FORM → FORMATION → MEMBERSHIP / BOARD → ASSET / FUNDING → ACTIVITY → ACCOUNTABILITY → STATE OVERSIGHT → DISSOLUTION

```text
PURPOSE
＝human rights？community？education？religion？culture？service？advocacy？

FORM
＝unincorporated association？incorporated association？foundation？company limited by guarantee？trust？

FORMATION
＝notification？registration？approval？

MEMBERSHIP / BOARD
＝誰是最高權力？如何選／罷免？

ASSET / FUNDING
＝dues？donations？grants？foreign funding？service revenue？

ACTIVITY
＝advocacy？service？commercial activity？election activity？

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝members？donors？beneficiaries？regulator？public？

STATE OVERSIGHT
＝filing？audit？AML？tax？public-benefit supervision？

DISSOLUTION
＝voluntary？court？regulator？asset destination？
```

- founding instrument；

核心：

> **NPO 案件不能因組織說自己「公益」就放掉治理，也不能因政府說「風險」就放掉結社自由；兩邊都必須把目的、權力、資金與證據交出來。**

Current FATF standards provide risk-based NPO and beneficial-ownership baselines, but charity/foundation form, dedicated-asset rules, public-benefit status, tax treatment, donor restrictions and supervisory powers remain jurisdiction-specific.

  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfrecommendations/Fatf-recommendations.html

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

以dedicated charitable/public-benefit assets、restricted funds/endowment、board/trustees、private benefit、purpose modification與dissolution形成獨立資產／組織結構。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-078-CHARITY_FOUNDATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "慈善／基金會法"
  english_name: "Charity / Foundation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "私人財產如何被有效捐入一個 charitable / public-benefit purpose；association、foundation、charitable trust 或其他 vehicle 中，donor、board / trustees、beneficiaries、regulator 與 public 各有何地位；restricted funds、endowment、grantmaking、fundraising、private benefit、tax exemption、related-party transactions、purpose modification 與 dissolution 如何確保資產不被目的標籤或管理者私人化？"

  historical_reason:
    "慈善制度允許私人把財產從一般私人消費／繼承邏輯中抽離，投入教育、救濟、宗教、健康、文化或其他公共利益。這會創造跨世代資產池與高度信任，但受益者往往無法像公司股東一樣監督，donor 也可能離場或死亡。因此 charity / foundation law 以 purpose lock、fiduciary governance、restricted funds、public-benefit tests、regulatory oversight、fundraising accountability、asset-lock 與 purpose modification 補上『沒有所有者監督者』的治理缺口。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "donor intent vs evolving public need"
    - "board discretion vs purpose lock"
    - "public benefit vs private benefit"
    - "endowment preservation vs current spending"
    - "fundraising efficiency vs donor protection"
    - "charitable autonomy vs State oversight"
    - "tax subsidy vs viewpoint neutrality"
    - "restricted gifts vs organizational flexibility"
    - "religious charity autonomy vs service-user rights"
    - "grantmaking discretion vs discrimination / corruption"
    - "family foundation control vs private benefit"
    - "perpetuity vs cy-près / purpose modification"

  shortest_maxim:
    "慈善／基金會法第一眼不是『錢是捐來做好事』；先問『誰把什麼財產交給哪個 legal vehicle、purpose 是什麼、是否 restricted / endowed、誰管理、誰能受益、誰監督、原目的不能做時誰有權改』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 84.1 第一眼｜VEHICLE → PURPOSE → DONOR / FOUNDER → ASSET → RESTRICTION → GOVERNING BODY → BENEFICIARY / PUBLIC BENEFIT → EXPENDITURE → OVERSIGHT → MODIFICATION / DISSOLUTION

```text
VEHICLE
＝foundation？charitable trust？association？religious charity？company?

PURPOSE
＝relief？education？health？religion？culture？environment？other public benefit？

DONOR / FOUNDER
＝living donor？estate？corporation？public grant？

ASSET
＝cash？land？shares？endowment？art？

RESTRICTION
＝general gift？restricted purpose？capital/endowment restriction？

GOVERNING BODY
＝trustees？board？members？

BENEFICIARY
＝defined class？general public？community？

EXPENDITURE
＝grants？services？salary？investment？related-party payment？

OVERSIGHT
＝regulator？court？tax authority？auditor？

MODIFICATION
＝amendment？cy-près？merger？dissolution？
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "NO_GLOBAL_PROFESSIONAL_ETHICS_CODE__PROFESSION_AND_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__AI_CROSS_RULES_CURRENT_WHEN_USED"

  global_closure: false
```

Professional ethics and discipline remain profession- and jurisdiction-specific; this pass does not substitute a global code. Where AI-generated or manipulated content is part of professional activity in the EU, Article 50 AI transparency rules now provide an additional cross-layer from 2 August 2026, without replacing the profession's own duties.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/guidelines-ai-transparency-obligations
  - Verified point: Article 50 transparency obligations apply from 2 August 2026, including transparency duties concerning certain AI-generated/deepfake content.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

專業者可為個人或組織，但法律資格來自professional role、competence、scope、conflict、independence、confidentiality與discipline；D規範其作為制度化服務行動者。

```yaml
  english_name: "Professional Responsibility / Professional Ethics Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當法律授予律師、會計師、醫師、工程師、建築師與其他專業者資格、資訊優勢、保密關係與社會信任時，專業者對 client、第三人、法院、市場、監管者與公共利益各負什麼 duty；competence、scope、independence、conflict、confidentiality、informed decision、documentation、discipline、civil liability 與 professional autonomy 如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "專業服務的核心問題是高度資訊不對稱：客戶通常無法即時判斷律師訴訟策略、醫療診斷、會計判斷或工程設計是否合理，因此社會透過 licensing、education、ethics、independence、confidentiality、discipline 與 liability 把可信度外部制度化。專業責任法不是讓專業者成為不可質疑權威，而是規定一個人憑專業身份取得他人信任後，必須如何留下 competence、method、conflict management、communication 與 accountability。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "professional autonomy vs client autonomy"
    - "loyalty vs law / public duty"
    - "confidentiality vs disclosure obligations"
    - "independence vs client / employer pressure"
    - "expert judgment vs hindsight review"
    - "specialization vs scope of competence"
    - "professional self-regulation vs public accountability"
    - "discipline vs professional independence"
    - "client secrecy vs third-party harm"
    - "commercial fee incentives vs fiduciary duty"
    - "innovation / AI use vs competence / supervision"
    - "ethical code vs civil / criminal law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "專業責任法第一眼不是『他是不是專家』；先問『他以什麼專業身份承接什麼任務、對誰負哪一種 duty、當時合理專業方法是什麼、有哪些 conflict／confidentiality／independence 問題、他的判斷如何被紀錄與說明』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 78.1 第一眼｜PROFESSION → LICENCE / SCOPE → CLIENT / BENEFICIARY → DUTY → STANDARD → INFORMATION → CONFLICT → JUDGMENT → COMMUNICATION → HARM → ACCOUNTABILITY

```text
PROFESSION
＝lawyer？accountant？doctor？engineer？architect？other licensed professional？

LICENCE / SCOPE
＝是否有執照？可以做什麼？

CLIENT / BENEFICIARY
＝誰委任？誰合理依賴？

DUTY
＝loyalty？care？confidentiality？independence？candour？

STANDARD
＝statute？professional rules？standard of care？contract？

INFORMATION
＝掌握哪些 privileged / confidential / technical facts？

CONFLICT
＝自己、另一客戶、雇主、第三人？

JUDGMENT
＝當時有哪些 options / uncertainty？

COMMUNICATION
＝有沒有解釋 risk / limitation / alternatives？

HARM
＝什麼損害？與服務錯誤有何 causation？

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝discipline？civil？criminal？contract？regulatory？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 78.34 證據特徵

- licence / registration；
- engagement / retainer；
- scope；
- conflict checks；
- consent；
- advice / opinion；
- research；
- calculations；
- correspondence；
- professional guidelines；
- supervision；
- expert consultation；
- file notes；
- billing；
- confidentiality / disclosure；
- AI tool use；
- disciplinary complaint；
- peer review；
- regulator inspection；
- professional insurance；
- termination / file transfer。

核心：

> **專業責任案不能因「他是專家」就相信，也不能因「結果不好」就判錯；要回到當時任務、資訊、方法、標準、限制、利益衝突、溝通與紀錄，重建一個合理專業者當時究竟有資格做到哪裡。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "CORPORATE_GOVERNANCE_FRAMEWORK_DYNAMIC__CSDDD_2026_AMENDED__DOMESTIC_DIRECTOR_DUTY_AND_LISTING_RULE_CHECK_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

Corporate-governance law remains a mixture of company law, listing rules and sector standards. EU sustainability due-diligence/reporting rules were amended in 2026 by Directive (EU) 2026/470, while directors' fiduciary/oversight duties continue to depend on applicable company law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Company law and corporate governance**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance_en
  - Verified point: The Commission's current page records a June 2026 report on review of the Shareholder Rights Directive and continuing EU company-law activity.
- **European Commission — Simplification / CSDDD and CSRD**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/law/law-making-process/better-regulation/simplification-implementation-and-enforcement/simplification_en
  - Verified point: Directive (EU) 2026/470 simplifying reporting/due-diligence requirements was adopted 24 February 2026 and entered into force 16 March 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

專門處理治理機關、董事／經理／控制者義務、衝突、監督與審查標準。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-139-CORPORATE_GOVERNANCE_DIRECTORS_DUTIES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公司治理／董事義務法"
  english_name: "Corporate Governance / Directors' Duties Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "公司內部哪個機關依法取得策略、監督、任命、代表或經營權；董事、經理人、控制股東或其他治理者對公司、股東或其他法定受益主體負什麼忠實、注意、善管、利益衝突、公司機會、資訊與監督義務；在具體決策脈絡中又由什麼審查標準、程序與救濟判斷責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "公司制度把資產所有、風險承擔與日常決策分離，使大量資本能被集中管理；但授權董事會與管理層意味著資訊與控制也被集中。公司治理法因此不只問『誰是老闆』，而是建立機關分工、監督、資訊、忠實與責任制度，使取得他人資本管理權的人不能只以經營權替自身決策封聖。"

  first_move:
    "先做ENTITY → GOVERNANCE BODY → DECISION → DUTY HOLDER → BENEFICIARY → CONFLICT → PROCESS → REVIEW STANDARD地圖；不要從公司虧損、股價下跌或董事『看起來偏心』直接跳到違反董事義務。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "managerial authority vs accountability"
    - "business risk-taking vs hindsight liability"
    - "board discretion vs shareholder intervention"
    - "loyalty vs self-interest / conflicts"
    - "information asymmetry vs informed decision-making"
    - "controlling influence vs entity-level loyalty"
    - "short-term returns vs long-term corporate interest"
    - "shareholder primacy formulations vs stakeholder / statutory interests"
    - "independence formality vs actual dependence"
    - "confidential board process vs litigation disclosure"
    - "group strategy vs duties to each legal entity"
    - "private ordering vs mandatory governance law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "董事義務第一眼不是『決策結果好不好』；先問『誰替哪一個法人作了哪一個決策、對誰負哪一種義務、是否有利益衝突、資訊與程序是否合格、該法域用什麼審查標準』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-74.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → ORGAN → AUTHORITY → DUTY HOLDER → BENEFICIARY → DECISION CONTEXT → CONFLICT → INFORMATION / PROCESS → STANDARD OF REVIEW → CAUSATION / REMEDY

```text
ENTITY
＝which corporation / subsidiary / group entity?

ORGAN
＝board? supervisory board? management board? officer? committee?

AUTHORITY
＝statute? charter? bylaws? delegation? shareholder resolution?

DUTY HOLDER
＝director? officer? shadow / de facto director? controller where recognized?

BENEFICIARY
＝company? shareholders as a whole? creditors in defined distress contexts? stakeholders under statute?

DECISION CONTEXT
＝ordinary business? conflicted transaction? takeover response? dividends? insolvency proximity? compliance oversight?

CONFLICT
＝personal interest? related party? controller influence? corporate opportunity?

PROCESS
＝notice? materials? deliberation? recusal? committee? expert advice? record?

REVIEW STANDARD
＝business judgment? duty of care? loyalty? fairness? enhanced scrutiny? statutory safe harbour?

REMEDY
＝damages? injunction? rescission? account? disqualification? derivative relief?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-74.17 Evidence architecture

核心材料：

- charter / bylaws；
- board and committee minutes；
- board packs；
- emails / messages；
- valuation and fairness materials；
- conflict disclosures；
- director independence data；
- related-party records；
- expert reports；
- internal controls / compliance reporting；
- delegation instruments；
- remuneration arrangements；
- controller communications；
- corporate opportunity records；
- contemporaneous decision rationale。

最重要：

> **董事義務案往往不是先證明「決策錯」，而是先重建誰決定、知道什麼、為何決定、是否有利益衝突與哪一個審查標準取得資格。**

個案必查：

1. company type / governing law；

3. allocation of board / shareholder powers；
4. director / officer definitions；
5. duty beneficiaries；
6. duty of care / loyalty / good faith / statutory duties；
7. business judgment or analogous standards；
8. controller duties；
9. conflict / related-party safe harbours；
10. independence rules；
11. corporate opportunity doctrine；
12. oversight duties；
13. distress / creditor-interest rules；
14. exculpation / indemnification / D&O insurance；
15. limitation periods；
16. standing and derivative/direct classification；
17. remedies。

The EU company-law framework remains current and the Commission records a June 2026 report reviewing the Shareholder Rights Directive. Derivative standing, oppression/unfair-prejudice, appraisal/buyout and meeting remedies remain dependent on the company's governing law and listing regime.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Company law and corporate governance**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance_en
  - Verified point: The Commission's current page records a June 2026 report on review of the Shareholder Rights Directive and continuing EU company-law activity.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

以股東／member status、direct/derivative、minority protection、standing與corporate remedy形成專門治理救濟鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-141-SHAREHOLDER_RIGHTS_MINORITY_REMEDIES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "股東權利／少數股東救濟法"
  english_name: "Shareholder Rights / Minority Shareholder Remedies Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某人基於何種shareholder / member身份取得哪些表決、資訊、提案、召集、選任、分配、查閱與重大交易參與權；當公司、董事、控制股東或多數股東侵害公司或股東利益時，請求究竟屬direct還是derivative、誰有standing、是否需先作demand／前置程序、又可取得何種撤銷、 injunction、損害、代表訴訟、oppression、buy-out、appraisal或其他救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "公司多數決使組織得以有效行動，但資訊、控制與訴訟資源通常集中在董事會與控制股東。若少數股東只能接受多數結果，控制權可能被用於掏空、稀釋、封鎖資訊或迫使退出；反之，若任何小股東都能無門檻阻止公司決策，公司也無法治理。因此股東權利法的核心不是讓少數永遠否決，而是建立權利、程序、standing與救濟門檻，使公司自治與可追責性並存。"

  first_move:
    "先問STATUS → RIGHT SOURCE → RIGHT HOLDER → CORPORATE OR PERSONAL HARM → DIRECT / DERIVATIVE → PRECONDITIONS → REMEDY；不要從『股東吃虧』直接跳到個人損害賠償，也不要從『多數通過』直接宣布無救濟。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "majority rule vs minority protection"
    - "board authority vs shareholder franchise"
    - "corporate injury vs individual injury"
    - "litigation accountability vs strike suits"
    - "information rights vs confidentiality"
    - "ownership transparency vs privacy"
    - "equal treatment vs differentiated share classes"
    - "controller power vs non-ratable benefits"
    - "collective action / free rider problem vs individual standing"
    - "exit rights vs transaction finality"
    - "shareholder activism vs market-abuse / concert-party regulation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "股東救濟第一眼不是『我持股所以公司做錯就是我的損害』；先問『受傷的是公司還是股東本人、哪一項權利被侵害、誰有standing、前置程序是否完成、救濟是替公司還是替自己取得』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-76.1 第一眼｜STATUS → CLASS / RIGHTS → INFORMATION → PARTICIPATION → HARM → DIRECT / DERIVATIVE → STANDING → DEMAND / PRECONDITIONS → REMEDY → COST / ENFORCEMENT

```text
STATUS
＝record holder? beneficial owner? nominee? former shareholder?

CLASS
＝common? preferred? non-voting? special class rights?

RIGHT
＝vote? information? dividend? nomination? meeting? proposal? transfer?

HARM
＝company-level? shareholder-specific? class-specific?

CLAIM TYPE
＝direct? derivative? representative? oppression?

STANDING
＝who may sue / demand inspection?

PRECONDITION
＝demand? demand futility? holding period? threshold? exhaustion?

REMEDY
＝injunction? rescission? damages? accounting? buy-out? appraisal? inspection?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-76.19 Evidence architecture

- share register / beneficial ownership records；
- class rights；
- charter / bylaws；
- shareholder agreements；
- meeting notices / agendas；
- proxy materials；
- voting records；
- information demands；
- board responses；
- related-party records；
- share issuance documents；
- distribution records；
- controller communications；
- derivative demand；
- appraisal demand；
- valuation materials；
- prior litigation / settlement records。

核心：

> **股東案先決定「誰的權利、誰的損害、誰取得訴權、救濟最後回到誰」；不先做這一步，就很容易把公司損害錯寫成股東自己的錢。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "UNCITRAL_INSOLVENCY_MODEL_FRAMEWORK_STABLE__DOMESTIC_RESTRUCTURING_ENACTMENT_AND_CASE_LAW_CHECK_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

UNCITRAL's cross-border and enterprise-group insolvency model-law architecture remains a stable international reference, but model laws require domestic enactment. Stay, class voting, valuation, new money, cramdown and group coordination must be checked in the governing restructuring regime.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — Insolvency model laws**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/insolvency
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL maintains model-law frameworks for cross-border insolvency, insolvency-related judgments and enterprise-group insolvency; enactment remains State-specific.
- **UNCITRAL — Model Law on Enterprise Group Insolvency (2019)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/MLEGI
  - Verified point: The 2019 Model Law provides a model framework for enterprise-group insolvency and rescue, not directly applicable national law.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

D↔C↔B橋接：以going-concern organization是否值得保存為主，並透過stay/new money/class voting/valuation/cramdown重組債權與股權。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-044-CORPORATE_RESTRUCTURING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "企業重整法"
  english_name: "Corporate Restructuring Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當企業財務困難但持續營運的整體價值可能高於拆散清算價值時，法律如何暫停個別追索、維持營運、取得新資金、重組債務與股權，並透過表決、估值與法院確認確保重整不是把損失偷偷轉嫁給弱勢債權人？"

  historical_reason:
    "企業價值常存在於組織、客戶、員工、許可、品牌、供應鏈與契約網絡，而不只是單一資產。若每個債權人同時查封，可能摧毀 going-concern premium；但若一味保護企業，又可能讓無價值公司持續消耗資產並犧牲債權人。重整法因此以 moratorium、DIP financing、plan、classes、voting、cramdown、valuation、new money、governance 與 confirmation tests 比較重整與清算。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "企業存續 vs 債權回收"
    - "going-concern value vs liquidation value"
    - "管理層持續控制 vs creditor oversight"
    - "新資金優先 vs 舊債權順位"
    - "多數表決 vs 少數債權人保護"
    - "股東保留控制 vs creditor priority"
    - "快速 restructuring vs 估值正確"
    - "工作與供應鏈維持 vs zombie company"
    - "court supervision vs commercial negotiation"
    - "國內重整 vs 跨境集團協調"

  shortest_maxim:
    "企業重整第一眼不是『公司還想不想活』；先問『它的 going-concern value 是否真的高於替代方案、誰提供新錢、誰承擔 haircut、誰表決、誰被 cram down、舊股東憑什麼保留價值』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 50.1 第一眼｜VIABILITY → VALUE → STAY → FUNDING → OPERATIONS → PLAN → CLASSES → VOTE → CRAMDOWN → CONFIRMATION → EXIT

```text
VIABILITY
＝核心業務是否仍有經濟價值？

VALUE
＝going-concern vs liquidation？

STAY
＝需要停止哪些個別追索？

FUNDING
＝誰提供 DIP / rescue finance？

OPERATIONS
＝哪些契約、員工、供應商必須維持？

PLAN
＝減債？展期？利率？debt-to-equity？asset sale？

CLASSES
＝債權人／股東如何分組？

VOTE
＝誰投票？門檻？

CRAMDOWN
＝不同意的一組能否被拘束？

CONFIRMATION
＝法院檢查哪些 fairness / feasibility tests？

EXIT
＝如何從重整程序退出？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 50.20 證據特徵

- business plan；
- cash-flow forecast；
- liquidation analysis；
- valuation；
- financing term sheet；
- restructuring support agreement；
- creditor classes；
- voting report；
- related-party disclosures；
- DIP budget；
- board minutes；
- customer / supplier data；
- employment data；
- intercompany claims；
- plan；
- confirmation order；
- exit financing。

核心：

> **重整案的核心證據不是「大家希望公司活」，而是「活下來的法律與經濟方案是否真的比替代方案好」。**


EU company-law materials remain active in 2026, but corporate-control transactions still depend on transaction form, governing company law, board/shareholder approvals, fiduciary standards and any cross-border conversion/merger rules. Competition clearance and takeover compliance remain separate routes.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Company law and corporate governance**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance_en
  - Verified point: The Commission's current page records a June 2026 report on review of the Shareholder Rights Directive and continuing EU company-law activity.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

處理公司法上的交易形式、機關批准、控制移轉、fiduciary/conflict process、少數退出與法人／資產負債變更。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-140-CORPORATE_MA_CONTROL_TRANSACTIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公司併購／控制權交易法"
  english_name: "Corporate M&A / Control Transactions Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項merger、share acquisition、asset sale、takeover bid、scheme、going-private、spin-off、division、conversion或其他控制權交易，依公司法是否需要董事會／股東批准、特別程序、資訊揭露、獨立審查、少數保護、appraisal／退出、債權人／員工保護與登記；交易完成後法人、資產、負債與治理位置又如何合法變更？"

  historical_reason:
    "控制權交易同時改變資產配置、治理權與股東退出位置。普通契約只處理買賣雙方承諾，卻不能完整回答董事會是否有權賣公司、股東是否必須表決、少數能否被現金擠出、債權與勞工如何被保護、法人是否合併消滅，以及跨境公司如何移動。公司併購法因此形成一套交易有效性與治理程序制度，與競爭法的市場結構審查並行但不同。"

  first_move:
    "先把TRANSACTION FORM → GOVERNING COMPANY LAW → CONTROL CHANGE → CORPORATE APPROVAL → FIDUCIARY / CONFLICT PROCESS → SHAREHOLDER EXIT / REMEDY → REGULATORY OVERLAYS分開；不要看到『併購』就先跑DLM-136 competition merger control。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "deal certainty vs shareholder protection"
    - "board negotiation power vs shareholder franchise"
    - "control premium vs minority equality"
    - "transaction speed vs informed approval"
    - "private contract freedom vs mandatory corporate procedure"
    - "controller exit / take-private flexibility vs minority coercion"
    - "cross-border mobility vs creditor / employee / anti-abuse safeguards"
    - "deal protection vs competing bids"
    - "confidential negotiation vs disclosure"
    - "appraisal exit vs collective transaction finality"
    - "corporate-law validity vs competition / securities / FDI clearance"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公司併購第一眼不是『這交易會不會壟斷』；先問『用什麼公司法交易形式移轉什麼控制、哪個機關有權批准、董事與控制股東有無衝突、少數如何表決或退出、哪些外部監管仍另要通過』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-75.1 第一眼｜STRUCTURE → GOVERNING LAW → CONTROL / CONSIDERATION → BOARD POWER → SHAREHOLDER VOTE → CONFLICT / PROCESS → DISCLOSURE → EXIT / APPRAISAL → CLOSING CONDITIONS → SUCCESSION EFFECT

```text
STRUCTURE
＝merger? share deal? asset sale? tender offer? scheme? conversion? division?

GOVERNING LAW
＝law of incorporation? target law? bidder law? securities law overlay?

CONTROL
＝does transaction transfer control or only investment position?

BOARD POWER
＝who negotiates / approves?

SHAREHOLDER VOTE
＝required? class vote? supermajority? no vote exception?

CONFLICT
＝controller on both sides? management rollover? special committee?

DISCLOSURE
＝what information must shareholders receive?

EXIT
＝appraisal? withdrawal? squeeze-out? sell-out? challenge?

CLOSING CONDITIONS
＝corporate approval + competition + FDI + securities + financing + sector approvals?

SUCCESSION
＝what entity survives and what assets / liabilities / proceedings continue?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-75.20 Evidence architecture

- merger / SPA / scheme documents；
- board minutes；
- special committee mandate；
- fairness opinions / valuations；
- shareholder circular / proxy；
- tender offer documents；
- voting records；
- financing commitments；
- controller / management rollover arrangements；
- conflicts disclosures；
- appraisal demands；
- registry filings；
- regulatory conditions precedent；
- employee / creditor notices；
- closing certificates；
- post-closing succession records。

核心：

> **公司併購案要把「簽約、公司法批准、股東退出、監管核准、交割、生效」拆成不同節點；任何一個完成都不能自證整筆交易已合法閉合。**

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-148-PUBLIC_TAKEOVER_TENDER_OFFER_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公開收購／要約收購法"
  english_name: "Public Takeover / Tender Offer Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當一個人或一致行動集團透過向公開公司證券持有人提出公開要約而取得或增加控制時，何時構成受規範的takeover / tender offer；是否觸發mandatory bid、equitable price、all-holders / equal-treatment、disclosure、withdrawal、conditions、financing、target-board response、competing bids、squeeze-out / sell-out與跨境程序？"

  historical_reason:
    "公開收購把原本可以在公司內部協商的控制權移轉，轉成直接向分散股東提出退出／出售選擇。這會同時產生資訊不對稱、時間壓力、控制溢價分配、少數股東保護、董事防禦措施與市場操縱風險。因此公開收購法不是一般契約法，也不是所有公司併購的同義詞；它的核心是當控制權取得透過公開證券市場進行時，誰有資格提出、股東如何平等被對待、何時必須全面出價，以及收購程序如何在不預判交易好壞的情況下保持可驗證。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識subject company、security class、bidder／beneficial owner、existing holdings、acting-in-concert、offer structure、control threshold與jurisdiction；不要先因『要買公司』就套merger、antitrust或mandatory bid。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "control acquisition vs minority shareholder protection"
    - "bidder speed / deal certainty vs investor decision time"
    - "control premium vs equal treatment"
    - "board defence vs shareholder choice"
    - "mandatory bid protection vs transaction flexibility"
    - "offer conditions vs irrevocability / certainty"
    - "financing certainty vs bidder optionality"
    - "market disclosure vs tactical confidentiality"
    - "competing bids vs orderly process"
    - "squeeze-out efficiency vs minority property rights"
    - "cross-border offer efficiency vs local investor protection"
    - "takeover procedure vs corporate / competition / securities-fraud law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公開收購第一眼不是『有人想買公司』；先問『買的是哪一類證券、透過什麼公開要約、誰與誰一致行動、控制門檻是否被跨過、價格與程序是否因此取得mandatory / equal-treatment義務』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-104.1 第一眼｜TARGET → SECURITY CLASS → BIDDER / BENEFICIAL OWNER → EXISTING HOLDING → ACTING IN CONCERT → OFFER TYPE → CONTROL THRESHOLD → PRICE → CONDITIONS / FINANCING → DISCLOSURE → TIMETABLE → BOARD RESPONSE → COMPETING BID → SQUEEZE / SELL-OUT

```text
TARGET
＝listed / public company? foreign private issuer? private company?

SECURITY CLASS
＝voting shares? non-voting shares? debt? convertible instrument?

BIDDER
＝third-party bidder? issuer? parent / affiliate? consortium?

EXISTING HOLDING
＝how much already owned / controlled?

ACTING IN CONCERT
＝whose holdings aggregate for control / bid duties?

OFFER TYPE
＝mandatory? voluntary? partial? all-cash? securities exchange? issuer tender?

CONTROL THRESHOLD
＝which jurisdiction defines control and how?

PRICE
＝equitable / highest price / adjustment / competing consideration?

CONDITIONS / FINANCING
＝minimum acceptance? regulatory approvals? financing certainty?

DISCLOSURE
＝offer document? Schedule TO? target response? financing source?

TIMETABLE
＝commencement? minimum offer period? withdrawal? extension?

BOARD RESPONSE
＝recommendation? neutrality? defensive measures? shareholder approval?

COMPETING BID
＝revision? competing timetable? information equality?

EXIT
＝squeeze-out? sell-out? going private? delisting?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-104.26 Evidence architecture

- target constitutional / listing status；
- subject security class；
- bidder identity / group structure；
- beneficial ownership records；
- derivatives / voting arrangements；
- acting-in-concert evidence；
- prior purchases / price history；
- financing commitments；
- offer announcement / offer document；
- regulator filings；
- board minutes / recommendation；
- fairness / adviser materials where relevant；
- competing-bid documents；
- shareholder acceptances；
- regulatory approvals；
- squeeze-out / sell-out notices；
- current national implementing law。

核心：

> **公開收購案不能從『他買超過某比例』直接閉合。要先把持股、控制、一致行動、offer類型、價格、程序與跨法域規則接成同一條可追溯鏈。**

```yaml

  - "跨過某個全球固定持股百分比就一定mandatory bid。"

  - "statutory merger＝Regulation 14D tender offer。"
  - "takeover程序核准＝competition merger control也完成。"
  - "公司法交易有效＝公開收購程序自然有效。"
```

EU competition law remains current but its implementation environment is moving in 2026: merger guidelines are under active review and technology-transfer rules were replaced from 1 May 2026. Relevant market, undertaking status, effects and remedy standards remain case-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Review of Merger Guidelines**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/mergers/review-merger-guidelines_en
  - Verified point: Draft revised Merger Guidelines were published 30 April 2026; consultation closed 26 June 2026 and finalisation is planned for Q4 2026.
- **European Commission — TTBER**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/antitrust-and-cartels/legislation/ttber_en
  - Verified point: Commission Regulation (EU) 2026/877 (TTBER) and the accompanying Technology Transfer Guidelines entered into force on 1 May 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-009-COMPETITION_ANTITRUST_LAW"
  chinese_name: "競爭法／反壟斷法"
  english_name: "Competition / Antitrust Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "企業追求利潤、規模、合作與市場力量本來都是商業活動的一部分；法律在什麼條件下介入，因為某種協議、單方行為、合併或制度安排開始破壞競爭過程而不是單純打敗競爭對手？"

  historical_reason:
    "市場競爭可以降低價格、促進品質、創新與選擇，但企業也可能透過卡特爾、排他行為、濫用市場力量或反競爭合併消除競爭壓力。競爭法因此不是懲罰成功企業，而是防止私人市場力量以特定方式取代競爭機制。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "企業自由 vs 市場競爭秩序"
    - "規模效率 vs 市場力量集中"
    - "合作效率 vs 卡特爾"
    - "低價競爭 vs 掠奪性排除"
    - "垂直整合效率 vs 封鎖競爭者"
    - "併購協同 vs 競爭壓力消失"
    - "創新／網路效應 vs 市場鎖定"
    - "快速執法 vs 程序公平與經濟證據"

  shortest_maxim:
    "競爭法第一眼不是『誰比較大、誰贏了』；先問『哪個行為透過什麼機制，削弱了哪一種競爭壓力』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 15.1 第一眼｜CONDUCT → MARKET POWER → THEORY OF HARM → EFFECT

競爭案件不能從「不公平」三個字開始。

先拆：

```text
CONDUCT
＝協議？單方行為？合併？平台規則？排他安排？

MARKET STRUCTURE / POWER
＝誰受到哪些競爭約束？是否具有市場力量？

THEORY OF HARM
＝價格協調？排除？封鎖？減少創新？提高進入障礙？資訊交換？

EFFECT / LIKELY EFFECT
＝競爭壓力如何被減弱？
```

核心：

> **競爭法保護的是競爭過程，不是保證每一個競爭者都活下來。**

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 15.10 證據特徵

競爭案件的證據常要證明「市場機制」：

- 價格資料；
- 銷量；
- 市場份額；
- 客戶轉換；
- 競爭者名單；
- 招標資料；
- 內部策略文件；
- 董事會／經營簡報；
- 電子郵件；
- 競爭者聯絡紀錄；
- 協會會議；
- 交易條款；
- 獨家契約；
- 成本資料；
- 毛利；
- 進入／退出資料；
- 消費者調查；
- 經濟學模型；
- 合併文件；
- 平台流量與多邊市場資料。

其中：

> **內部文件可以證明目的／認知，但不能單獨取代市場效果分析。**

反過來：

> **市場份額很高，也不能單獨證明非法行為。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 15.13 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依現行法域現查：

1. 卡特爾／協議禁止規則；
2. per se / object / effect 等分析架構；
3. 市場支配／壟斷／濫用要件；
4. 市場界定方法；
5. 垂直限制；
6. 單方行為；
7. 掠奪性定價、拒絕交易、綁售等特殊標準；
8. 併購申報門檻；
9. 合併實體審查標準；
10. 效率與抗辯；
11. 主管機關調查權；
12. 寬恕／自首制度；
13. 民事、行政、刑事責任；
14. 域外適用；
15. 平台／數位市場特別法。

分別承擔集中控制、協調行為與支配地位三個不同competition gates；不得因都涉及市場力量而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-136-MERGER_CONTROL_CONCENTRATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "企業結合／併購審查法"
  english_name: "Merger Control / Concentration Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項share deal、asset deal、joint venture、merger、acquisition of control或系列交易，是否先構成特定法域可審查的concentration / merger；是否達notification / call-in / local-nexus門檻、受何種standstill與申報義務；若進入實體審查，交易是否可能顯著減損競爭、形成或強化市場力量、封鎖投入／客戶、消滅潛在競爭、降低創新／勞動／採購競爭；又有哪些效率、反事實、remedy與程序救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統反托拉斯主要處理已發生的協議或濫用，但企業結合會在行為發生前改變市場結構：兩個原本互相競爭、供應、採購或創新的獨立決策中心可能永久合併。若只等結合後再用行為規範修補，某些競爭結構與創新能力可能已不可逆消失，因此各法域逐步形成事前申報、standstill、調查、禁止／附條件批准與remedy制度。其核心不是反對企業成長，而是辨識哪些控制權變動會把原本的競爭壓力變成單一控制。"

  first_move:
    "先做TRANSACTION / CONTROL / JURISDICTION MAP；不要從『兩家公司合併後很大』直接跳到違法，也不要先做市場份額再忘記交易是否根本落入申報制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "transaction freedom vs structural prevention"
    - "legal certainty / thresholds vs under-the-radar acquisitions"
    - "speed of dealmaking vs effective investigation"
    - "market concentration vs firm-specific competitive evidence"
    - "short-term price effects vs innovation / dynamic competition"
    - "horizontal rivalry loss vs vertical efficiencies"
    - "potential competition vs speculative future harm"
    - "buyer power / labour effects vs seller-side focus"
    - "global transactions vs multiple sovereign review regimes"
    - "structural remedies vs behavioural commitments"
    - "business confidentiality vs third-party participation"
    - "clearance finality vs post-closing enforcement / call-in powers"

  shortest_maxim:
    "併購審查第一眼不是『合併後市占多少』；先問『什麼交易、誰取得何種control、哪個法域有審查權、何時能closing、競爭消失在哪一個具體維度、反事實是什麼、remedy能不能真的保住那個競爭』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-65.1 第一眼｜TRANSACTION → CONTROL → JURISDICTION → NOTIFICATION → STANDSTILL → MARKET / COMPETITIVE PROCESS → THEORY OF HARM → COUNTERFACTUAL → EFFICIENCIES → REMEDIES → CLEARANCE / CHALLENGE

```text
TRANSACTION
＝shares? assets? merger? JV? contractual control? serial acquisitions?

CONTROL
＝sole? joint? decisive influence? negative control? durable JV?

JURISDICTION
＝turnover? assets? transaction value? local nexus? market share? call-in?

NOTIFICATION
＝mandatory? voluntary? pre-closing? post-closing? filing party?

STANDSTILL
＝may parties close / integrate before clearance?

COMPETITIVE PROCESS
＝which products/services/labour/input/innovation dimensions actually compete?

THEORY OF HARM
＝horizontal unilateral? coordination? vertical foreclosure? conglomerate leveraging? potential entry loss?

COUNTERFACTUAL
＝what likely happens without the transaction?

EFFICIENCIES
＝merger-specific? verifiable? timely? passed through / competition-enhancing?

REMEDIES
＝divestiture? access? interoperability? behavioural condition? monitoring?

OUTCOME
＝clearance? conditional clearance? prohibition? withdrawal? judicial review?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-65.22 Evidence architecture

- transaction documents / SPA / merger agreement；
- cap table / governance / veto rights；
- board materials；
- ordinary-course covenants；
- notification thresholds and nexus evidence；
- internal deal rationale；
- customer switching / bidding data；
- pricing / margin / capacity；
- product roadmap / R&D pipeline；
- supplier / labour data；
- entry evidence；
- economic studies；
- remedy package / purchaser viability；
- clean-team protocols；
- regulator communications；
- closing / integration timeline。

核心：

> **併購案要證明的是一個結構反事實：如果沒有這筆交易，哪一個獨立競爭壓力原本最可能繼續存在；交易後它在哪裡消失，又有沒有可驗證的效率或remedy把它補回來。**

```yaml

  DLM_ID: "DLM-137"

  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-66｜DLM-137｜限制競爭協議／卡特爾法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B46"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "A:政府／國家／公權力"
```


## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ARTICLE101_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__2026_TTBER_IN_FORCE__LENIENCY_EXEMPTION_AND_DOMESTIC_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Article 101 remains the core EU agreements/cartel framework. Regulation (EU) 2026/877 (TTBER) and new Technology Transfer Guidelines entered into force on 1 May 2026, so technology-licensing analysis must use the new regime while cartel/leniency and other exemptions remain context-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — TTBER**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/antitrust-and-cartels/legislation/ttber_en
  - Verified point: Commission Regulation (EU) 2026/877 (TTBER) and the accompanying Technology Transfer Guidelines entered into force on 1 May 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔集中控制、協調行為與支配地位三個不同competition gates；不得因都涉及市場力量而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-137-RESTRICTIVE_AGREEMENTS_CARTEL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "限制競爭協議／卡特爾法"
  english_name: "Restrictive Agreements / Cartel Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "兩個以上仍具獨立經濟決策地位的undertakings、競爭者、供應商／經銷商、雇主、採購者或association，是否透過agreement、concerted practice、decision、資訊交換、共同演算法或其他協調形成限制競爭的共同意志／行為；該限制依法域屬hard-core / by object / per se，或需作effects / rule-of-reason分析；是否存在法定豁免、block exemption、效率、合作必要性、leniency與程序責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "競爭可以在企業不合併的情況下被消滅。若市場參與者仍形式獨立，卻協調價格、產量、客戶、市場、投標、工資或其他競爭參數，市場看似有多家公司，實際上卻只剩一個協調決策。現代競爭法因此把『共同協調』與『單方市場力量』分離；同時也承認研發、標準、採購、技術授權、供應與其他合作可能產生效率，不能把所有合作都叫cartel。"

  first_move:
    "先證明coordination object：誰和誰、透過什麼接觸／機制、就哪個競爭參數形成何種共同理解或互相調適；不要從價格一起上漲或幾家公司同時做一樣的事直接推定cartel。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "independent rivalry vs legitimate cooperation"
    - "hard-core deterrence vs over-criminalisation"
    - "direct agreement evidence vs inference from conduct"
    - "information transparency vs collusive signalling"
    - "association coordination vs independent member choice"
    - "algorithmic assistance vs human / firm attribution"
    - "horizontal collaboration efficiencies vs cartel camouflage"
    - "vertical distribution control vs intra-brand efficiencies"
    - "leniency incentives vs procedural fairness"
    - "public procurement integrity vs competition enforcement"
    - "multi-jurisdiction sanctions vs double / parallel proceedings"
    - "confidential business planning vs evidence preservation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "卡特爾／限制協議第一眼不是『價格變一樣了』；先問『獨立事業之間到底有沒有可歸責的協調、協調的是哪個競爭參數、這種限制在該法域屬哪個法律類型、需不需要證明效果、是否存在效率／豁免，以及誰以什麼證據取得處罰資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-66.1 第一眼｜ACTORS → INDEPENDENCE → CONTACT / MECHANISM → AGREEMENT / CONCERTATION → COMPETITIVE PARAMETER → LEGAL CATEGORY → EFFECT / EXEMPTION → ATTRIBUTION → EVIDENCE → SANCTION / REMEDY

```text
ACTORS
＝competitors? supplier/distributor? employers? buyers? association members?

INDEPENDENCE
＝are they separate undertakings for competition-law purposes?

CONTACT / MECHANISM
＝meeting? chat? trade association? platform? algorithm? intermediary?

COORDINATION
＝agreement? concerted practice? decision? signalling plus responsive conduct?

PARAMETER
＝price? wage? output? customer? territory? bid? quality? innovation? terms?

LEGAL CATEGORY
＝hard-core? by object? per se? effects / rule of reason? vertical restriction?

EXEMPTION / EFFICIENCY
＝block exemption? statutory exemption? Article 101(3)-type test? ancillary restraint?

ATTRIBUTION
＝which entity / parent / individual is legally responsible?

EVIDENCE
＝communications? parallel conduct plus? bid pattern? leniency statement? device data?

OUTCOME
＝fine? criminal charge? damages? nullity? injunction? debarment? leniency?
```
