## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-66.21 Evidence architecture

- competitor communications；
- trade-association documents；
- pricing / wage / bid datasets；
- tender submissions；
- meeting / travel records；
- distribution contracts；
- platform / algorithm logs；
- data-sharing protocols；
- joint-venture agreements；
- leniency / whistleblower materials；
- dawn-raid chain of custody；
- market structure；
- customer / supplier testimony；
- compliance training / internal warnings。

核心：

> **限制協議案不能把「市場結果很像」升格成「協議已證明」；但也不能要求只有白紙黑字寫著cartel才承認協調。真正工作是建立接觸、共同理解、競爭參數、行為與法律類型之間的可追溯鏈。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ARTICLE102_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__GUIDANCE_AND_CASELAW_DYNAMIC__MARKET_POWER_AND_JURISDICTION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Article 102 remains the EU dominance baseline, but guidance development and case law remain dynamic. Dominance cannot be inferred from firm size or platform status alone; market definition, power, conduct, objective justification and remedy must be checked against current authority.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Application of Article 102 TFEU**
  - URL: https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/antitrust-and-cartels/legislation/application-article-102-tfeu_en
  - Verified point: Article 102 remains the EU abuse-of-dominance legal framework; Commission guidance development remains a dynamic policy process requiring current case-law and guidance checks.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔集中控制、協調行為與支配地位三個不同competition gates；不得因都涉及市場力量而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-138-ABUSE_OF_DOMINANCE_MONOPOLIZATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "支配地位濫用／獨占法"
  english_name: "Abuse of Dominance / Monopolization Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一undertaking是否先在具體relevant market取得dominant position、monopoly power或依法域相當之顯著市場力量；爭議行為是competition on the merits、正常商業選擇，還是利用／取得／維持該力量所作的exclusionary、exploitative、discriminatory、tying、rebate、predatory、refusal-to-deal、margin-squeeze、self-preferencing或其他受規範行為；是否有objective justification、efficiency、causal effect與適當remedy？"

  historical_reason:
    "競爭法保護競爭，不保護每一個競爭者免於失敗。企業可能因更好產品、創新、效率或歷史優勢取得極大市場份額；若法律僅因成功就處罰，反而會削弱競爭誘因。但當一個已不受充分競爭約束的事業利用該力量排除競爭、封鎖不可替代投入、綁售、歧視交易對手或以非競爭本身的方式維持力量，普通契約自由可能不足以保護市場。因此不同法域形成dominance abuse或monopolization規則；兩者共享市場力量問題，但構成要件並不相同。"

  first_move:
    "先證明MARKET POWER / DOMINANCE GATE，再分析conduct；不要從公司很大、平台很重要、價格很高或競爭者抱怨直接跳到濫用。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "successful competition vs unlawful exclusion"
    - "firm autonomy vs special responsibility of dominant firms"
    - "market definition certainty vs digital / ecosystem complexity"
    - "low prices vs predation"
    - "discount competition vs loyalty foreclosure"
    - "property / IP control vs access duties"
    - "integration efficiencies vs tying / leveraging"
    - "self-preferencing efficiency vs gatekeeper foreclosure"
    - "short-term consumer benefit vs long-term exclusion"
    - "ex ante regulation vs ex post competition enforcement"
    - "dominance evidence vs market-share shortcuts"
    - "effective remedies vs forced business redesign"

  shortest_maxim:
    "濫用支配／獨占第一眼不是『它是不是市場老大』；先問『它在哪個市場有什麼程度的持久力量、這項行為是不是靠競爭本身取得成果，還是利用／維持該力量排除競爭或榨取交易條件、法律需要證明什麼效果與因果、有哪些正當化與remedy』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-67.1 第一眼｜MARKET → MARKET POWER → DOMINANCE / MONOPOLY STANDARD → CONDUCT → THEORY OF EXCLUSION / EXPLOITATION → EFFECT → JUSTIFICATION → CAUSATION → REMEDY

```text
MARKET
＝product? service? platform side? ecosystem? labour/input?

POWER
＝share? barriers? network effects? switching? data? capacity? buyer dependence?

LEGAL STATUS
＝dominance? monopoly power? attempted monopolization? collective dominance?

CONDUCT
＝price? rebate? tie? refusal? discrimination? self-preference? access restriction?

THEORY
＝foreclosure? raising rivals' costs? exploitative extraction? maintenance of monopoly?

EFFECT
＝capable / likely / actual effect under applicable standard?

JUSTIFICATION
＝objective necessity? efficiency? product improvement? security? capacity?

CAUSATION
＝did conduct materially contribute to exclusion / maintenance / exploitation?

REMEDY
＝cease? access? non-discrimination? contract change? divestiture? fine? damages?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-67.22 Evidence architecture

- market / substitution evidence；
- shares over time；
- entry barriers；
- switching / multi-homing；
- internal strategy；
- pricing / rebate formulas；
- contracts；
- access requests / refusals；
- API / ranking / default design；
- cost data；
- capacity；
- competitor / customer testimony；
- economic models；
- product-security justifications；
- regulator decisions；
- market outcomes before / after conduct。

核心：

> **濫用支配案不能把市場成功當成罪證；它必須把『力量從哪裡來』『行為做了什麼』『競爭而不是某個競爭者如何被削弱』『正當理由是否成立』四條鏈接起來。**

# G2-68｜第四十六批交界辨識器

```yaml
COMPETITION_SPECIALIZATION_ROUTER_V0_46:

  MERGER_CONTROL:
    first_question:
      "競爭風險是否來自一筆交易把原本獨立的控制／競爭決策中心結構性合併？"
    route_when:
      - "share / asset acquisition"
      - "merger / joint venture"
      - "change of control"
      - "pre-merger notification / standstill"
      - "horizontal / vertical / conglomerate effects"
      - "potential-competition acquisition"
    do_not_substitute:
      - "ordinary competitor agreement with no structural control change"
      - "unilateral conduct by already dominant firm"

  RESTRICTIVE_AGREEMENTS_CARTEL:
    first_question:
      "是否有兩個以上獨立市場參與者透過agreement / concerted practice / decision協調本來應各自決定的競爭參數？"
    route_when:
      - "price / wage fixing"
      - "bid rigging"
      - "market / customer allocation"
      - "information exchange"
      - "trade association coordination"
      - "horizontal / vertical restrictive agreements"
      - "algorithmic coordination candidate"
    do_not_substitute:
      - "merger structural review only"
      - "purely unilateral conduct absent coordination"

  ABUSE_DOMINANCE_MONOPOLIZATION:
    first_question:
      "是否先存在可證明的dominance / monopoly power，再由單一或依法域特定的dominant actor以特定conduct排除、維持、擴張或濫用該力量？"
    route_when:
      - "predatory pricing"
      - "loyalty rebates / exclusivity by dominant firm"
      - "tying / bundling"
      - "refusal to deal / access restriction"
      - "margin squeeze"
      - "self-preferencing"
      - "exploitative abuse where applicable"
    do_not_substitute:
      - "big company without dominance analysis"
      - "cartel / competitor agreement"
```

最短路由：

```text
「這筆交易會不會把原本競爭者變成同一控制？」
→ DLM-136

「這幾家原本獨立的公司是不是在協調競爭參數？」
→ DLM-137

「這一家已有市場力量的公司是不是用特定行為維持／濫用那個力量？」
→ DLM-138
```

# G2-69｜與第一卷 DLM-009 的 cross-volume specialization

```yaml
COMPETITION_SPECIALIZATION_CROSS_VOLUME_V0_46:

  DLM_009_COMPETITION_ANTITRUST_LAW:
    status: "GENERAL_COMPETITION_MOTHER_ROUTER / KEEP"
    role:
      - "辨識問題是否進入competition / antitrust world"
      - "處理跨制度market power / competition-process共通第一眼"
      - "在尚不能分流時維持general competition hypothesis"

  DLM_136_MERGER_CONTROL:
    specialized_for:
      - "structural transaction / concentration"
      - "notification / standstill / clearance"
      - "SLC / SIEC / structural theories of harm"

  DLM_137_RESTRICTIVE_AGREEMENTS_CARTEL:
    specialized_for:
      - "multilateral coordination"
      - "horizontal / vertical restrictive agreements"
      - "cartel detection / leniency / agreement evidence"

  DLM_138_ABUSE_DOMINANCE_MONOPOLIZATION:
    specialized_for:
      - "unilateral conduct + market-power gate"
      - "dominance / monopoly maintenance"
      - "exclusionary / exploitative conduct"

  INTEGRATION_RULE:
    - "DLM-009不因136..138出現而失效。"
    - "同一案件可同時觸發多張卡，例如dominant platform收購nascent rival後又要求商家MFN。"
    - "最終整合時再處理DLM-009中與三張專門卡重複的細節。"
```

競爭法三分法第一眼：

```text
STRUCTURE CHANGES THROUGH TRANSACTION
→ MERGER CONTROL

INDEPENDENT ACTORS COORDINATE
→ RESTRICTIVE AGREEMENTS / CARTEL

POWERFUL ACTOR ACTS UNILATERALLY
→ ABUSE / MONOPOLIZATION
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "DSA_DMA_FRAMEWORKS_CURRENT__DESIGNATIONS_MARKET_INVESTIGATIONS_AND_PROCEEDINGS_DYNAMIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The DSA is generally applicable from 17 February 2024, while obligations/enforcement for designated very large services are service-specific. DMA gatekeeper status is also dynamic: the Commission's June 2026 cloud announcement was a preliminary designation position, not a final universal classification.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — DSA enforcement framework**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/dsa-enforcement
  - Verified point: The DSA's general application date is 17 February 2024; designated VLOP/VLOSE obligations and enforcement are service-specific and dynamic.
- **European Commission — DMA cloud designation preliminary position**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/news/commission-reaches-preliminary-position-amazons-and-microsofts-market-leading-cloud-services-should
  - Verified point: On 25 June 2026 the Commission announced preliminary views on potential DMA gatekeeper designation of AWS and Azure, illustrating dynamic designation/investigation status.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

平台不是一般公司治理或一般競爭法的替代品；以gateway/rule-setting/ranking/account/data/ecosystem control形成數位市場中介專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-015-PLATFORM_LAW"
  chinese_name: "平台法"
  english_name: "Platform Law / Online Intermediary Governance"

  domain_core_question:
    "當私人平台不只提供服務，而同時掌握使用者身分、內容排序、交易入口、商家可見度、帳號生死、資料、支付或生態系規則時，私人自治何時開始產生接近公共基礎設施的外部效果；法律應如何配置平台責任、使用者程序、透明、競爭與系統風險？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統契約通常是雙方之間的交換，但大型數位平台可能同時連接數百萬使用者與商家，設定進入市場與表達空間的規則，並透過排序、推薦、帳號與 API 控制他人可見度與交易機會。單純把平台視為『網站業者』或只靠服務條款處理，容易忽略其網路效應、規則制定力、資訊優勢與系統風險。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "私人契約自治 vs 大規模基礎設施性權力"
    - "內容治理 vs 言論／程序公平"
    - "快速移除違法內容 vs 過度刪除"
    - "平台創新與安全 vs 透明與可問責"
    - "推薦最佳化 vs 系統性風險"
    - "平台整合效率 vs 商業使用者依賴"
    - "資料優勢 vs 競爭可爭奪性"
    - "自動化治理 vs 人類救濟"
    - "全球平台規則 vs 地方法律"

  shortest_maxim:
    "平台法第一眼不是『服務條款寫什麼』；先問『平台控制哪一個入口、對誰有規則制定權、哪個決定改變誰的表達、交易、資料或市場機會』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 21.1 第一眼｜FUNCTION → POWER → RELATIONSHIP → DECISION → EFFECT → REVIEW

平台案件先拆：

```text
FUNCTION
＝社群？搜尋？電商？App Store？廣告？雲端？影音？支付？媒合？

POWER
＝平台控制帳號、排序、推薦、搜尋、API、商家接觸客戶、支付、資料？

RELATIONSHIP
＝平台—使用者？平台—商家？平台—廣告主？使用者—使用者？

DECISION
＝下架？封號？降權？推薦？排序？抽成？排他？資料使用？

EFFECT
＝表達受限？收入中斷？市場被封鎖？資料被利用？風險被放大？

REVIEW
＝有理由、通知、申訴、人工審查、外部救濟嗎？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 21.11 證據特徵

平台案件最重要的往往不是公開條款，而是：

- Terms of Service 版本；
- Community Guidelines；
- moderation policy；
- enforcement notice；
- reason statement；
- appeal record；
- account logs；
- recommendation／ranking logs；
- seller agreement；
- API terms；
- commission／fee records；
- ad delivery logs；
- content reach data；
- notice-and-action records；
- transparency reports；
- risk assessments；
- audit reports；
- regulator designation／decision；
- data access；
- internal policy change history。

核心：

> **平台寫了什麼規則，與平台實際怎麼執行，是兩個證據問題。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 21.13 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與平台類型現查：

1. 中介服務分類；
2. 平台免責／責任；
3. notice-and-action；
4. 內容下架程序；
5. 帳號停權／終止；
6. 商家平台規則；
7. 透明與理由；
8. 推薦系統義務；
9. 廣告透明；
10. 兒童／未成年人；
11. 系統性風險；
12. DSA 適用與 VLOP/VLOSE 指定；
13. DMA gatekeeper 指定與核心平台服務；
14. 平台競爭義務；
15. 資料共享／可攜；
16. 消費者與電商；
17. IP 中介責任；
18. 域外適用與跨境執法。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_CONSUMER_ADVERTISING_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__GREEN_TRANSITION_RULES_APPLY_2026_09_27__DIGITAL_AND_SECTOR_RULES_CASE_SPECIFIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

EU consumer-facing marketing rules are moving during 2026. Directive (EU) 2024/825 applies from 27 September 2026, adding green-transition consumer protections. Influencer, platform, financial, health, gambling and AI-generated advertising can trigger separate sector/data/platform rules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Sustainable consumption**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/topics/consumers/consumer-rights-and-complaints/sustainable-consumption_en
  - Verified point: Directive (EU) 2024/825 was due for transposition by 27 March 2026 and applies from 27 September 2026.
- **European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/guidelines-ai-transparency-obligations
  - Verified point: Article 50 transparency obligations apply from 2 August 2026, including transparency duties concerning certain AI-generated/deepfake content.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

跨零售、平台、金融、旅遊、食品、健康與兒少市場；以廣告主／媒介／claim／substantiation／disclosure／targeting形成商業傳播橫向控制。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-060-ADVERTISING_MARKETING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "廣告法"
  english_name: "Advertising / Marketing Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當企業、品牌、代言人、influencer、平台或其他主體以訊息影響消費者交易決定時，如何辨識什麼是廣告、誰是廣告主、主張了什麼明示／暗示訊息、哪些內容必須有證據支持、商業關係如何揭露，並防止價格、評價、健康、環境、兒童與個人化投放造成誤導或操控？"

  historical_reason:
    "市場需要企業說服消費者，但交易決策高度依賴資訊不對稱。廣告法不禁止誇張與說服，而是防止商家用無法驗證、隱藏代價、偽裝獨立推薦或利用脆弱性的方法取得交易。數位市場又把廣告嵌入內容、社群、搜尋、推薦、遊戲與個人資料，使『誰在賣東西』本身都可能被隱藏，因此 modern advertising law 同時處理 ad identification、substantiation、endorsement、targeting、dark patterns 與 platform roles。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "商業表達 vs consumer protection"
    - "創意誇張 vs 可驗證主張"
    - "簡潔廣告 vs 完整揭露"
    - "價格競爭 vs drip / hidden pricing"
    - "influencer authenticity vs sponsorship transparency"
    - "personalization vs manipulation / privacy"
    - "native advertising vs editorial independence"
    - "children marketing vs vulnerability"
    - "health / green claims vs scientific uncertainty"
    - "comparative advertising vs competitor reputation"
    - "platform monetization vs advertiser responsibility"
    - "AI-generated advertising vs identity / evidence / deception"

  shortest_maxim:
    "廣告法第一眼不是『這廣告有沒有誇張』；先問『誰在替誰賣什麼、消費者會理解成什麼主張、哪些是可驗證事實、證據在廣告發布前就存在嗎、商業關係與真正總價有沒有被看見』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 66.1 第一眼｜COMMERCIAL PURPOSE → ADVERTISER → FORMAT → CLAIM → IMPRESSION → EVIDENCE → DISCLOSURE → TARGET → TRANSACTION → REMEDY

```text
COMMERCIAL PURPOSE
＝是否為促進商品／服務／交易？

ADVERTISER
＝品牌？seller？agency？influencer？affiliate？platform？

FORMAT
＝TV？search？native？livestream？short video？review？AI avatar？

CLAIM
＝價格？性能？健康？環境？比較？推薦？限量？

IMPRESSION
＝普通／目標消費者實際會理解什麼？

EVIDENCE
＝有什麼 substantiation？何時取得？

DISCLOSURE
＝廣告身份、sponsor、material connection、terms 是否清楚？

TARGET
＝children？elderly？financially vulnerable？specific profile？

TRANSACTION
＝landing page / checkout 是否延續相同印象？

REMEDY
＝take-down？correction？refund？fine？damages？injunction？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 66.31 證據特徵

- creative files；
- copy variants；
- ad labels；
- influencer contract；
- payment / gifts；
- affiliate records；
- substantiation studies；
- product specs；
- pricing history；
- mandatory fees；
- targeting parameters；
- audience data；
- A/B tests；
- landing pages；
- checkout；
- review provenance；
- AI-generation logs；
- consumer complaints；
- conversion data；
- correction / takedown。

核心：

> **廣告案最重要的問題不是『廣告主說自己想表達什麼』，而是『目標消費者實際被設計成理解什麼，且那個理解在發布當時有沒有證據資格』。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 66.33 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依產品、平台、目標市場現查：

1. advertising definition；
2. deceptive / misleading test；
3. materiality；
4. substantiation；
5. implied claims；
6. comparative advertising；
7. price advertising；
8. mandatory fees / drip pricing；
9. endorsements；
10. influencer disclosure；
11. native advertising；
12. reviews / ratings；
13. children；
14. vulnerable consumers；
15. unsolicited marketing；
16. targeted / personalized ads；
17. data protection；
18. dark patterns；
19. health / food / drug claims；
20. environmental / green claims；
21. financial advertising；
22. political advertising；
23. platform liability / duties；
24. AI / synthetic media；
25. remedies / corrective advertising；
26. cross-border enforcement。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25


WADA's 2026 Prohibited List has been in force since 1 January 2026 and is expressly updated annually. Anti-doping classification therefore requires the current List plus applicable Code/federation rules, while eligibility, governance, labour and competition questions remain separate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **World Anti-Doping Agency — 2026 Prohibited List**
  - URL: https://www.wada-ama.org/en/resources/world-anti-doping-program/prohibited-list
  - Verified point: The 2026 Prohibited List entered into effect on 1 January 2026 and is an annually updated International Standard.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔體育、娛樂創意、旅遊、旅館餐旅、博弈等高度組織化產業生態系；一般公司／契約／消保／監管只作cross-route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-074-SPORTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "體育法"
  english_name: "Sports Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "運動員、俱樂部、聯盟、國家協會、國際 federation、event organizer、anti-doping body、agent、sponsor 與 broadcaster 如何形成競賽法律秩序；private sporting rules 何時有效、何時受 labour、competition、human-rights、medical、contract、arbitration 與 State law 限制；eligibility、discipline、anti-doping、selection、transfer 與 safeguarding 如何取得公平程序？"

  historical_reason:
    "體育依賴統一規則與快速裁決：沒有共同資格、賽程與裁判，競賽無法運作。但體育組織也可能壟斷市場、職業生涯與參賽入口，對運動員身體、收入、國籍／隊籍與聲譽產生巨大影響。體育法因此不是單純『自治規則』，而是 sport rules 與國家契約、勞動、競爭、人權、醫療、反禁藥及爭議解決相互包覆的混合秩序。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "sport autonomy vs public law / judicial review"
    - "field-of-play finality vs legal fairness"
    - "competitive balance vs competition law"
    - "team control vs athlete labour rights"
    - "eligibility uniformity vs non-discrimination / human rights"
    - "anti-doping integrity vs bodily privacy / due process"
    - "athlete health vs performance pressure"
    - "selection discretion vs transparent criteria"
    - "fast arbitration vs effective defence"
    - "commercial sponsorship vs sporting integrity"
    - "betting markets vs match integrity"
    - "youth development vs exploitation"
    - "federation monopoly vs athlete mobility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "體育法第一眼不是『規則就是規則』；先問『誰制定哪一類規則、運動員因何取得／失去參賽或職業位置、這是 field-of-play、eligibility、discipline、contract、anti-doping 還是 governance 決定、能向誰申訴』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 80.1 第一眼｜SPORT / LEVEL → ACTOR → RULE SOURCE → STATUS → DECISION → EVIDENCE → SANCTION / CONSEQUENCE → APPEAL

```text
SPORT / LEVEL
＝professional？amateur？school？national team？international event？

ACTOR
＝athlete？club？league？federation？NADO？event organizer？

RULE SOURCE
＝statute？federation rules？league contract？collective agreement？Code？

STATUS
＝employee？independent contractor？member？selected athlete？

DECISION
＝selection？eligibility？discipline？doping？transfer？field of play？

EVIDENCE
＝sample？video？contract？medical？ranking？betting data？

CONSEQUENCE
＝fine？suspension？ineligibility？loss of medal？termination？

APPEAL
＝internal panel？national tribunal？arbitration / CAS？court？
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_AI_ACT_ART50_APPLICABLE_2026_08_02__DEEPFAKE_TRANSPARENCY_CURRENT__IP_PERSONALITY_AND_CONTRACT_CHECK_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

Article 50 AI Act transparency obligations apply from 2 August 2026 and cover specified AI interactions and AI-generated/manipulated content, including deepfakes. They add a current transparency layer to entertainment/creative production but do not replace copyright, performer, personality, contract or platform rules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/guidelines-ai-transparency-obligations
  - Verified point: Article 50 transparency obligations apply from 2 August 2026, including transparency duties concerning certain AI-generated/deepfake content.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔體育、娛樂創意、旅遊、旅館餐旅、博弈等高度組織化產業生態系；一般公司／契約／消保／監管只作cross-route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-075-ENTERTAINMENT_CREATIVE_INDUSTRIES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "娛樂／創意產業法"
  english_name: "Entertainment / Creative Industries Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "電影、音樂、電視、舞台、演唱會、遊戲、網路節目與藝人商業活動如何從 idea / underlying work 經創作、表演、製作、融資、權利取得、宣傳、發行、平台利用與收益分配；誰真正擁有哪一項 right、誰可授權哪個 territory / term / medium、誰承擔 accounting、labour、privacy、defamation、AI replica 與 production risk？"

  historical_reason:
    "娛樂產品很少由一個人完成。一部電影可能同時需要劇本、小說改編權、導演、演員表演、音樂 composition、sound recording、場景、商標、肖像、工會、保險與平台發行。產業為了把數十種權利壓進一個可融資、可發行的 package，發展出 chain of title、option、license、talent agreement、royalty / residual、distribution windows、completion risk 等特殊契約結構。娛樂法因此不是獨立法典，而是多領域法律被 production lifecycle 重新組織的實務法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "創作者控制 vs producer / investor exploitation rights"
    - "performer dignity vs commercial reuse"
    - "exclusive rights vs public access / expression"
    - "talent bargaining power vs standard-form contracts"
    - "creative freedom vs defamation / privacy"
    - "work-for-hire / assignment vs moral / performer rights"
    - "platform reach vs territorial licensing"
    - "royalty complexity vs accounting transparency"
    - "AI synthetic production vs consent / identity"
    - "child performer opportunity vs exploitation"
    - "fan / remix culture vs infringement"
    - "financing certainty vs creative uncertainty"

  shortest_maxim:
    "娛樂法第一眼不是『誰創作的』；先問『這個 project 裡有哪些作品／表演／人物／錄音／品牌，誰原始取得哪個 right、誰把它授權給誰、territory / term / medium / exclusivity 到哪裡、收入如何結算』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 81.1 第一眼｜PROJECT → ASSET / RIGHT → ORIGINAL HOLDER → CONTRACT → CHAIN OF TITLE → PRODUCTION → EXPLOITATION → REVENUE → ACCOUNTING → DISPUTE

```text
PROJECT
＝film？series？music？concert？game？stage？creator channel？

ASSET / RIGHT
＝script？composition？master？performance？image？brand？format？

ORIGINAL HOLDER
＝author？performer？producer？employer？commissioner？

CONTRACT
＝option？assignment？licence？talent agreement？distribution？

CHAIN OF TITLE
＝每一項必要權利是否真的進入 project？

PRODUCTION
＝shooting / recording / location / labour / safety？

EXPLOITATION
＝cinema？TV？streaming？social？games？merchandise？

REVENUE
＝fee？royalty？residual？participation？ad revenue？

ACCOUNTING
＝gross / net definition？recoupment？audit right？

DISPUTE
＝injunction？damages？accounting？credit？termination？
```

## 81.35 證據特徵

> **娛樂案件最忌諱一句「我們有版權」；真正問題永遠是「哪一項 right、誰原始持有、是否真的移轉、在哪個 territory / term / medium、這次利用到底落在哪個授權範圍」。**

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_PACKAGE_TRAVEL_DIRECTIVE_AMENDED_2026__TRANSPOSITION_TRANSITION_AND_NATIONAL_IMPLEMENTATION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
```

The 2026 amendments to the Package Travel Directive entered into force on 28 May 2026. Member States have 28 months to transpose them, with operator binding effect later; therefore old/current national transposition and future amended rules must not be collapsed into one date.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Package travel amendments**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/news-and-media/news/package-travel-stronger-rights-travellers-and-simpler-rules-travel-industry-2026-05-28_en
  - Verified point: Amendments to the Package Travel Directive entered into force 28 May 2026; Member States have 28 months to transpose, with binding operator application later.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔體育、娛樂創意、旅遊、旅館餐旅、博弈等高度組織化產業生態系；一般公司／契約／消保／監管只作cross-route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-094-TOURISM_TRAVEL_SERVICES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "旅遊／旅行服務法"
  english_name: "Tourism / Travel Services Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "traveller在離開居住地後透過travel agent、tour organizer、OTA、carrier、hotel、guide、activity provider與destination authority購買／組合哪些服務；誰是principal / intermediary、誰收款、誰承諾整體performance、誰負pre-contract information、assistance、refund、insolvency protection與complaint；跨境事故／取消／破產時哪套consumer、contract、transport、insurance、PIL與tourism law各自進場？"

  historical_reason:
    "旅行交易的特殊性在於消費者往往先付款、後履行，履行地遠離居住地，並由多個供應者串接。旅行者若遇到航班取消、飯店倒閉、天災、旅行社破產或目的地危機，很難逐一與外國supplier談判。旅遊法因此發展出organizer / agent distinctions、package protection、information duties、insolvency / repatriation mechanisms、emergency assistance與cross-border consumer cooperation。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "travel freedom vs border / security regulation"
    - "cheap dynamic booking vs clear organizer responsibility"
    - "intermediary neutrality vs transaction control"
    - "supplier fault vs organizer responsibility"
    - "non-refundable pricing vs exceptional events"
    - "consumer protection vs business insolvency risk"
    - "destination risk vs traveller choice"
    - "emergency assistance vs unlimited organizer liability"
    - "tourism growth vs host-community / environment impacts"
    - "personalization vs data / discrimination"
    - "cross-border convenience vs jurisdiction / applicable law"
    - "mass tourism vs local carrying capacity"
    - "accessibility vs service complexity"

  shortest_maxim:
    "旅遊法第一眼不是『旅客買了行程』；先問『買的是單一服務還是組合、誰是organizer／agent／supplier、誰收款、誰承諾整體履行、哪一段失敗、旅客當時在哪裡、取消／危機／破產由哪一層法律承接』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 100.1 第一眼｜TRAVELLER → PRODUCT → CONTRACT STRUCTURE → ACTOR / ROLE → MONEY FLOW → SERVICE CHAIN → INFORMATION → DISRUPTION → ASSISTANCE / REFUND → INSOLVENCY → CROSS-BORDER REMEDY

```text
TRAVELLER
＝tourist？business traveller？resident? consumer?

PRODUCT
＝flight only？hotel only？package？excursion？cruise？dynamic bundle？

CONTRACT STRUCTURE
＝principal contract？agency？intermediation？package?

ACTOR / ROLE
＝organizer？agent？OTA？carrier？hotel？guide？platform?

MONEY FLOW
＝merchant of record？agency collection？supplier direct?

SERVICE CHAIN
＝transport → lodging → activity → local transfer?

INFORMATION
＝price？visa? cancellation? accessibility? risk?

DISRUPTION
＝supplier cancellation？extraordinary event？traveller cancellation?

ASSISTANCE / REFUND
＝reroute？care？repatriation？refund?

INSOLVENCY
＝who holds money / security?

CROSS-BORDER REMEDY
＝jurisdiction？applicable law？ADR？chargeback? court?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 100.31 Evidence architecture

- booking flow screenshots；
- confirmation；
- terms at booking date；
- payment record；
- itinerary；
- supplier vouchers；
- cancellation notices；
- travel advisories；
- communications；
- assistance offers；
- visa advice；
- insurance；
- insolvency security；
- refund / chargeback；
- actual service records。

核心：

> **旅遊案不能只拿一張行程表判責；要重建誰在何時向旅客承諾哪一段旅程，以及旅客的錢、資訊與實際服務究竟經過哪些主體。**

26. merchant-of-record / payment issues；
27. conflict of laws；
28. jurisdiction；
29. ADR / chargeback；
30. ICPT at non-binding level；
31. Global Code of Ethics at voluntary level；
32. Tourism Ethics Convention party / entry-in-force status。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_SHORT_TERM_RENTAL_DATA_REGULATION_APPLICABLE_2026_05_20__LOCAL_HOTEL_LICENSING_HOUSING_AND_SERVICE_RULES_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Regulation (EU) 2024/1028 on short-term accommodation rental data collection/sharing applies from 20 May 2026. It does not replace local hotel/accommodation licences, planning/housing rules, guest-contract duties, fire/health rules or platform obligations.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Regulation (EU) 2024/1028**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2024/1028/oj/eng
  - Verified point: Regulation (EU) 2024/1028 on short-term accommodation rental data collection/sharing applies from 20 May 2026.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔體育、娛樂創意、旅遊、旅館餐旅、博弈等高度組織化產業生態系；一般公司／契約／消保／監管只作cross-route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-095-HOTEL_ACCOMMODATION_HOSPITALITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "旅館／住宿與餐旅法"
  english_name: "Hotel / Accommodation & Hospitality Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "誰是真正accommodation operator、誰擁有building、誰管理brand / booking；guest reservation如何成立、room與common areas何時進入hotel control、guest property / privacy / safety如何保護、overbooking / cancellation / deposit / no-show / eviction如何處理；short-term rental、restaurant / catering、accessibility、classification、fire / health、data與online platform如何交叉？"

  historical_reason:
    "住宿交易的特殊性在於guest暫時把身體、財物、身份資料與夜間安全交給陌生業者控制。傳統innkeeper law因此對guest property與接待義務形成特殊規則；現代hotel又加入brand management、franchise、OTA、payment systems、CCTV、electronic locks、food service、pool / spa、accessible rooms與short-term rentals，使責任不再只存在於『房東—房客』二分。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "guest privacy vs premises security"
    - "operator control vs owner / franchisor separation"
    - "overbooking efficiency vs confirmed reservation"
    - "hotel autonomy vs anti-discrimination / accessibility"
    - "property protection vs liability limits"
    - "guest safety vs personal autonomy"
    - "dynamic pricing vs transparency"
    - "brand standards vs local law"
    - "short-term rental innovation vs housing / zoning"
    - "food hospitality vs food-safety risk"
    - "staff entry / housekeeping vs room privacy"
    - "ID / security registration vs data minimization"
    - "sustainability certification vs actual environmental performance"

  shortest_maxim:
    "旅館／餐旅法第一眼不是『客人住進飯店』；先問『誰是operator、reservation由誰承諾、哪個room / facility由誰控制、guest交付了哪些財物與資料、事件發生在哪個空間／時間、hotel licence / safety / food / privacy哪一條鏈真正適用』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 101.1 第一眼｜PROPERTY / PREMISES → OPERATOR / OWNER / BRAND → LICENCE / CLASSIFICATION → RESERVATION → CHECK-IN → ROOM / FACILITY CONTROL → GUEST PROPERTY / DATA → SERVICE / FOOD → INCIDENT → CHECK-OUT / CLAIM

```text
PROPERTY / PREMISES
＝hotel？hostel？resort？serviced apartment？home-share？

OPERATOR / OWNER / BRAND
＝owner？lessee？management company？franchisee？franchisor?

LICENCE / CLASSIFICATION
＝business licence？tourism registration？star rating？fire / health approvals?

RESERVATION
＝hotel direct？OTA？agent？prepaid？deposit?

CHECK-IN
＝ID？payment hold？terms？room assignment?

ROOM / FACILITY CONTROL
＝room？pool？gym？spa？parking？restaurant?

GUEST PROPERTY / DATA
＝luggage？safe？vehicle？passport data？CCTV?

SERVICE / FOOD
＝housekeeping？breakfast？allergens？room service?

INCIDENT
＝theft？fall？fire？food illness？privacy breach？overbooking?

CHECK-OUT / CLAIM
＝charges？deposit release？lost property？complaint?
```

- property / operator contracts；

核心：

> **旅館案不能讓「品牌」或「房號」代替責任鏈；必須先知道誰控制空間、誰控制員工、誰對guest承諾服務，以及事件發生時那個具體設施由誰實際維護。**


There is no single harmonised EU gambling code for substantive licensing. The Commission notes a wide Member-State margin for justified gambling restrictions and primary national enforcement, while FATF in June 2026 launched additional work on casino/broader gambling illicit-finance risks.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — EU-law monitoring / gambling**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/law/application-eu-law/implementing-eu-law/infringement-procedure/2023-annual-report-monitoring-application-eu-law/communications-networks-content-and-technology-2023_en
  - Verified point: The Commission notes Member States have a wide margin for justified gambling restrictions and that national authorities/courts primarily assess concrete compliance.
- **FATF — Outcomes of Plenary, 17–19 June 2026**
  - URL: https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/publications/Fatfgeneral/outcomes-fatf-plenary-june-2026.html
  - Verified point: FATF approved a seventh targeted update on virtual assets/VASPs and new work on gambling-sector illicit-finance risks.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔體育、娛樂創意、旅遊、旅館餐旅、博弈等高度組織化產業生態系；一般公司／契約／消保／監管只作cross-route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-096-GAMBLING_BETTING_GAMING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "博弈／博彩法"
  english_name: "Gambling / Betting / Gaming Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項交易是否構成regulated gambling / betting / lottery / gaming activity；stake、chance / skill、prize、operator control與player location如何被法律分類；誰需operator / supplier / premises / personal licence、哪些games / bet types可提供、如何保護underage / vulnerable players、player funds、game integrity、self-exclusion、advertising、AML/CFT、sports integrity、tax與cross-border remote enforcement？"

  historical_reason:
    "博彩市場以不確定結果交換財產風險，具有娛樂價值，也容易形成欺詐、成癮、未成年人傷害、洗錢、比賽操縱與地下市場。國家因此常以licensing、game approval、age / location controls、player-fund protection、advertising restrictions、responsible-gambling tools、AML與tax建立高密度regulation。數位化又使operator在一國、server在另一國、player在第三國成為常態，使jurisdiction / geolocation / payment controls成為核心。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "personal autonomy vs addiction / vulnerability"
    - "legal market channelization vs prohibition"
    - "operator profit vs player protection"
    - "game innovation vs regulatory classification"
    - "skill vs chance"
    - "privacy vs affordability / AML checks"
    - "cash / crypto convenience vs financial-crime risk"
    - "advertising freedom vs inducement / minors"
    - "sports betting market vs sport integrity"
    - "national licensing vs cross-border internet"
    - "state revenue vs regulator independence"
    - "responsible-gambling intervention vs user autonomy"
    - "platform / affiliate marketing vs operator accountability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "博弈法第一眼不是『有人輸錢』；先問『玩家到底交付什麼stake、結果由chance／skill如何決定、prize是什麼、誰控制game / odds / settlement、玩家在哪個jurisdiction、operator有哪些licences與player-protection / AML obligations』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 102.1 第一眼｜ACTIVITY → STATUTORY CLASSIFICATION → STAKE / CONSIDERATION → CHANCE / SKILL → PRIZE → OPERATOR / SUPPLIER → LICENCE → PLAYER / LOCATION → GAME INTEGRITY → MONEY FLOW → PLAYER PROTECTION → AML / SPORTS INTEGRITY → ENFORCEMENT

```text
ACTIVITY
＝casino？sports bet？lottery？poker？bingo？prediction market？prize draw？

CLASSIFICATION
＝gambling？betting？lottery？contest？skill game？promotional game？

STAKE
＝money？token？virtual item？subscription? purchase?

CHANCE / SKILL
＝random outcome？sports event？player skill？mixed?

PRIZE
＝cash？goods？credits？withdrawable token？status only?

OPERATOR / SUPPLIER
＝B2C operator？B2B platform？RNG supplier？affiliate?

LICENCE
＝operator？premises？remote？game? key person?

PLAYER / LOCATION
＝age？residence？geolocation？excluded person?

GAME INTEGRITY
＝rules？RNG？odds？settlement？audit?

MONEY FLOW
＝deposit？wallet？player funds？withdrawal？crypto?

PLAYER PROTECTION
＝limits？self-exclusion？marketing? intervention?

AML / SPORTS INTEGRITY
＝CDD？STR？source of funds？suspicious betting?

ENFORCEMENT
＝block? fine? licence action? criminal offence?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 102.40 Evidence architecture

- licence / scope；
- game rules；
- software / version；
- RNG / lab report；
- player KYC；
- age / geolocation；
- deposits / withdrawals；
- player-fund ledger；
- self-exclusion；
- responsible-gambling interactions；
- marketing / affiliate content；
- AML monitoring；
- sports-betting alerts；
- suspicious-match data；
- tax；
- regulator correspondence；
- terms at wager time。

核心：

> **博弈案不能只看輸贏；必須把玩家、jurisdiction、licence、遊戲版本、賠率／隨機機制、資金與保護措施鎖在同一時間點，否則最容易讓「玩家自願」或「平台有牌」替整個系統免責。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "WTO_CORE_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__ECOMMERCE_MORATORIUM_LAPSED_2026_03_30__ECA_NOT_YET_GENERAL_WTO_RULE__FTA_AND_TRADE_REMEDY_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status only; domestic merits and classification not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The WTO e-commerce Work Programme and customs-duty moratorium lapsed on 30 March 2026 after MC14 failed to extend them. Separately, 67 Members adopted an interim pathway for the E-Commerce Agreement, which enters into force only after 45 instruments of acceptance; it must not be treated as already binding on all WTO Members.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WTO — Work Programme on E-Commerce / moratorium**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/tratop_e/ecom_e/ecom_work_programme_e.htm
  - Verified point: The WTO e-commerce Work Programme and customs-duty moratorium lapsed on 30 March 2026 after MC14 failed to reach consensus on extension.
- **WTO — Agreement on Electronic Commerce**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/tratop_e/ecom_e/joint_statement_e.htm
  - Verified point: 67 WTO Members adopted an interim pathway in March 2026; the E-Commerce Agreement enters into force after 45 instruments of acceptance.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列 current framework / transition / treaty-or-regulatory status。若案件取決於特定國家、公司類型、金融產品分類、牌照、門檻、轉置、主管機關、動態清單、市場定義或交易日期，runtime 必須重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留跨境商業交換的交易／貨物流／付款／邊境／國際承諾分層路由；不取代海關085、制裁069、貿易金融110、貨物單證111或國際私法004。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-003-INTERNATIONAL_TRADE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際貿易法"
  english_name: "International Trade Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "跨境商品、服務、資金、技術與商業承諾如何在私人交易、國家邊境管制、國際經貿承諾與風險分配之間取得可預測性？"

  historical_reason:
    "跨境交易同時穿越私人契約、運輸、支付、海關、產品規範、國家市場准入與國際經貿承諾。若只把它看成『兩家公司簽買賣契約』，會漏掉邊境與國家監管；若只把它看成 WTO 國家間爭端，又會漏掉私人交易、付款、運送、保險與仲裁。因此國際貿易法本質上是多層法律秩序的接口。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家監管主權 vs 對外市場開放承諾"
    - "非歧視與可預測性 vs 公共政策例外"
    - "自由貿易 vs 反傾銷／補貼／防衛等貿易救濟"
    - "私人契約自治 vs 強制性邊境／制裁／出口管制"
    - "全球供應鏈效率 vs 國家安全／產業政策"
    - "交易形式 vs 貨物流、付款流、文件流與風險流"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際貿易第一眼不是『誰違約』；先把契約、貨物流、付款、邊境監管與國際承諾拆成不同法律層。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 6.1 第一眼｜LAYER BEFORE RULE

任何「國際貿易」案件進來，先做分層：

```text
PRIVATE COMMERCIAL LAYER
買賣／代理／經銷／供應／技術授權

TRANSPORT & LOGISTICS LAYER
海運／空運／陸運／倉儲／提單／報關

PAYMENT & FINANCE LAYER
信用狀／託收／銀行付款／融資／保險

CUSTOMS & BORDER LAYER
關稅／稅則分類／估價／原產地／進出口申報

PRODUCT REGULATION LAYER
SPS／TBT／檢驗／標示／安全／許可

TRADE REMEDIES LAYER
反傾銷／反補貼／防衛措施

SANCTIONS & EXPORT CONTROL LAYER
制裁／禁運／出口管制／受管制技術／終端用途

WTO / FTA / TREATY LAYER
國家之間的市場准入、非歧視與例外義務

CONFLICT OF LAWS & DISPUTE LAYER
選法／管轄／仲裁／判決與仲裁判斷執行
```

沒有先分層，後面找到再多法條都可能是錯的。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 6.9 證據特徵

國際貿易案件的重要材料可能包括：

- 主契約；
- 訂單／PO；
- 發票；
- 裝箱單；
- 提單／空運單；
- 原產地證明；
- 海關申報；
- HS classification 資料；
- 估價資料；
- 信用狀與銀行文件；
- 保單；
- 付款 SWIFT／銀行紀錄；
- 出口許可；
- 終端使用者聲明；
- 制裁篩查紀錄；
- 產品檢驗與認證；
- 技術規格；
- 電子郵件；
- 經銷／代理協議；
- 仲裁條款；
- 選法條款；
- 國家公布之關稅、管制措施與行政決定。

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下承接來源卡；BUILD01 未重新驗證 2026 現行法。監管邊界、牌照、資本標準、清單、門檻、國際協定、轉置日期與市場規則若影響案件，必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 6.11 Positive Law Gate

國際貿易是最不適合靠模型記憶完成的領域之一。

以下必須現查：

1. WTO 會員、承諾表、爭端機制與現行協定狀態；
2. FTA 是否生效及具體原產地規則；
3. 現行關稅率、HS 分類、估價與行政解釋；
4. 反傾銷、反補貼、防衛措施；
5. 制裁名單與禁令；
6. 出口管制清單、目的地、最終用途與再出口規則；
7. 進出口許可；
8. CISG 是否適用／是否排除；
9. 現行國際私法、選法與仲裁法；
10. 產品衛生、技術與安全規範。

這些可能隨時間、國家、商品、技術、交易對象與政策快速變動。

## FAILURE_MODES

## 6.10 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "國際貿易法＝WTO 法。"
  - "國際貿易法＝國際買賣契約。"
  - "Incoterms＝所有權移轉規則。"
  - "CISG＝所有跨境交易都適用。"
  - "FTA 有優惠關稅＝這批貨當然符合原產地規則。"
  - "WTO 規則存在＝私人公司可以直接用 WTO 條文告另一家公司。"
  - "國家安全／健康例外一提出＝措施當然合法。"
  - "貨從某國出＝原產地必然就是該國。"
  - "契約合法＝出口、制裁與海關就當然合法。"
  - "有出口許可＝所有後續轉運／終端用途都被授權。"
```

| `EU_AML` | European Commission — AML/CFT at EU level — https://finance.ec.europa.eu/financial-crime/anti-money-laundering-and-countering-financing-terrorism-eu-level_en | The EU AML package includes AMLR 2024/1624, described by the Commission as applying from 2027, and the AMLA Regulation 2024/1620. |
| `MIFID_TRANS` | European Commission — MiFID II amendment transposition status — https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/enforcement-and-infringements-banking-and-finance-law/monitoring-banking-and-finance-directives/markets-financial-instruments-directive-ii-amendment-mifid-ii-amended-directive-eu-2024790_en | As of 7 August 2026, 22 Member States reported full transposition; five reported no measures and infringement proceedings were pending against 13. |
| `SOLVENCY_II` | European Commission — Solvency II implementing/delegated acts — https://finance.ec.europa.eu/regulation-and-supervision/financial-services-legislation/implementing-and-delegated-acts/solvency-2_en | The Solvency II framework remains current; Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/269 was published in February 2026 and the framework continues to be amended. |
| `EU_INVESTMENT_FIRMS` | European Commission — Prudential rules for investment firms — https://finance.ec.europa.eu/financial-markets/financial-markets-policy/prudential-rules-investment-firms_en | The IFR/IFD framework remains the EU bespoke prudential regime for non-systemic investment firms. |
| `BASEL_2026` | Basel Committee / GHOS — Basel III implementation progress, 9 March 2026 — https://www.bis.org/press/p260309.htm | About 75% of Basel Committee member jurisdictions had implemented or would shortly implement the standards; remaining jurisdictions reported implementation plans. |
| `BASEL_RCAP` | Basel Committee — RCAP assessment schedule — https://www.bis.org/bcbs/implementation/rcap_aoi.htm | Jurisdictional Basel III assessments continue on schedules extending through 2030, confirming non-uniform implementation timing. |
| `SOLVENCY_2027` | European Commission — Q&A on Solvency II delegated regulation — https://finance.ec.europa.eu/news/questions-and-answers-solvency-ii-delegated-regulation-2025-10-29_en | Directive (EU) 2025/2 updates Solvency II and the Commission states the new rules take effect on 30 January 2027. |
| `FSB_INSURANCE` | Financial Stability Board — Insurer recovery/resolution planning guidance — https://www.fsb.org/2026/04/fsb-finalises-guidance-on-insurers-subject-to-recovery-and-resolution-planning/ | On 29 April 2026 the FSB finalised guidance for authorities on which insurers should be subject to recovery and resolution planning. |
| `EU_COMPANY` | European Commission — Company law and corporate governance — https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance_en | The Commission's current page records a June 2026 report on review of the Shareholder Rights Directive and continuing EU company-law activity. |
| `EU_INC` | European Commission — EU Inc. proposal — https://commission.europa.eu/topics/business-and-industry/company-law-and-corporate-governance/eu-inc-new-harmonised-corporate-legal-regime_en | EU Inc. was presented on 18 March 2026 as a proposal; it is not an already-operative harmonised company-law code. |
| `FATF_NPO` | FATF — Non-profit organisations — https://www.fatf-gafi.org/en/topics/non-profit-organisations.html | FATF emphasizes targeted and proportionate risk-based measures for the subset of NPOs exposed to terrorist-financing abuse, with 2025 procedures addressing unintended consequences. |
| `AI_ART50` | European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/guidelines-ai-transparency-obligations | Article 50 transparency obligations apply from 2 August 2026, including transparency duties concerning certain AI-generated/deepfake content. |
| `CSDDD_2026` | European Commission — Simplification / CSDDD and CSRD — https://commission.europa.eu/law/law-making-process/better-regulation/simplification-implementation-and-enforcement/simplification_en | Directive (EU) 2026/470 simplifying reporting/due-diligence requirements was adopted 24 February 2026 and entered into force 16 March 2026. |
| `UNCITRAL_INSOLVENCY` | UNCITRAL — Insolvency model laws — https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/insolvency | UNCITRAL maintains model-law frameworks for cross-border insolvency, insolvency-related judgments and enterprise-group insolvency; enactment remains State-specific. |
| `UNCITRAL_EGI` | UNCITRAL — Model Law on Enterprise Group Insolvency (2019) — https://uncitral.un.org/en/MLEGI | The 2019 Model Law provides a model framework for enterprise-group insolvency and rescue, not directly applicable national law. |
| `EU_MERGER_2026` | European Commission — Review of Merger Guidelines — https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/mergers/review-merger-guidelines_en | Draft revised Merger Guidelines were published 30 April 2026; consultation closed 26 June 2026 and finalisation is planned for Q4 2026. |
| `EU_TTBER_2026` | European Commission — TTBER — https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/antitrust-and-cartels/legislation/ttber_en | Commission Regulation (EU) 2026/877 (TTBER) and the accompanying Technology Transfer Guidelines entered into force on 1 May 2026. |
| `EU_ART102` | European Commission — Application of Article 102 TFEU — https://competition-policy.ec.europa.eu/antitrust-and-cartels/legislation/application-article-102-tfeu_en | Article 102 remains the EU abuse-of-dominance legal framework; Commission guidance development remains a dynamic policy process requiring current case-law and guidance checks. |
| `DSA` | European Commission — DSA enforcement framework — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/dsa-enforcement | The DSA's general application date is 17 February 2024; designated VLOP/VLOSE obligations and enforcement are service-specific and dynamic. |
| `DMA_CLOUD` | European Commission — DMA cloud designation preliminary position — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/news/commission-reaches-preliminary-position-amazons-and-microsofts-market-leading-cloud-services-should | On 25 June 2026 the Commission announced preliminary views on potential DMA gatekeeper designation of AWS and Azure, illustrating dynamic designation/investigation status. |
| `EU_GREEN_CONSUMER` | European Commission — Sustainable consumption — https://commission.europa.eu/topics/consumers/consumer-rights-and-complaints/sustainable-consumption_en | Directive (EU) 2024/825 was due for transposition by 27 March 2026 and applies from 27 September 2026. |
| `WADA_2026` | World Anti-Doping Agency — 2026 Prohibited List — https://www.wada-ama.org/en/resources/world-anti-doping-program/prohibited-list | The 2026 Prohibited List entered into effect on 1 January 2026 and is an annually updated International Standard. |
| `PACKAGE_TRAVEL_2026` | European Commission — Package travel amendments — https://commission.europa.eu/news-and-media/news/package-travel-stronger-rights-travellers-and-simpler-rules-travel-industry-2026-05-28_en | Amendments to the Package Travel Directive entered into force 28 May 2026; Member States have 28 months to transpose, with binding operator application later. |
| `SHORT_RENTAL` | EUR-Lex — Regulation (EU) 2024/1028 — https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2024/1028/oj/eng | Regulation (EU) 2024/1028 on short-term accommodation rental data collection/sharing applies from 20 May 2026. |
| `EU_GAMBLING` | European Commission — EU-law monitoring / gambling — https://commission.europa.eu/law/application-eu-law/implementing-eu-law/infringement-procedure/2023-annual-report-monitoring-application-eu-law/communications-networks-content-and-technology-2023_en | The Commission notes Member States have a wide margin for justified gambling restrictions and that national authorities/courts primarily assess concrete compliance. |
| `WTO_ECOM_WORK` | WTO — Work Programme on E-Commerce / moratorium — https://www.wto.org/english/tratop_e/ecom_e/ecom_work_programme_e.htm | The WTO e-commerce Work Programme and customs-duty moratorium lapsed on 30 March 2026 after MC14 failed to reach consensus on extension. |
| `WTO_ECA` | WTO — Agreement on Electronic Commerce — https://www.wto.org/english/tratop_e/ecom_e/joint_statement_e.htm | 67 WTO Members adopted an interim pathway in March 2026; the E-Commerce Agreement enters into force after 45 instruments of acceptance. |

## BUILD02 的閉合資格

```text
36 REFRESH_REQUIRED
→ 36 QUALIFIED CURRENT-LAW STATUS

但：

QUALIFIED STATUS PASS
!= EVERY JURISDICTION VERIFIED
!= EVERY IMPLEMENTATION DATE UNIFORM
!= EVERY REGULATORY THRESHOLD FROZEN
!= EVERY MARKET / PRODUCT CLASSIFICATION CLOSED
```

DB2 現在取得：

```text
V1.0 DB2 RELEASE-CANDIDATE QUALIFICATION
FOR STABLE METHOD + CURRENT FRAMEWORK / TRANSITION ROUTING

具體案件仍須重新查：
JURISDICTION / DATE / ENTITY TYPE / PRODUCT CLASSIFICATION / LICENCE / THRESHOLD / TRANSITION / DYNAMIC LIST / MARKET DEFINITION
```
